# PM_62 kod işleri (Unity, `Assets/_Game/`)

Mimari: Core ← Presentation ← Platform. Core saf C# (Unity API yok). Presentation oyun durumunu yalnız Core olaylarıyla izler. Altın değer testlerini (`Golden/*.json`) yeniden üretme. `Library/ Logs/ Builds/ *.csproj` okuma. Görseller `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/` altına başka bir Codex tarafından çiziliyor (liste `docs/art/task46/LISTE.md`); kod bu yolları kullansın, dosya henüz yoksa mevcut kareye düşsün (çökmesin).

1. **Test modu** URL `&test=1` yalnız `c=ic` ya da `c=p21` ile geçerli: bütün bölümler açık, bütün booster'lar açık ve sayısı 99 (azalmaz), altın 99999. Telemetri kayıtları `cohort=ic` gider. Başka cohort'ta bayrak yok sayılır. Mevcut URL bayraklarını (`&coin=`, `&devam=` vb.) nasıl okunuyorsa öyle oku.
2. **Kilitli booster'a dokununca** booster düğmesinin üstünde kısa balon: "Seviye N'de açılır" (TR; EN "Unlocks at level N"), ~1,5 sn sonra söner. N = açılış seviyesi.
3. **Makas** animasyonu: gidiş 0,45 sn, kesim 0,3 sn, çıkış 0,45 sn (toplam ~1,2–1,4 sn). Sabitler adlandırılmış alan olsun.
4. **Değnek** seçim modunda oyun süresi DURMASIN (şu an duruyorsa duraklatmayı kaldır).
5. **Saat booster yeniden** (eski kopuk kol çizimi/kodu kalksın): Jigo madalyonda `T46/clock/extend` kareleri → uzanmış `hold` döngüsü; fırça ucu Süre paneline değince `spark` + `ice_overlay` panele oturur, süre rakamları mavi-beyaz renge geçer; panelin altında 10 sn bar (`freeze_bar_bg/fill`) görünür sayımla boşalır; süre bu sırada durur (Core'daki mevcut dondurma mantığını kullan, süreyi parametre `FreezeSeconds = 10` yap); bitince `ice_break` + `retract`, Jigo normale döner.
6. **Kilit okunurluğu**: kilit görseli %15 büyük; kilit sayısı beyaz, kalın koyu kontur (TMP outline), punto büyük.
7. **Kombo**: herhangi bir yanlış hamle (takılma/bloklanan çekiş) komboyu anında sıfırlar.
8. **Kazanma ekranı**: yürüyen Jiggle'lar kalksın. Yerine Jiggle ikonu + "30 Jiggle kurtarıldı!" (sayı 0'dan sayarak artar, bitince küçük pop ölçek). Düğmeler: **Sonraki Seviye** (büyük, yeşil) ve **Menü** (küçük, ikincil). Dikey düzen: başlık, Jigo, sandık ilerlemesi, Jiggle sayısı, düğmeler; eşit boşluk. EN metin: "30 Jiggles rescued!".
9. **Jigo madalyon**: göz kırpma rastgele 4–7 sn aralıkla. Durum geçişleri (normal↔mutlu vb.) 0,25 sn crossfade + hafif ölçek esnemesi; `T46/jigo/normal_to_happy`, `happy_to_normal` varsa onları oynat. Kare animasyonun üstüne sürekli kod nefesi: sinüs ölçek ±%2 (~2,2 sn), hafif dönme ±1,5°. `T46/jigo/normal_loop`, `happy_loop` varsa onları ve 12–24 fps kullan.
10. **Bölüm haritası**: oyuncunun mevcut bölümü görünür alanda değilse kenarda soluk küçük hap "Seviye N ↓" ya da "↑" (EN "Level N"); dokununca o bölüme yumuşak kayar. Ek olarak küçük "en üste / en alta" tuşu.
11. **Kenar payı**: 24 pt kalır ama tek bir parametre (ör. `SafeMarginPt`) üzerinden; 28'e çevirmek tek satır olsun.

Değişikliklerden sonra derlemeyi kontrol et: `bash Tools/unity_batch.sh tests` (EditMode; Perf testleri hariç değerlendirilir). Kırılan test varsa düzelt. Sonunda KISA özet: değişen dosyalar (tek satırda) + test sayıları + yapılamayan madde varsa nedeni.

using System;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* What the game opens with (PRODUCT.md A6b, 2026-10-13). A level asked for
       in the link (?lv=N) waits behind the name gate: until the player has
       given a name the menu sits under the gate, so no attempt starts and no
       clock runs while they type; then the level opens at full time. Without
       the gate (name known, debug, the level editor) it opens straight away. */
    public readonly struct LaunchPlan
    {
        /* Start this level now (StartNow), or this one once the name is in (AfterName), -1 for none. */
        public readonly bool StartNow;
        public readonly int Level, AfterName;

        LaunchPlan(bool now, int level, int after) { StartNow = now; Level = level; AfterName = after; }

        /* requested: level index from the link or the editor, -1 for the menu. */
        public static LaunchPlan For(int requested, bool askName)
        {
            if (requested < 0) return new LaunchPlan(false, -1, -1);
            return askName ? new LaunchPlan(false, -1, requested) : new LaunchPlan(true, requested, -1);
        }

        /* The opening sequence GameRoot runs: the level or the menu now, then the
           gate; when the name arrives, nameGiven and then the linked level. */
        public static LaunchPlan Run(int requested, bool askName, Action<int> startLevel, Action showMenu,
                                     Action<Action<string>> askForName, Action<string> nameGiven)
        {
            var plan = For(requested, askName);
            if (plan.StartNow) startLevel(plan.Level); else showMenu();
            if (askName)
                askForName(name =>
                {
                    nameGiven(NormalizePlayerName(name));
                    if (plan.AfterName >= 0) startLevel(plan.AfterName);   // the level from the link, at full time
                });
            return plan;
        }

        public static string NormalizePlayerName(string name)
        {
            name = (name ?? "").Trim();
            if (name.Length <= 12) return name;
            int length = char.IsHighSurrogate(name[11]) ? 11 : 12;
            return name.Substring(0, length);
        }
    }
}

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public sealed class BoosterInventory
    {
        public const int BoosterStart = 3;
        public const string Key = "rs_boosters";
        [Serializable] public sealed class Data
        {
            public int[] stock = new int[4];
            public bool[] trained = new bool[4];
            public int[] chestBonus;
        }
        readonly IKeyValueStore _store;
        readonly Data _data;
        public BoosterInventory(IKeyValueStore store, LevelData level, Func<int, bool> won)
        {
            _store = store;
            try { _data = JsonUtility.FromJson<Data>(store.Get(Key) ?? "null"); }
            catch (Exception) { _data = null; }
            bool missing = _data == null || _data.stock == null || _data.stock.Length != 4;
            var trained = _data?.trained;
            if (missing) _data = new Data();
            if (trained != null && trained.Length == 4) _data.trained = trained;
            else _data.trained = new bool[4];
            if (missing)
                foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All)
                    if (Trained(b) || (level.Unlock != null && level.Unlock.TryGetValue(b.Id(), out int at) && won(at - 1)))
                        _data.stock[(int)b] = BoosterStart;
            if (_data.chestBonus != null && _data.chestBonus.Length != 4) _data.chestBonus = null;
            foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All) _data.stock[(int)b] = Math.Max(0, _data.stock[(int)b]);
            Save();
        }
        public bool Trained(Booster b) => _data.trained[(int)b];
        public int Stock(Booster b) => _data.stock[(int)b];
        public void Apply(GameSession session)
        {
            foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All) if (session.IsUnlocked(b)) session.SetStock(b, Stock(b));
        }
        public void Complete(Booster b)
        {
            if (Trained(b)) return;
            _data.trained[(int)b] = true;
            int bonus = Art.T54 && TelemetryMeta.Current?.Cohort == "p21" && _data.chestBonus != null ? Math.Max(0, _data.chestBonus[(int)b]) : 0;
            _data.stock[(int)b] = (int)Math.Min(int.MaxValue, (long)BoosterStart + bonus);
            if (_data.chestBonus != null) _data.chestBonus[(int)b] = 0;
            Save();
        }
        public void Spend(Booster b)
        {
            _data.stock[(int)b] = Math.Max(0, Stock(b) - 1);
            Save();
        }
        void Save() { _store.Set(Key, JsonUtility.ToJson(_data)); _store.Save(); }
    }
}

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public sealed partial class GameRoot
    {
        bool P21Boosters => Art.T54 && TelemetryMeta.Current?.Cohort == "p21";
        P21BoosterView _boosterView21;
        Booster? _selected21, _effect21;
        int _region21 = -1, _piece21;
        (int x, int y, bool vertical) _joint21;
        readonly bool[] _regions21 = new bool[4];
        Vector2 _target21;
        float _effectAge21, _clockAge21 = -1;
        bool _effectApplied21;
        bool BoosterBusy21 => P21Boosters && _effect21.HasValue;
        bool BoosterSelection21 => P21Boosters && _selected21.HasValue;

        void ResetBoosters21()
        {
            _selected21 = _effect21 = null; _region21 = -1; _clockAge21 = -1;
            _boosterView21 = null;
        }

        void BuildBoosters21()
        {
            _boosterView21 = P21Boosters ? new P21BoosterView(_world, _screen, _safe.y, _board, _bar) : null;
            _boosterView21?.Selection(_selected21, _region21, _regions21);
            if (_boosterView21 == null) return;
            if (_effect21 == Booster.Hammer) _target21 = PieceCenter21(_piece21);
            if (_effect21 == Booster.Wand) _target21 = _boosterView21.ZoneRect(_region21).center;
            if (_effect21 == Booster.Scissors) _target21 = JointCenter21();
        }

        Vector2 JointCenter21() => _board.Origin + new Vector2(_joint21.x + (_joint21.vertical ? 0 : .5f), _joint21.y + (_joint21.vertical ? .5f : 0)) * _board.Cell;
        Vector2 PieceCenter21(int piece)
        {
            var center = Vector2.zero;
            foreach (var c in _session.Level.Pieces[piece]) center += new Vector2(c.X + .5f, c.Y + .5f);
            return _board.Origin + center / _session.Level.Pieces[piece].Length * _board.Cell;
        }

        void TestBooster21()
        {
            if (!P21Boosters) return;
            string flag = Host.Current.Query("booster");
            Booster? b = flag == "cekic" ? Booster.Hammer : flag == "makas" ? Booster.Scissors
                : flag == "degnek" ? Booster.Wand : flag == "saat" ? Booster.Clock : (Booster?)null;
            if (!b.HasValue) return;
            _session.TestBoosterUnlock = b;
            _session.SetStock(b.Value, System.Math.Max(1, _session.Stock(b.Value)));
            _introOpen = false; _tutorialSteps.Clear(); if (_spotlight != null) Destroy(_spotlight.gameObject); _spotlight = null;
            _hand?.Remove(); _hand = null; CloseCard(); _bar.ShowTutorial(false);
            SelectBooster21(b.Value);
        }

        void SelectBooster21(Booster b)
        {
            if (_selected21 == b)
            {
                _selected21 = null; _region21 = -1; _boosters.Disarm();
                if (_trainingUse == b) { _trainingUse = null; _session.FreeUse = null; }
            }
            else
            {
                bool training = TutorialActive && TutorialScript.BoosterOf(_tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].booster, out var taught) && taught == b;
                if (!_session.IsUnlocked(b) || (_session.Stock(b) == 0 && _session.FreeUse != b && !training))
                { _boosters.Tap(b); return; }
                if (_selected21.HasValue && _trainingUse == _selected21)
                { _trainingUse = null; _session.FreeUse = null; }
                if (training) _session.FreeUse = b;
                if (training) { _trainingUse = b; if (_spotlight != null) Destroy(_spotlight.gameObject); _spotlight = null; }
                _drag.Cancel(); _boosters.Disarm(); _selected21 = null; _region21 = -1;
                if (b == Booster.Clock)
                {
                    if (_session.FreezeLeft > 0) { _boosterView21?.Selection(null, -1, _regions21); RefreshPlaying(); return; }
                    _clockAge21 = 0; StartEffect21(b, Vector2.zero); return;
                }
                _selected21 = b;
                if (b == Booster.Scissors || b == Booster.Hammer)
                {
                    int stock = _session.Stock(b);
                    _session.SetStock(b, System.Math.Max(1, stock)); _boosters.Tap(b); _session.SetStock(b, stock);
                }
                if (b == Booster.Wand) for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) _regions21[i] = _session.CanWandRegion(i);
            }
            _boosterView21?.Selection(_selected21, _region21, _regions21);
            RefreshPlaying();
        }

        bool PressBooster21(Vector2 point)
        {
            if (!P21Boosters || _session.IsOver) return false;
            if (BoosterBusy21) return true;
            var b = _bar.Hit(point);
            if (TutorialActive && !_selected21.HasValue &&
                (_tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur != "booster" ||
                 !TutorialScript.BoosterOf(_tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].booster, out var taught) || b != taught)) return false;
            if (b.HasValue) { SelectBooster21(b.Value); return true; }
            if (!_selected21.HasValue) return false;
            if (_selected21 == Booster.Wand)
            {
                if (_region21 >= 0 && _boosterView21.ConfirmRect.Contains(point))
                { StartEffect21(Booster.Wand, _boosterView21.ZoneRect(_region21).center); return true; }
                for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                    if (_regions21[i] && _boosterView21.ZoneRect(i).Contains(point))
                    { _region21 = i; _boosterView21.Selection(_selected21, _region21, _regions21); break; }
            }
            else if (_selected21 == Booster.Scissors)
            {
                var j = _board.JointAt(point);
                if (j.HasValue)
                {
                    _joint21 = j.Value;
                    StartEffect21(Booster.Scissors, JointCenter21());
                }
            }
            else
            {
                int piece = _board.PieceAt(point);
                if (piece >= 0)
                {
                    _piece21 = piece; StartEffect21(Booster.Hammer, PieceCenter21(piece));
                }
            }
            return true;
        }

        void StartEffect21(Booster booster, Vector2 target)
        {
            _selected21 = null; _boosters.Disarm();
            _effect21 = booster; _effectAge21 = 0; _effectApplied21 = false; _target21 = target;
            _boosterView21?.Selection(null, -1, _regions21); RefreshPlaying();
        }

        void TickBoosters21()
        {
            if (!P21Boosters || _boosterView21 == null) return;
            bool active = !_pageHidden && _card == null && _list == null && _continuePanel == null && _celebration == null;
            if (active && _clockAge21 >= 0) _clockAge21 += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            if (active && _effect21.HasValue)
            {
                _effectAge21 += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                var b = _effect21.Value;
                float hit = b == Booster.Hammer ? .47f : b == Booster.Scissors ? .4f : b == Booster.Clock ? .65f : .7f;
                if (!_effectApplied21 && _effectAge21 >= hit)
                {
                    _effectApplied21 = true;
                    bool skipTarget = _trainingUse == b && TutorialActive && _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur == "booster" &&
                        _tutorialAt + 1 < _tutorialSteps.Count && _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt + 1].tur == "booster_hedef";
                    if (skipTarget) _tutorialAt++;
                    var result = b == Booster.Hammer ? _session.UseHammer(_piece21)
                        : b == Booster.Scissors ? _session.UseScissors(_joint21.x, _joint21.y, _joint21.vertical)
                        : b == Booster.Clock ? _session.UseClock() : _session.UseWandRegion(_region21);
                    if (result == CommandResult.Ok)
                    {
                        if (b == Booster.Hammer) _board.BoosterShake();
                        if (b == Booster.Wand) _board.RegionDance(_region21);
                        if (_session.IsOver) _endAt = Mathf.Max(_endAt, Time.unscaledTime + (b == Booster.Wand ? 1.5f : 1) - _effectAge21 + .03f);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (skipTarget) _tutorialAt--;
                        _trainingUse = null; _session.FreeUse = null;
                        _bar.Notice(result == CommandResult.NoArrangement ? Texts.WandFailed : Texts.BoosterPrompt(_boosters), 1.6f);
                    }
                }
                float duration = b == Booster.Wand ? 1.5f : b == Booster.Clock ? .65f : 1;
                if (_effectAge21 >= duration) _effect21 = null;
            }
            _boosterView21.Selection(_selected21, _region21, _regions21);
            _boosterView21.TickSelection();
            _boosterView21.Effect(_effect21 == Booster.Clock ? null : _effect21, _effectAge21, _target21, _joint21.vertical);
            bool clock = _boosterView21.Clock(_clockAge21, _session.FreezeLeft, _session.IsOver, _safe.y);
            _hud.ShowClockPortrait(clock);
        }
    }
}
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    sealed class JigoPortrait54
    {
        readonly Sprite[][] _frames = new Sprite[5][];
        readonly SpriteRenderer _front, _back;
        readonly int _forced;
        readonly bool _joy;
        public void Visible(bool visible) { _front.gameObject.SetActive(visible); _back.gameObject.SetActive(visible); }
        int _current = -1, _previous, _time;
        int? _fuse;
        bool _lost, _won;
        float _happyUntil, _changedAt;

        public JigoPortrait54(Transform parent, Vector2 center, Vector2 size, int order, GameSession session)
        {
            string forced = Host.Current.Query("jigo");
            _forced = System.Array.IndexOf(Art54.Moods, forced);
            _joy = forced == "sevinc";
            for (int m = 0; m < _frames.Length; m++)
            {
                _frames[m] = new Sprite[6];
                for (int f = 0; f < 6; f++) _frames[m][f] = Art.Get((m == 4 ? "jigo-clip/" : "jigo/") + Art54.Moods[m] + "/" + (f + 1).ToString("00"));
            }
            // Frames are pre-clipped to the arch: High stripping removes SpriteMask from the player.
            var portraitSize = Vector2.one * (size.x * .85f);
            _back = Art54.Place(parent, _joy ? Art54.Joy : Art54.NormalFrame, center, portraitSize, order);
            _front = Art54.Place(parent, _joy ? Art54.Joy : Art54.NormalFrame, center, portraitSize, order + 1);
            Sync(session);
            Update();
        }

        void Sync(GameSession session)
        {
            _time = session.TimeLeft;
            _lost = session.IsOver && session.Outcome != Outcome.Win;
            _won = session.Outcome == Outcome.Win;
            _fuse = null;
            foreach (var bomb in session.Bombs)
                if (!_fuse.HasValue || bomb.Value < _fuse.Value) _fuse = bomb.Value;
        }

        public void OnEvent(GameEvent e, GameSession session)
        {
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved) _happyUntil = Time.unscaledTime + JigoMood.HappySeconds;
            Sync(session);
        }

        public void Update()
        {
            if (_joy) { _back.color = Color.clear; return; }
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            int mood = _forced >= 0 ? _forced : (int)JigoMood.Read(_time, _fuse, now < _happyUntil, _lost, _won);
            if (mood != _current)
            {
                _previous = _current < 0 ? mood : _current;
                _current = mood; _changedAt = now;
            }
            int frame = (int)(now * JigoMood.FramesPerSecond) % 6;
            _front.sprite = _frames[_current][frame];
            _back.sprite = _frames[_previous][frame];
            float alpha = Mathf.Clamp01((now - _changedAt) / JigoMood.FadeSeconds);
            _front.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, alpha);
            _back.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 1 - alpha);
        }
    }
}
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    // Resource keys shared by the HUD and booster bar.
    static class Art54
    {
        public const string Header = "hud/header", Top = "hud/top-strip", Bottom = "hud/bottom-strip",
            Panel = "hud/counter-panel", Piece = "hud/piece", Window = "hud/portrait-window",
            Socket = "hud/socket", Badge = "hud/badge",
            Lock = "hud/locked-icon", Pause = "hud/pause", Joy = "jigo/sevinc/01", NormalFrame = "jigo/normal/01";
        public static readonly string[] Bodies = { "hud/booster-normal", "hud/booster-pressed", "hud/booster-locked", "hud/booster-zero" };
        public static readonly string[] Settings = { "hud/settings-normal", "hud/settings-pressed", "hud/settings-locked", "hud/settings-zero" };
        public static readonly string[] Boosters = { "hud/scissors", "hud/wand", "hud/hammer", "hud/clock" };
        public static readonly string[] Moods = { "normal", "tedirgin", "korku", "mutlu", "uzgun" };

        public static SpriteRenderer Place(Transform parent, string key, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order)
        {
            var r = Art.Place(parent, key, size, order);
            if (r != null) r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return r;
        }

        public static SpriteRenderer Stretch(Transform parent, string key, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order)
        {
            var r = Place(parent, key, c, size, order);
            if (r != null) r.transform.localScale = new Vector3(size.x / r.sprite.bounds.size.x, size.y / r.sprite.bounds.size.y, 1);
            return r;
        }
    }
}

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* Faz 4 G8 switches (PRODUCT.md "Faz 4"). Both stay off until Zeyd says so:
         RedesignDefault  the redesigned set (redesign.json, R.. levels) becomes the
                          game's default set; only on Zeyd's command "yeni seti canlıya al".
         AskFun           the 👍/👎 question after a won level; only after
                          docs/sql/004_keyif.sql has been run and checked, with Zeyd's approval.
       The redesigned set can always be played with ?debug=1&set=redesign (nothing is sent). */
    public static class SetFlags
    {
        public static readonly bool RedesignDefault = true;    // Build 5 (PM_MESAJ_14): SET40 v3 at the root; published only on Zeyd's command
        public static readonly bool AskFun = true;             // Build 5: 👍/👎 on
    }

    /* Which level file the game plays. */
    public static class SetChoice
    {
        public enum Kind { Levels, Designs, Redesign, Zeydbot }

        /* set=zeydbot (ZeydBot sessions, Zeyd 2026-10-19) and set=redesign (SET40, PM 2026-10-21) work without debug: its rows and moves are sent
           under the player's name (TEST_ZEYD), every level open; nothing else changes for the live set. */
        public static Kind For(bool debug, string setParam, bool hasDesigns, bool hasRedesign, bool redesignDefault, bool hasZeydbot = false)
        {
            if (setParam == "zeydbot" && hasZeydbot) return Kind.Zeydbot;
            if (debug && setParam == "designs" && hasDesigns) return Kind.Designs;
            // PM 2026-10-21 (PM_MESAJ_5): the redesigned set opens without debug too, recorded like set=zeydbot
            if (setParam == "redesign" && hasRedesign) return Kind.Redesign;
            if (debug && setParam == "levels") return Kind.Levels;
            if (setParam == "classic") return Kind.Levels;            // Build 5: the old live 40 stay reachable
            if (redesignDefault && hasRedesign) return Kind.Redesign;
            return Kind.Levels;
        }
    }
}

namespace ReverseSolver.Platform
{
    /* C# side of WebBridge.jslib. Outside a web player the calls fall back to
       harmless defaults, so callers never need their own platform checks. */
    public static class WebBridge
    {
#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern void RS_BootReport(string message);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern string RS_Query(string name);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern float RS_PixelRatio();
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern float RS_SafeInset(int side);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern void RS_AskName(string obj, string method);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern void RS_PlayIntro(string parts, string wake, string skip, string ending, int sound, string obj, string method);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern void RS_WatchVisibility(string obj, string method);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] static extern void RS_SetLang(string code);
        public const bool InBrowser = true;
#else
        static void RS_BootReport(string message) => Debug.Log("[boot] " + message);
        static string RS_Query(string name) => null;
        static float RS_PixelRatio() => 1f;
        static float RS_SafeInset(int side) => 0f;
        static void RS_AskName(string obj, string method) { }
        static void RS_PlayIntro(string parts, string wake, string skip, string ending, int sound, string obj, string method) { }
        static void RS_WatchVisibility(string obj, string method) { }
        static void RS_SetLang(string code) { }
        public const bool InBrowser = false;
#endif

        public static void BootReport(string message) => RS_BootReport(message);
        public static string Query(string name) => RS_Query(name);
        public static float PixelRatio => RS_PixelRatio();
        public static float SafeInset(int side) => RS_SafeInset(side);
        public static void AskName(string obj, string method) => RS_AskName(obj, method);
        public static void PlayIntro(string parts, string wake, string skip, string ending, bool sound, string obj, string method) =>
            RS_PlayIntro(parts, wake, skip, ending, sound ? 1 : 0, obj, method);
        public static void WatchVisibility(string obj, string method) => RS_WatchVisibility(obj, method);
        public static void SetLang(string code) => RS_SetLang(code);
    }

    /* The page as Presentation sees it, installed before the first scene loads. */
    public sealed class WebHost : IHost
    {
        readonly IKeyValueStore _store = new PlayerPrefsStore();
        Action<string> _nameDone;
        Action<string> _introEvents;

        public string Query(string name) => WebBridge.Query(name);
        public float PixelRatio => WebBridge.PixelRatio;
        public Vector4 SafeInsets => new Vector4(WebBridge.SafeInset(0), WebBridge.SafeInset(1),
                                                 WebBridge.SafeInset(2), WebBridge.SafeInset(3));
        public void ReportBoot(string message) => WebBridge.BootReport(message);

        public IKeyValueStore Store => _store;
        public ITelemetryTransport Transport => WebTransport.Instance;
        public string SupabaseUrl => TelemetrySettings.SupabaseUrl;
        public string AnonKey => TelemetrySettings.AnonKey;
        public bool SendEnabled => TelemetrySettings.SendEnabled;

        /* Unity's WebGL player takes every key on the page by default (captureAllKeyboardInput),
           so a computer keyboard could not type into the name gate. Off while the gate is open,
           back on when the name arrives. The iPhone keyboard is the browser's own and unaffected. */
        public void AskName(Action<string> done)
        {
            _nameDone = done;
#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR
            WebGLInput.captureAllKeyboardInput = false;
#endif
            WebBridge.AskName(Receiver.Name, nameof(Receiver.OnPlayerName));
        }

        public void PlayIntro(string parts, string wake, string skip, string ending, bool sound, Action<string> events)
        {
            if (!WebBridge.InBrowser) { events("done"); return; }
            _introEvents = events;
            WebBridge.PlayIntro(parts, wake, skip, ending, sound, Receiver.Name, nameof(Receiver.OnIntroEvent));
        }

        internal void IntroEvent(string value)
        {
            var callback = _introEvents;
            if (value == "done") _introEvents = null;
            callback?.Invoke(value);
        }

        public event Action<bool> VisibilityChanged;

        internal void NameAnswered(string name)
        {
#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR
            WebGLInput.captureAllKeyboardInput = true;
#endif
            var d = _nameDone; _nameDone = null; d?.Invoke(LaunchPlan.NormalizePlayerName(name));
        }
        internal void Visibility(bool visible) => VisibilityChanged?.Invoke(visible);

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.BeforeSceneLoad)]
        static void Install()
        {
            if (!WebBridge.InBrowser) return;
            var host = new WebHost();
            Host.Current = host;
            Receiver.Create(host);
            WebBridge.WatchVisibility(Receiver.Name, nameof(Receiver.OnVisibility));
        }

        /* Target of the page's SendMessage calls. */
        sealed class Receiver : MonoBehaviour
        {
            public const string Name = "RSWebHost";
            WebHost _host;

            public static void Create(WebHost host)
            {
                var go = new GameObject(Name);
                DontDestroyOnLoad(go);
                go.AddComponent<Receiver>()._host = host;
            }

#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR
            void OnEnable() { Lang.Changed += SyncLanguage; SyncLanguage(); }
            void OnDisable() => Lang.Changed -= SyncLanguage;
            void SyncLanguage() => WebBridge.SetLang(Lang.Tr ? "tr" : "en");
#endif

            public void OnPlayerName(string name) => _host.NameAnswered(name);
            public void OnIntroEvent(string value) => _host.IntroEvent(value);
            public void OnVisibility(string state) => _host.Visibility(state != "hidden");
        }
    }
}
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* Codex art (docs/art/HANDOFF.md) for the SET40 v3 preview. Which A/B option is used is chosen in ONE
       place: Tools/art_choice.py copies that option's files into Resources/Art/ (the build carries only the
       chosen one). Art is on only for ?set=redesign (GameRoot sets On); the live set keeps the drawn look.
       Every file is @3x: 3 px = 1 pt, the game's world unit. A missing file falls back to the drawn look. */
    public static class Art
    {
        public static bool On;
        public static bool T28 = true;
        public static bool T54 => T28 && On;

        // PM_MESAJ_16: the player's Jiggle avatar, kept in the browser (PlayerPrefs = localStorage on WebGL), never sent.
        // Until Codex's five avatars arrive (Task 15): tinted copies of the approved one (blue, pink, red, green, purple).
        public static readonly string[] AvatarKeys = { "avatar-blue", "avatar-pink", "avatar-red", "avatar-green", "avatar-purple" };
        static readonly Color[] AvatarTints = { Color.white, new Color(1f, .55f, .8f), new Color(1f, .42f, .42f), new Color(.55f, 1f, .5f), new Color(.75f, .55f, 1f) };
        public static int AvatarIndex
        {
            get => Mathf.Clamp(PlayerPrefs.GetInt("rs_avatar", 0), 0, 4);
            set { PlayerPrefs.SetInt("rs_avatar", value); PlayerPrefs.Save(); }
        }
        /* The avatar sprite for slot i (Codex's own file when present, else the tinted placeholder). */
        public static SpriteRenderer Avatar(Transform parent, int i, Vector2 size, int order)
        {
            var r = Place(parent, "t20-" + AvatarKeys[i], size, order);
            if (r != null) return r;
            r = Place(parent, AvatarKeys[i], size, order);
            if (r != null) return r;
            r = Place(parent, "m13-profile-avatar", size, order);
            if (r != null) r.color = AvatarTints[i];
            return r;
        }
        // settings (PM_MESAJ_9): sound on/off, kept in the browser between visits
        public static bool Muted
        {
            get => PlayerPrefs.GetInt("rs_mute", 0) == 1;
            set { PlayerPrefs.SetInt("rs_mute", value ? 1 : 0); PlayerPrefs.Save(); }
        }
        static readonly Dictionary<string, Object> _cache = new Dictionary<string, Object>();

        static T Load<T>(string key) where T : Object
        {
            if (!On) return null;
            string cacheKey = (T28 ? "yeni/" : "") + key;
            if (!_cache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var o))
            {
                o = T28 ? Resources.Load<T>("Art/yeni/" + key) : null;
                _cache[cacheKey] = o = o != null ? o : Resources.Load<T>("Art/" + key);
            }
            return o as T;
        }

        public static Sprite Get(string key) => Load<Sprite>(key);
        public const string T23Win = "t23-win";
        public const string T23LossBomb = "t23-loss-bomb";
        public const string T23LossTime = "t23-loss-time";
        public const string T23Pause = "t23-pause";
        public const string T23RatingNormal = "t23-rating-normal";
        public const string T23RatingSelected = "t23-rating-selected";
        public const string T23Intro = "t23-intro";
        public const string T23Profile = "t23-profile";
        public const string T23Primary = "t23-button-primary";
        public const string T23Secondary = "t23-button-secondary";
        public static string T23Panel(string family) =>
            family == T23LossBomb || family == T23LossTime ? "t23-loss-panel" : family + "-panel";
        /* Codex Task 14 in-game set present (cards with a title banner, combined booster buttons, icon pills). */
        public static bool T14 => Get("loss-reason-bomb") != null;
        /* Codex Task 15 present (HUD pills without baked icons, short tall card buttons, Jiggles counter icon). */
        public static bool T15 => Get("jiggle-counter") != null;
        public static TMPro.TMP_FontAsset Lilita => On ? Resources.Load<TMPro.TMP_FontAsset>("Fonts/Lilita") : null;
        public static Texture2D Texture(string key) => Load<Texture2D>(key);
        public static AudioClip Sound(string key) => Load<AudioClip>("audio/" + key);

        /* An icon sprite centred at c (pt); false when the art is off (draw the vector icon instead). */
        public static bool Icon(Transform parent, string key, Vector2 c, float size, int order)
        {
            var r = Place(parent, key, new Vector2(size, size), order);
            if (r != null) r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return r != null;
        }

        /* A sprite fitted inside size (pt), centred on the parent's origin; sliced sprites fill size exactly. */
        public static SpriteRenderer Place(Transform parent, string key, Vector2 size, int order, bool sliced = false, bool tiled = false)
        {
            var s = Get(key);
            if (s == null) return null;
            var r = new GameObject(key).AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
            r.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            r.sprite = s;
            r.sortingOrder = order;
            if (tiled) { r.drawMode = SpriteDrawMode.Tiled; r.tileMode = SpriteTileMode.Continuous; r.size = size; }
            else if (sliced) { r.drawMode = SpriteDrawMode.Sliced; r.size = size; }
            else
            {
                float k = Mathf.Min(size.x / s.bounds.size.x, size.y / s.bounds.size.y);
                r.transform.localScale = new Vector3(k, k, 1);
            }
            return r;
        }
    }
}
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public enum BarAction { None, Pause }

    sealed class BoosterBar54
    {
        sealed class Slot
        {
            public Rect Rect;
            public SpriteRenderer Body, Icon, Badge, Lock;
            public TextView Count;
            public BoosterButton State;
            public bool Armed, Fresh, Initialized;
            public int Stock, Language;
        }

        readonly Slot[] _slots = new Slot[4];
        readonly Rect _pause;
        readonly SpriteRenderer _pauseBody;
        readonly TextView _pauseLabel, _prompt;
        readonly float _scale;
        int _language;
        string _notice;
        float _noticeUntil, _pressedUntil;

        public BoosterBar54(Transform root, Vector2 screen, float safeBottom, int order)
        {
            _scale = Hud54.Scale(screen.x);
            float top = Hud54.BottomTop(screen, safeBottom), cy = top + 15.5f * _scale;
            Art54.Stretch(root, Art54.Bottom, new Vector2(screen.x / 2, (top + screen.y) / 2), new Vector2(screen.x, screen.y - top), order);
            _prompt = TextView.Create(root, "prompt", "", 12.5f * _scale, Palette.Cream, order: order + 8);
            _prompt.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen.x / 2, Hud54.ButtonsTop(screen, safeBottom) - 12 * _scale);
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                float cx = (42 + i * 76.5f) * _scale;
                var c = new Vector2(cx, cy);
                var rect = new Rect(cx - 31 * _scale, cy - 31 * _scale, 62 * _scale, 62 * _scale);
                Art54.Place(root, Art54.Socket, c, Vector2.one * (62 * _scale), order + 1);
                var body = Art54.Place(root, i == 4 ? Art54.Settings[0] : Art54.Bodies[0], c, Vector2.one * (62 * _scale), order + 2);
                var icon = Art54.Place(root, i == 4 ? Art54.Pause : Art54.Boosters[i], c,
                    Vector2.one * ((i == 4 ? 32 : 62 * 195f / 232) * _scale), order + 3);
                if (i == 4)
                {
                    _pause = rect; _pauseBody = body; _language = Lang.Revision;
                    _pauseLabel = TextView.Create(root, "pauseLabel", Lang.T("Pause", "Durdur"), 11 * _scale, Palette.Cream, order: order + 4).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
                    _pauseLabel.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, cy + 39 * _scale);
                    continue;
                }
                var badgeCenter = c + new Vector2(25, 25) * _scale;
                var slot = _slots[i] = new Slot { Rect = rect, Body = body, Icon = icon };
                slot.Badge = Art54.Place(root, Art54.Badge, badgeCenter, Vector2.one * (24 * _scale), order + 4);
                slot.Lock = Art54.Place(root, Art54.Lock, badgeCenter, Vector2.one * (15 * _scale), order + 6);
                slot.Count = TextView.Create(root, "count", "", 15 * _scale, Palette.Cream, order: order + 5).Font(Art.Lilita).Outline(Palette.Navy, .25f);
                slot.Count.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(badgeCenter);
            }
        }

        public Rect RectOf(Booster b) => _slots[(int)b].Rect;
        public Booster? Hit(Vector2 pt)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) if (_slots[i].Rect.Contains(pt)) return (Booster)i;
            return null;
        }
        public BarAction HitAction(Vector2 pt)
        {
            if (!_pause.Contains(pt)) return BarAction.None;
            _pressedUntil = Time.unscaledTime + .12f;
            _pauseBody.sprite = Art.Get(Art54.Settings[1]);
            return BarAction.Pause;
        }

        public void Notice(string text, float seconds) { _notice = text; _noticeUntil = Time.unscaledTime + seconds; }

        string _lastPrompt;
        bool _lastQuiet;
        public void Refresh(BoosterControls bar, GameSession session, bool quiet)
        {
            int language = Lang.Revision;
            bool languageChanged = _language != language;
            if (languageChanged) { _language = language; _pauseLabel.Text = Lang.T("Pause", "Durdur"); }
            string prompt = quiet ? "" : !string.IsNullOrEmpty(bar.Prompt) ? bar.Prompt : Time.unscaledTime < _noticeUntil ? _notice : "";
            if (prompt != _lastPrompt || quiet != _lastQuiet || languageChanged)
            {
                _lastPrompt = prompt; _lastQuiet = quiet;
                _prompt.Text = !quiet && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(bar.Prompt) ? Texts.BoosterPrompt(bar) : prompt;
            }
            if (_pressedUntil > 0 && Time.unscaledTime >= _pressedUntil)
            {
                _pressedUntil = 0; _pauseBody.sprite = Art.Get(Art54.Settings[0]);
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                var b = (Booster)i; var s = _slots[i];
                var state = session.FreeUse == b && session.IsUnlocked(b) ? BoosterButton.Ready : bar.StateOf(b);
                bool armed = bar.Armed == b, fresh = bar.IsFresh(b) && state == BoosterButton.Ready && !armed;
                int stock = session.Stock(b);
                float glow = fresh ? .9f + .1f * Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * Mathf.PI * 2 / 1.3f) : 1;
                s.Body.color = new Color(1, glow, glow, 1);
                if (s.Initialized && s.State == state && s.Armed == armed && s.Fresh == fresh && s.Stock == stock && s.Language == language) continue;
                s.Initialized = true; s.State = state; s.Armed = armed; s.Fresh = fresh; s.Stock = stock; s.Language = language;
                bool locked = state == BoosterButton.Locked;
                s.Body.sprite = Art.Get(Art54.Bodies[armed ? 1 : locked ? 2 : state == BoosterButton.Out ? 3 : 0]);
                s.Icon.color = locked || state == BoosterButton.Out ? new Color(.55f, .55f, .55f, 1) : Color.white;
                s.Badge.gameObject.SetActive(!locked);
                s.Lock.gameObject.SetActive(locked);
                s.Count.Text = locked ? "" : stock.ToString();
            }
        }
    }
}
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    sealed class Hud54
    {
        public const float PulseAmount = .045f;
        readonly TextView _left, _clock, _piecesTitle, _timeTitle;
        readonly float _scale;
        readonly JigoPortrait54 _portrait;
        public void ShowClockPortrait(bool showing) => _portrait.Visible(!showing);
        int _lastLeft = -1, _lastTime = -1;
        int _language;

        public static float Scale(float width) => width / 390f;
        static float TopOffset(float width, float safeTop) => Mathf.Max(0, safeTop - 37 * Scale(width));
        public static float TopBottom(Vector2 screen, float safeTop) =>
            Mathf.Max(screen.y * .19f + 9 * Scale(screen.x), 168 * Scale(screen.x) + TopOffset(screen.x, safeTop));
        public static float BottomTop(Vector2 screen, float safeBottom) => screen.y - screen.y * .086f - safeBottom;
        public static float ButtonsTop(Vector2 screen, float safeBottom) => BottomTop(screen, safeBottom) - 15.5f * Scale(screen.x);

        public Hud54(Transform root, Vector2 screen, float safeTop, int order, GameSession session)
        {
            _scale = Scale(screen.x);
            float offset = TopOffset(screen.x, safeTop), bottom = TopBottom(screen, safeTop);
            Art54.Stretch(root, Art54.Top, new Vector2(screen.x / 2, bottom / 2), new Vector2(screen.x, bottom), order);
            _language = Lang.Revision;
            _left = Panel(root, 201f / 3, offset, order, out _piecesTitle, Lang.T("Pieces", "Parça"));
            _clock = Panel(root, 972f / 3, offset, order, out _timeTitle, Lang.T("Time", "Süre"));
            Art54.Place(root, Art54.Piece, new Vector2(39, 87) * _scale + new Vector2(0, offset), Vector2.one * (19 * _scale), order + 3);
            var center = new Vector2(195, 103) * _scale + new Vector2(0, offset);
            var windowSize = new Vector2(127, 104) * _scale;
            Art54.Stretch(root, Art54.Window, center, windowSize, order + 2);
            _portrait = new JigoPortrait54(root, center, windowSize, order + 3, session);
        }

        TextView Panel(Transform root, float x, float offset, int order, out TextView title, string label)
        {
            var shift = new Vector2(0, offset);
            var body = new Vector2(113, 290f / 3) * _scale;
            var center = new Vector2(x, (181 + 145) / 3f) * _scale + shift;
            Art54.Stretch(root, Art54.Panel, center, body, order + 1);
            Art54.Place(root, Art54.Header, new Vector2(x, (123 + 83) / 3f) * _scale + shift,
                new Vector2(364f / 3, 166f / 3) * _scale, order + 2);
            title = TextView.Create(root, "title", label, 16 * _scale, Palette.Cream, order: order + 4).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
            title.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(new Vector2(x, 62) * _scale + shift);
            var number = TextView.Create(root, "number", "", body.y * .34f, Palette.Cream, order: order + 4)
                .Heading(TextView.RimTeal);   // same thick rim as the titles: cream fill alone vanishes on the cream body
            number.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(center);
            return number;
        }

        public void SetLeft(int n)
        {
            if (n == _lastLeft) return;
            if (_lastLeft >= 0 && n < _lastLeft && TelemetryMeta.Current?.Cohort == "p21")
                Tweens.Run(_left, .2f, t => { if (_left != null) _left.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * Mathf.Lerp(1.15f, 1, t); });
            _lastLeft = n; _left.Text = n.ToString();
        }

        public void PauseTutorialTime() { _clock.gameObject.SetActive(false); _timeTitle.gameObject.SetActive(false); }

        public void SetTime(int seconds)
        {
            int t = Mathf.Max(0, seconds);
            if (t != _lastTime)
            {
                _lastTime = t;
                _clock.Text = $"{t / 60}:{t % 60:00}";
                _clock.Color = t <= JigoMood.WorriedSeconds ? Palette.Coral : Palette.Cream;
            }
            float pulse = t <= JigoMood.WorriedSeconds ? 1 + PulseAmount * Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * Mathf.PI * 2) : 1;
            _clock.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * pulse;
            if (_language != Lang.Revision)
            {
                _language = Lang.Revision; _piecesTitle.Text = Lang.T("Pieces", "Parça"); _timeTitle.Text = Lang.T("Time", "Süre");
            }
            _portrait.Update();
        }

        public void OnEvent(GameEvent e, GameSession session) => _portrait.OnEvent(e, session);
        public Vector3 ClockPosition => _clock.transform.localPosition;
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs Unity in batchmode with the full log in Logs/batch/<name>.log and prints only
# the summary: test counts and failing tests, compile errors, exceptions, and the
# lines the method itself tags with [tag]. Unity must be closed (project lock).
#
#   Tools/unity_batch.sh tests                              EditMode tests except Perf
#   Tools/unity_batch.sh tests Perf                         performance tests only
#   Tools/unity_batch.sh tests "ReverseSolver.Tests.FunSim" one fixture/namespace (-testFilter)
#   Tools/unity_batch.sh method <Namespace.Class.Method> [extra Unity args...]
#
# UNITY_TIMEOUT_MIN (default 300): the run is killed after this many minutes (exit 124).
# Exit code: 0 when the run and every test passed, 1 otherwise.
set -u
UNITY="${UNITY:-/c/Program Files/Unity/Hub/Editor/6000.6.4f1/Editor/Unity.exe}"
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
OUT="$ROOT/Logs/batch"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
LIMIT="${UNITY_TIMEOUT_MIN:-300}m"
stamp=$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)
mode="${1:-}"; shift || true

summary_log() {  # $1 = log file
  local log="$1"
  grep -E "error CS[0-9]+" "$log" | sort -u | head -20
  grep -E "^[A-Za-z.]*Exception|Aborting batchmode|Scripts have compiler errors" "$log" | sort -u | head -10
  grep -E "^\[[a-z0-9-]+\]" "$log" | tail -40
}

case "$mode" in
  tests)
    log="$OUT/tests-$stamp.log"; xml="$OUT/tests-$stamp.xml"
    filter=(-testCategory '!Perf')
    if [ $# -gt 0 ]; then
      if [ "$1" = Perf ]; then filter=(-testCategory Perf)
      else filter=(-testFilter "$1"); fi
    fi
    start=$(date +%s)
    timeout "$LIMIT" "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -projectPath "$ROOT" -runTests -testPlatform EditMode \
      -testResults "$xml" "${filter[@]}" -logFile "$log" >/dev/null 2>&1
    code=$?
    echo "tests: $(( $(date +%s) - start )) s, Unity exit $code, log $log"
    if [ -f "$xml" ]; then
      head -c 4000 "$xml" | grep -o '<test-run[^>]*>' | grep -oE '(total|passed|failed|skipped)="[0-9]+"' | tr '\n' ' '; echo
      grep -oE '<test-case [^>]*result="Failed"[^>]*>' "$xml" | grep -oE 'fullname="[^"]*"' | head -30
      grep -q 'result="Failed"' "$xml" && code=1
    else
      echo "no results xml"; summary_log "$log"; code=1
    fi
    exit $code ;;
  method)
    m="$1"; shift
    log="$OUT/$(echo "$m" | awk -F. '{print $(NF-1)"."$NF}')-$stamp.log"
    start=$(date +%s)
    timeout "$LIMIT" "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath "$ROOT" -executeMethod "$m" "$@" \
      -logFile "$log" >/dev/null 2>&1
    code=$?
    echo "$m: $(( $(date +%s) - start )) s, Unity exit $code, log $log"
    summary_log "$log"
    [ $code -eq 0 ] || exit 1 ;;
  *)
    sed -n '2,10p' "$0"; exit 2 ;;
esac

exec

    /* Boots the game and owns the screen: menu, level list, the board with its
       strip and booster bar, and the cards (intro, booster question, result).
       It runs one GameSession at a time; leaving a level unfinished (restart,
       menu, another level) ends the attempt as a quit row.

       Screen layout (points, y down), the web game's flex column:
         safe top + 4 | strip (10 + 46 + 6) | stage: tray centred |
         prompt 17 + booster bar 97 | 4 + safe bottom
       Cell size: Presentation BoardSizing (Core's vertical reserve, at least
       24 CSS points of horizontal clearance).
       Nothing is drawn below the safe bottom inset (iOS home indicator).

       URL: ?lv=N opens level N directly; ?debug=1 shows the load panel, the FPS
       line and unlocks every level. ?debug=1&set=designs plays the level
       editor's designs instead (designs.json), and lv may then be an id
       (?debug=1&set=designs&lv=D03). Without debug the set parameter is
       ignored; debug sessions never send telemetry. */

            // PM_MESAJ_43: the profile question waits for the first win of level 2 or later (NavigateResult)
            _profileSender.Flush();

        // ---- PM_MESAJ_25: who plays (cohort, device, platform, version; one profile question) --------

        TelemetryQueue _profileQueue;
        TelemetrySender _profileSender;

        void MarkIntro(string key)
        {
            Host.Current.Store.Set(key, "1");
            Host.Current.Store.Save();
        }

        void PlayStory(string parts, bool replay, System.Action done)
        {
            if (_introPlaying) return;
            _introPlaying = true;
            _drag?.Cancel();
            Host.Current.PlayIntro(parts, Texts.IntroWake, Texts.IntroSkip, Texts.IntroEnding, !Art.Muted, value =>
            {
                if (value == "done") { _introPlaying = false; done(); }
                else RecordIntro(value, replay);
            });
        }

        void RecordIntro(string value, bool replay)
        {
            string[] fields = value.Split('|');
            if (fields.Length < 3) return;
            string ev = fields[0] == "shown" ? "intro_shown" : fields[0] == "skip" ? "intro_skipped"
                : fields[0] == "wake" ? "intro_wake_tap" : fields[0] == "fail" ? "intro_failed" : null;
            if (ev == null || !_introSender.Enabled) return;
            if (!double.TryParse(fields[2], System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double sec)
                || double.IsNaN(sec) || double.IsInfinity(sec)) sec = 0;
            string id = System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            var m = TelemetryMeta.Current;
            var row = new Core.Json.JsonWriter().BeginObject().Field("row_id", id).Field("player", _save.Player)
                .Field("device_id", m.DeviceId).Field("cohort", m.Cohort).Field("app_version", m.AppVersion)
                .Field("event", ev).Field("part", fields[1]).Key("sec").Raw(sec.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
                .Key("tapped").Value(fields[0] == "wake" && fields.Length > 3 && fields[3] == "1")
                .Key("replay").Value(replay).EndObject().ToString();
            _introQueue.Add(id, row);
            _introSender.Flush();
        }

        static string PlatformName() =>
            Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.IPhonePlayer ? "ios"
            : Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.Android ? "android"
            : Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.WebGLPlayer && Application.isMobilePlatform ? "mobile-web" : "desktop";

        static string AppVersion() => (Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.IPhonePlayer ? "ios-" : "web-") + BuildStamp.Value;

        string Cohort => TelemetryMeta.Current?.Cohort;
        string ProfileKey => "rs_profile_" + Cohort;

        /* Once per cohort on this device, right after the name: "Do you play puzzle games like this?" */
        void AskProfile()
        {
            if (Cohort == null || _showSet || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_save.Player) || Host.Current.Store.Get(ProfileKey) != null) return;
            OpenCard("profile", new Card(_world, _screen, CardOrder, "Profile") { Compact = true }.Title(Texts.AskProfileTitle)
                .Body(Texts.AskProfile, sizePt: 14, gapAfter: 8)
                .Button("exp_0", Texts.ProfileNever, false)
                .Button("exp_1", Texts.ProfileSometimes, false)
                .Button("exp_2", Texts.ProfileOften, false).Finish());
        }

        void AnswerProfile(int exp)
        {
            Host.Current.Store.Set(ProfileKey, exp.ToString());
            Host.Current.Store.Save();
            var m = TelemetryMeta.Current;
            if (_telemetry.Enabled) _profileQueue.Add(m.Cohort + "|" + _save.Player + "|" + m.DeviceId, ProfileRow.Json(m.Cohort, _save.Player, m.DeviceId, m.Platform, exp));
            _profileSender.Flush();
            CloseCard();
            // PM_MESAJ_40: the question may have covered a level's intro card; it comes back, never swallowed
            if (_mode == Mode.Playing && _introOpen) OpenCard("intro", Screens.Intro(_world, _screen, _session.Level, CardOrder));
        }

        /* 1-10 after a level: SET40 with a cohort asks once per level (win or loss, "Skip" allowed); without a cohort
           SET40 does not ask; the ZeydBot set asks after every attempt, as before. */
        bool AskHardNow(string levelId) =>
            _redesignSet ? Cohort != null && Host.Current.Store.Get("rs_hard_" + Cohort + "_" + levelId) == null : _zeydbotSet;

        void HardAsked(string levelId)
        {
            if (!_redesignSet || Cohort == null) return;
            Host.Current.Store.Set("rs_hard_" + Cohort + "_" + levelId, "1");
            Host.Current.Store.Save();
        }

        /* iOS: the levels saved from GitHub Pages at an earlier launch (Platform.RemoteLevels), when they still parse
           as a full set; otherwise the copy inside the app. */
        string RedesignJson()
        {
            string remote = Application.platform == RuntimePlatform.IPhonePlayer ? PlayerPrefs.GetString("rs_remote_redesign", null) : null;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(remote))
            {
                try { if (LevelParser.Parse(remote).Levels.Count >= 40) return remote; } catch { }
            }
            return redesign.text;
        }

        LevelSet ChooseSet()
        {
            // PM_MESAJ_17: lv=SHOW1 opens the demo level by itself, with the new look and the painting
            // PM_MESAJ_19: ?lang=en|tr sets the language (shared links, QA); otherwise the saved choice, English first
            string lang = Host.Current.Query("lang");
            if (lang == "tr" || lang == "en") Lang.Tr = lang == "tr";
            if (Host.Current.Query("lv") == "SHOW1" || Host.Current.Query("lv") == "SHOW_ART")
            {
                var show = Resources.Load<TextAsset>(Host.Current.Query("lv") == "SHOW_ART" ? "show_art" : "show");
                if (show != null) { _showSet = true; _newArt = Art.On = true; return LevelParser.Parse(show.text); }
            }
            Art.On = false;                                    // static: no domain reload between Play sessions
            _paintSet = false;
            // PM_MESAJ_48: level-agent preview sets (?set=ajan1 …) from Resources/AjanSets/<name>.json; recorded and unlocked like set=zeydbot
            string setName = Host.Current.Query("set");
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(setName) && setName.StartsWith("ajan"))
            {
                var ajan = Resources.Load<TextAsset>("AjanSets/" + setName);
                if (ajan != null) { _zeydbotSet = !Host.Debug; _newArt = Art.On = _paintSet = true; return LevelParser.Parse(ajan.text); }
            }
            switch (SetChoice.For(Host.Debug, Host.Current.Query("set"), HasLevels(designs), HasLevels(redesign), SetFlags.RedesignDefault, HasLevels(zeydbot)))
            {
                case SetChoice.Kind.Zeydbot: _zeydbotSet = true; return LevelParser.Parse(zeydbot.text);
                case SetChoice.Kind.Designs: _designSet = true; return LevelParser.Parse(designs.text);
                // SET40 (PM_MESAJ_5): recorded like the ZeydBot set (moves, 1-10), also when the flag makes it the default
                case SetChoice.Kind.Redesign: _zeydbotSet = !Host.Debug; _redesignSet = true; _newArt = Art.On = _paintSet = true; return LevelParser.Parse(RedesignJson());
                default: return LevelParser.Parse(levels.text);
            }
        }

        /* ZeydBot: every action of the attempt with its time and the time spent before it (thinking). */
        void RecordMove(GameEvent e)
        {
            string kind;
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved) kind = e.ByHammer ? "hammer" : "exit";
            else if (e.Kind == EventKind.Jammed) kind = "jam";
            else if (e.Kind == EventKind.BoosterUsed && e.Booster != Booster.Hammer) kind = e.Booster.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
            else return;
            string dir = e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved && !e.ByHammer ? e.Dir.ToString()
                       : e.Kind == EventKind.Jammed ? _drag?.Direction?.ToString() : null;   // jam: the way it was pulled (null: a press on a nail)
            WriteMove(kind, e.Piece, dir);
        }

        /* One moves row; touch rows carry how long the piece was held. Pause / resume and touches do not
           reset the thinking clock: think_ms runs from the last real action. */
        void WriteMove(string kind, int piece, string dir, int? holdMs = null)
        {
            if (_moveQueue == null || _session == null) return;
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            var fuses = string.Join(";", System.Linq.Enumerable.Select(_session.Bombs, b => b.Key + ":" + b.Value));
            int no = kind == "start" ? 0 : ++_moveNo;                  // start: move_no 0 (PM_MESAJ_25)
            _moveQueue.Add(_rowId + "-" + no, MoveRow.Json(_rowId, _save.Player, _session.Level.Id, _session.Level.Version, _session.Attempt,
                no, kind, piece, dir, (int)((now - _moveT0) * 1000), (int)((now - _moveLast) * 1000), _session.TimeLeft, fuses, holdMs,
                Cohort == "p21" && (kind == "exit" || kind == "jam" || kind == "tut") ? _moveInput : null));
            bool action = kind != "touch" && kind != "hide" && kind != "show" && kind != "start" && kind != "quit";
            if (action) _moveLast = now;
            // sent on hide, on quit and every 10 rows, so a closed tab loses at most the last few (PM_MESAJ_25)
            if (kind == "hide" || kind == "quit" || no % 10 == 0) _moveSender?.Flush();
        }

        static bool HasLevels(TextAsset file)
        {
            if (file == null) return false;
            var root = Core.Json.JsonReader.Parse(file.text);
            return root.TryGet("levels", out var list) && list.Count > 0;
        }

        /* lv as a number (1-based position) or as a level id. */
        bool TryLevelIndex(string lv, out int index)
        {
            index = 0;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(lv)) return false;
            if (int.TryParse(lv, out int n)) { index = Mathf.Clamp(n, 1, _set.Levels.Count) - 1; return true; }
            for (int i = 0; i < _set.Levels.Count; i++)
                if (_set.Levels[i].Id == lv) { index = i; return true; }
            return false;
        }

        void OnVisibility(bool visible)
        {
            _pageHidden = !visible;
            if (!visible && _deferredLoss != null)
            {
                ResolveContinue("withdrawn", false);
                _continueResultOnReturn = true;
            }
            else if (!visible && _continuePreview) CloseContinue();
            if (_session != null && _session.Continues > 0 && !_session.IsOver)
            {
                if (visible) _continueQueue.Remove(_rowId + ":final");
                else RecordContinueFinal(_session.PreviewQuit(), false);
            }
            if (visible)
            {
                _continueSender?.Flush();
                if (_continueResultOnReturn) { _continueResultOnReturn = false; ShowResult(); }
            }
            if (_zeydbotSet && _mode == Mode.Playing && _session != null && !_session.IsOver) WriteMove(visible ? "show" : "hide", -1, null);
            if (visible) _telemetry.PageVisible(_mode == Mode.Playing ? _session : null, _rowId);
            else
            {
                _telemetry.PageHidden(_mode == Mode.Playing ? _session : null, _rowId);
                if (!_previewWins) _save.Store(_log);
            }
        }

        // ---- flow -------------------------------------------------------------------------

        void LeaveLevel()
        {
            _win21?.Destroy(); _win21 = null; _winPreview21 = false;
            ResetBoosters21();
            _continueResultOnReturn = false;
            if (_deferredLoss != null) ResolveContinue("withdrawn", false);
            CloseContinue();
            _celebration?.Destroy();
            _celebration = null;
            _celebrationResult = false;
            if (_session == null) return;
            if (!_session.IsOver) { WriteMove("quit", -1, null); _session.Quit(); }   // OnEvent logs the quit row
            _session.EventRaised -= OnEvent;
        }

        void ShowMenu()
        {
            CommitPendingHard();
            _rated = null;
            LeaveLevel();
            _session = null;
            if (PlayOverride.Exit != null) { PlayOverride.Exit(); return; }   // back to the level editor
            _mode = Mode.Menu;
            Layout();
            AskProfileIfDue();
        }

        /* PM_MESAJ_43: asked once, after "Next" or "Menu" on the first won level numbered 2 or more. On the next level
           it covers the intro card, which comes back when the question is answered (AnswerProfile). */
        bool _profileDue;
        void AskProfileIfDue()
        {
            if (!_profileDue) return;
            _profileDue = false;
            AskProfile();
        }

        void StartLevel(int index)
        {
            LeaveLevel();
            _index = Mathf.Clamp(index, 0, _set.Levels.Count - 1);
            var level = _set.Levels[_index];
            // G1: this level's picture and the next one's; let go of the rest
            string nextImage = ImageOf(_index + 1);
            _images.KeepOnly(ImageOf(_index), nextImage);
            _images.Get(nextImage);
            CommitPendingHard();
            _rated = null;                                     // answers come only while the result card is open
            _funAnswered = false;
            _askFun = false;
            _hardAnswered = _askHard = false;
            _session = _log.Start(level, Client, new Mulberry32((uint)System.Environment.TickCount));
            _session.ClockFreezesTime = P21Boosters;
            _rowId = _telemetry.NewRowId();
            if (!_previewWins) _save.Store(_log);                                 // the attempt counter, before anything can go wrong
            _trainingUse = null;
            _tutorialClockHold = false;
            _inventory = _redesignSet ? new BoosterInventory(Host.Current.Store, level,
                i => i >= 0 && i + (_p21Flow ? 2 : 0) < _set.Levels.Count && _progress.Won(i + (_p21Flow ? 2 : 0))) : null;
            _inventory?.Apply(_session);
            _session.EventRaised += OnEvent;
            _boosters = new BoosterControls(_session);
            _moveT0 = _moveLast = Time.unscaledTime;
            _moveNo = 0;
            _flickInputs = _dragInputs = 0;
            WriteMove("start", -1, null);                     // PM_MESAJ_25: an attempt that never ends still shows up
            _mode = Mode.Playing;
            _endAt = -1;
            // web loadLevel(i): the intro shows on a level's first start, not on retries
            bool firstVisit = !_introSeen.Contains(level.Id);
            _introOpen = !P21Prelude && Screens.HasIntro(level) && firstVisit;
            _introSeen.Add(level.Id);
            ResetScriptedTutorial(firstVisit);
            ResetP21Tutorial();
            Layout();
            _board?.Deal();                                    // web render(true): pieces deal in on a fresh start
            TestBooster21();
            TestWin21();
            if (Host.Debug) Debug.Log("[level-ready] " + level.Id);   // PM_MESAJ_28 B2: verify_build --only perf times this
            AskProfileIfDue();
            if (ContinueEnabled && Host.Current.Query("devam") == "1")
            {
                _continuePreview = true;
                _continueOffer = _continueRules.Offer(ContinueKind.Time, 0, level.TimerSeconds, _save.Coins);
                ShowContinue();
            }
            else if (Art.T54 && Host.Current.Query("jigo") == "sevinc") BeginCelebration(false);
        }

        /* Zeyd 2026-10-28: a new browser save (Safari's 7-day clean-up, Chrome or WhatsApp's own browser, another
           phone) loses the progress, but every win is already a `plays` row under the player's name. When the save is
           empty and a name is typed, those rows bring the best stars back (anon read, the schema untouched). */
        System.Collections.IEnumerator RecoverProgress(string name)
        {
            if (_resetVisit) yield break;
            var host = Host.Current;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(host.SupabaseUrl) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(host.AnonKey) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) yield break;
            string url = host.SupabaseUrl + "/rest/v1/plays?select=level_id,stars&result=in.(win,won_after_continue)&player=eq." + UnityEngine.Networking.UnityWebRequest.EscapeURL(name);
            using (var req = UnityEngine.Networking.UnityWebRequest.Get(url))
            {
                req.SetRequestHeader("apikey", host.AnonKey);
                req.SetRequestHeader("Authorization", "Bearer " + host.AnonKey);
                yield return req.SendWebRequest();
                if (req.result != UnityEngine.Networking.UnityWebRequest.Result.Success) { if (Host.Debug) Debug.Log("[recover] " + req.error); yield break; }
                var best = new Dictionary<string, int>();
                try
                {
                    var rows = ReverseSolver.Core.Json.JsonReader.Parse(req.downloadHandler.text);
                    if (rows.Kind == ReverseSolver.Core.Json.JsonKind.Array)
                        foreach (var row in rows.Items)
                            if (row.TryGet("level_id", out var id) && row.TryGet("stars", out var st) && st.Kind == ReverseSolver.Core.Json.JsonKind.Number)
                                best[id.AsString] = System.Math.Max((int)st.AsNumber, best.TryGetValue(id.AsString, out int b) ? b : 0);
                }
                catch (System.Exception) { yield break; }
                int n = _save.MergeBest(best);
                if (Host.Debug) Debug.Log($"[recover] {name}: {best.Count} won levels on the server, {n} restored");
                if (n == 0) yield break;
                _log = _save.Restore();
                _progress = new Progress(_log, _set.Levels) { UnlockAll = _progress.UnlockAll };
                if (_mode == Mode.Menu) Layout();
            }
        }

        void ResetScriptedTutorial(bool firstVisit)
        {
            _tutorialScript ??= TutorialScript.Load();
            _tutorialSteps = _redesignSet && !PlayOverride.Active && !P21Prelude && !P21StoryLevel ? _tutorialScript.ForSlot(_index + 1 - (_p21Flow ? 2 : 0), firstVisit) : new List<TutorialStep>();
            _tutorialSteps.RemoveAll(step => (step.tur == "booster" || step.tur == "booster_hedef") &&
                (!TutorialScript.BoosterOf(step.booster, out var b) || _inventory == null || _inventory.Trained(b) || !_session.IsUnlocked(b)));
            _tutorialAt = 0;
            _tutorialUntil = -1;
            _session.TutorialMove = false;
        }

        /* Scripted SET40 steps, with the old hand retained for other tutorials. */
        void ShowTutorialStep()
        {
            if (P21StoryLevel) return;
            if (_redesignSet && !PlayOverride.Active && _tutorialScript.ForSlot(_index + 1, true).Count > 0)
            {
                ShowScriptedTutorial();
                return;
            }
            if (_board == null || !Tutorial.Step(_session, out var m)) return;
            // Zeyd 2026-10-28: the hand on level 1's first two moves only, never on level 2 (tutorial 2)
            int done = _session.Level.Pieces.Count - _session.Board.PresentCount;
            if (_session.Level.Tutorial != 1 || done >= 2) return;
            var origin = PlayingBoardPlacement(_screen, _safe, _session.Level, out _);
            _hand = TutorialHand.Show(_board, _board.PieceViewOf(m.Piece), m, HandOrder, new Rect(-origin, _screen));
            if (_session.Level.Tutorial == 1 || done == 0) _bar?.Notice(done == 0 ? Texts.TutorialFirst : Texts.TutorialNext, 2.5f);
        }

        void ShowScriptedTutorial()
        {
            if (_spotlight != null) Destroy(_spotlight.gameObject);
            _spotlight = null;
            bool boosterStep = TutorialActive && (_tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur == "booster" || _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur == "booster_hedef");
            _bar?.ShowTutorial(TutorialActive && !_introOpen && !boosterStep);
            if (_introOpen || _board == null) return;
            while (TutorialActive)
            {
                var step = _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt];
                bool valid = step.tur == "kural" || (step.tur == "goster" && TutorialScript.ObstacleOf(_session.Level, step.engel, out _, out _));
                if (step.tur == "surukle")
                    valid = TutorialScript.MoveOf(_session.Level, step, out _tutorialMove) &&
                        (_tutorialUntil >= 0 || (_session.Board.IsPresent(_tutorialMove.Piece) && _session.NailAt(_tutorialMove.Piece) == 0 && _session.Probe(_tutorialMove.Piece, _tutorialMove.Dir).Exit));
                if (step.tur == "hata")
                {
                    valid = _tutorialUntil >= 0 || TutorialScript.ErrorMove(_session, step, out _tutorialMove);
                    if (valid && _tutorialAt + 1 < _tutorialSteps.Count && _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt + 1].tur == "surukle")
                    {
                        var next = _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt + 1];
                        _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt + 1] = new TutorialStep { slot = next.slot, adim = next.adim,
                            tur = next.tur, parca = _tutorialMove.Piece, metinAnahtari = next.metinAnahtari };
                    }
                }
                _session.TutorialMove = valid && step.tur == "hata";
                if (TutorialScript.BoosterOf(step.booster, out var booster))
                {
                    if (step.tur == "booster")
                    {
                        valid = true;
                        // Preview the free use at zero stock without altering persistent inventory.
                        if (_tutorialUntil < 0) _session.FreeUse = booster;
                    }
                    else if (step.tur == "booster_hedef")
                        valid = _tutorialUntil >= 0 || TutorialScript.BoosterTarget(_session, booster, out _boosterTarget, out _boosterJoint);
                }
                if (valid)
                {
                    _spotlight = boosterStep
                        ? TutorialSpotlight.ShowBooster(_world, _screen, _board, step, _bar.RectOf(booster), _boosterTarget, _boosterJoint)
                        : TutorialSpotlight.Show(_world, _screen, _board, step, _tutorialMove);
                    if (_tutorialUntil >= 0)
                    {
                        if (step.tur == "hata") _spotlight.Stuck(); else _spotlight.Success();
                    }
                    return;
                }
                Debug.LogWarning($"Tutorial: skipped slot {_index + 1}, step {step.adim} (missing target or invalid move).");
                _tutorialAt++;
            }
            _bar?.ShowTutorial(false);
        }

        void NextScriptedTutorial()
        {
            if (_drag.Active)
            {
                var limit = _drag.Limit;
                var result = _drag.Release(out int piece, out var dir);
                _board.EndDrag(piece, result, dir, limit.Cells);
            }
            _session.TutorialMove = false;
            _tutorialAt++;
            _tutorialUntil = -1;
            ShowScriptedTutorial();
        }

        // Run before DragModel.Move: even probing a wrong direction must not record a jam.
        bool RejectTutorialDirection(Vector2 pt)
        {
            if (!TutorialActive || _tutorialUntil >= 0 ||
                (_tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur != "surukle" && _tutorialSteps[_tutorialAt].tur != "hata") || !_drag.Active) return false;
            var delta = pt - _tutorialPress;
            if (!TutorialScript.WrongDirection(_tutorialMove.Dir, _drag.Direction, delta.x, delta.y, out var dir)) return false;
            int piece = _drag.Piece;
            _drag.Cancel();
            _board.TutorialShake(piece, dir);
            _spotlight?.Restart();
            return true;
        }

        void MenuAction(Vector2 pt)
        {
            if (Art.On)
            {
                if (_profileCard != null) { ProfileAction(_profileCard.Hit(pt)); return; }
                if (_menuSettings != null) { SettingsAction(_menuSettings.Hit(pt)); return; }
                if (_paintingDetail != null)
                {
                    string action = _paintingDetail.Hit(pt);
                    if (action == "replay") { int index = _paintingDetail.Index; _paintingDetail.Destroy(); _paintingDetail = null; StartLevel(index); }
                    else if (action == "close") { _paintingDetail.Destroy(); _paintingDetail = null; }
                    return;
                }
                int tab = _tabs?.Hit(pt) ?? -1;
                if (tab >= 0)
                {
                    if (_tabAnimating) return;
                    RememberMenuScroll(); _tabAnimating = true;
                    _tabs.Press(tab, () => { _tabAnimating = false; if (_mode != Mode.Menu) return; _tab = (MenuTab)tab; _levelsMapOpen = false; Layout(); });
                    return;
                }
                if (_tabs != null && pt.y >= _tabs.Top - 24) return;
                if (_levelsMap != null)
                {
                    int hit = _levelsMap.Hit(pt);
                    if (hit == -2) { RememberMenuScroll(); _levelsMapOpen = false; Layout(); }
                    else if (hit >= 0) StartLevel(hit);
                    return;
                }
                if (_collection != null)
                {
                    int hit = _collection.Hit(pt);
                    if (hit >= 0)
                    {
                        if (!_progress.Won(hit) && hit == _progress.Next()) StartLevel(hit);
                        else _paintingDetail = new PaintingDetail(_world, _screen, hit, _images, CardOrder, i => _stats.Of(_set.Levels[i].Id)?.BestWinSeconds);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                if (_jiggles != null)
                {
                    int avatar = _jiggles.Hit(pt);
                    if (avatar >= 0) { Art.AvatarIndex = avatar; RememberMenuScroll(); Layout(); }
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (_menu == null) return;
            if (_menu.Settings.Contains(pt)) OpenSettings();
            else if (_menu.Profile.Contains(pt) && Art.On) OpenPlayerProfile();
            else if (_menu.Play.Contains(pt)) StartLevel(_progress.Next());
            else if (_menu.Levels.Contains(pt))
            {
                if (Art.On) { _levelsMapOpen = true; _mapOffset = null; Layout(); }
                else OpenList();
            }
        }

        void RememberMenuScroll()
        {
            if (_collection != null) _collectionOffset = _collection.Scroll.Resting;
            if (_levelsMap != null) _mapOffset = _levelsMap.Scroll.Resting;
            if (_jiggles != null) _jigglesOffset = _jiggles.Scroll.Resting;
            if (_achievements != null) _achievementsOffset = _achievements.Scroll.Resting;
        }

        AchievementProgress[] CurrentAchievements() => Achievements.Compute(_set.Levels, _progress.Won, _stats, _save);

        void OpenPlayerProfile()
        {
            int played = 0, first = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < _set.Levels.Count; i++)
            {
                string id = _set.Levels[i].Id;
                if (_log.AttemptsOf(id) > 0) played++;
                if (_stats.FirstTry(id, _progress.Won(i), _save)) first++;
            }
            string[] values = { $"{_progress.Next() + 1}/{_set.Levels.Count}", Texts.Count(SavedJiggles()), Texts.Minutes(_stats.TotalSeconds / 60),
                played == 0 ? "—" : $"{first * 100 / played}%", $"{Achievements.Completed(CurrentAchievements())}/9",
                _stats.WinCount == 0 ? "—" : Texts.Seconds(_stats.SumLeftOnWins / _stats.WinCount) };
            _profileCard?.Destroy();
            _profileCard = new ProfileCard(_world, _screen, _save.Player, values, CardOrder);
        }

        void ProfileAction(string action)
        {
            if (action == null) return;
            _profileCard.Destroy(); _profileCard = null;
            if (action == "avatar") { RememberMenuScroll(); _tab = MenuTab.Jiggles; _levelsMapOpen = false; Layout(); }
            else if (action == "name")
            {
                _awaitingName = true;
                Host.Current.AskName(name =>
                {
                    _save.SetPlayer(name); _awaitingName = false;
                    if (_mode == Mode.Menu) { Layout(); OpenPlayerProfile(); }
                });
            }
        }

        void SettingsAction(string id)
        {
            if (id == null) return;
            if (id == "story")
            {
                _menuSettings?.Destroy(); _menuSettings = null;
                CloseCard();
                PlayStory("ABC", true, ShowMenu);
                return;
            }
            if (id == "privacy") { Application.OpenURL(MenuSettings.PrivacyUrl); return; }
            if (id == "contact") { Application.OpenURL(MenuSettings.ContactUrl); return; }
            if (id == "sound") Art.Muted = !Art.Muted;
            else if (id == "music") Music.On = !Music.On;
            else if (id == "haptics") Haptics.On = !Haptics.On;
            else if (id == "tr" || id == "en")
            {
                Lang.Tr = id == "tr"; RememberMenuScroll(); Layout(); OpenSettings(); return;
            }
            _menuSettings.Destroy(); _menuSettings = null;
            if (id != "close") OpenSettings();
        }

        int TotalStars()
        {
            int n = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < _set.Levels.Count; i++) n += _progress.Stars(i);
            return n;
        }

        /* PM_MESAJ_9 profile box: every piece of a won level is a rescued Jiggle. */
        int SavedJiggles()
        {
            int n = 0;
