using System.Collections.Generic;
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    // Passive T42 layers. GameRoot owns input, timing and the delayed Core command.
    sealed class P21BoosterView
    {
        const int Order = 3500;
        readonly Transform _root;
        readonly Vector2 _screen;
        readonly float _safeTop;
        readonly BoardView _board;
        readonly BoosterBar _bar;
        readonly Dictionary<string, Sprite> _sprites = new Dictionary<string, Sprite>();
        Transform _selection;
        SpriteRenderer _ring, _tool, _shadow, _burst, _trail, _cut, _clock, _flyingClock, _button, _barFrame, _barFill;
        Booster? _selected;
        int _zone = -2, _language = -1;
        float _clockEnded = -1;
        bool _clockWasActive;
        public Rect ConfirmRect { get; private set; }

        public P21BoosterView(Transform parent, Vector2 screen, float safeTop, BoardView board, BoosterBar bar)
        {
            _root = new GameObject("P21 boosters").transform; _root.SetParent(parent, false);
            _screen = screen; _safeTop = safeTop; _board = board; _bar = bar;
            _tool = Layer(_root, "tool", Order + 10); _shadow = Layer(_root, "shadow", Order + 8);
            _burst = Layer(_root, "burst", Order + 11); _trail = Layer(_root, "trail", Order + 9);
            _cut = Layer(_root, "cut", Order + 10);
            _clock = Layer(_root, "Jigo clock", Order - 96); _flyingClock = Layer(_root, "flying clock", Order - 95);
            _button = Layer(_root, "dropped button", Order - 95);
            _barFrame = Layer(_root, "freeze channel", Order - 94); _barFill = Layer(_root, "freeze remaining", Order - 93);
        }

        static SpriteRenderer Layer(Transform parent, string name, int order)
        {
            var r = Draw.Child<SpriteRenderer>(parent, name); r.sortingOrder = order; r.enabled = false; return r;
        }

        void Paint(SpriteRenderer r, string key, Vector2 point, Vector2 size, float alpha = 1, float angle = 0)
        {
            if (key == null || alpha <= 0) { r.enabled = false; return; }
            if (!_sprites.TryGetValue(key, out var sprite)) _sprites[key] = sprite = Resources.Load<Sprite>("Art/yeni/boosters21/" + key);
            r.sprite = sprite; r.enabled = sprite != null;
            if (sprite == null) return;
            r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(point);
            r.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, angle);
            r.transform.localScale = new Vector3(size.x / sprite.bounds.size.x, size.y / sprite.bounds.size.y, 1);
            r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, alpha);
        }

        static string Frame(string path, int frame) => path + "/" + frame.ToString("00");
        public Rect ZoneRect(int index)
        {
            var r = WandRegion.Quadrant(_board.Session.Level, index);
            return new Rect(_board.Origin + new Vector2(r.X, r.Y) * _board.Cell, new Vector2(r.Width, r.Height) * _board.Cell);
        }

        public void Selection(Booster? selected, int zone, bool[] valid)
        {
            if (_selected == selected && _zone == zone && _language == Lang.Revision) return;
            _selected = selected; _zone = zone; _language = Lang.Revision;
            if (_selection != null)
            {
                var old = _selection;
                if (selected.HasValue) Object.Destroy(old.gameObject);
                else
                {
                    var shapes = old.GetComponentsInChildren<ShapeView>(); var sprites = old.GetComponentsInChildren<SpriteRenderer>();
                    var labels = old.GetComponentsInChildren<TextView>();
                    Tweens.Run(old, .1f, t =>
                    {
                        foreach (var shape in shapes) if (shape != null) shape.Alpha(1 - t);
                        foreach (var sprite in sprites) if (sprite != null) sprite.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 1 - t);
                        foreach (var label in labels) if (label != null) label.Alpha = 1 - t;
                    }, () => { if (old != null) Object.Destroy(old.gameObject); });
                }
                _selection = null;
            }
            _ring = null; ConfirmRect = default;
            if (!selected.HasValue) return;
            _selection = new GameObject("selection").transform; _selection.SetParent(_root, false);
            var area = new Rect(_board.Origin, _board.Size);
            void Dim(Rect r)
            {
                if (r.width <= 0 || r.height <= 0) return;
                ShapeView.Create(_selection, "dim", r.size, 0, Order).Fill(Draw.Rgba(0, 0, 0, .72f))
                    .transform.localPosition = Draw.W(r.center);
            }
            Dim(new Rect(0, 0, _screen.x, area.yMin));
            Dim(new Rect(0, area.yMax, _screen.x, _screen.y - area.yMax));
            Dim(new Rect(0, area.yMin, area.xMin, area.height));
            Dim(new Rect(area.xMax, area.yMin, _screen.x - area.xMax, area.height));
            var b = selected.Value;
            float scale = Hud54.Scale(_screen.x), top = Mathf.Max(_safeTop + 40 * scale, area.yMin - 130 * scale);
            Paint(Layer(_selection, "icon", Order + 2), "Selection/Icons/" + b.Id(), new Vector2(_screen.x / 2, top), Vector2.one * (78 * scale));
            Paint(Layer(_selection, "title badge", Order + 1), "Selection/title-badge", new Vector2(_screen.x / 2, top + 57 * scale), new Vector2(260, 48) * scale);
            var title = TextView.Create(_selection, "title", Texts.BoosterTitle21(b), 22 * scale, Palette.Cream, order: Order + 3).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
            title.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(_screen.x / 2, top + 57 * scale);
            var hint = TextView.Create(_selection, "instruction", Texts.BoosterHint21(b), 13 * scale, Palette.Cream, order: Order + 3);
            hint.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(_screen.x / 2, top + 94 * scale);
            var button = _bar.RectOf(b);
            Art54.Place(_selection, Art54.Bodies[1], button.center, button.size, Order + 1);
            Art54.Place(_selection, Art54.Boosters[(int)b], button.center, button.size * .84f, Order + 2);
            _ring = Layer(_selection, "sparkle ring", Order + 3);
            if (b != Booster.Wand) return;
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                var r = ZoneRect(i);
                Paint(Layer(_selection, "region", Order + 1), "Boosters/Wand/zone-" + (zone == i ? "selected" : "idle"), r.center, r.size);
                if (!valid[i]) ShapeView.Create(_selection, "unavailable", r.size, 0, Order + 2)
                    .Fill(Draw.Rgba(65, 65, 65, .65f)).transform.localPosition = Draw.W(r.center);
            }
            if (zone < 0) return;
            var center = new Vector2(_screen.x / 2, Mathf.Min(area.yMax + 40 * scale, _bar.RectOf(Booster.Wand).yMin - 45 * scale));
            ConfirmRect = new Rect(center - new Vector2(90, 35) * scale, new Vector2(180, 70) * scale);
            Paint(Layer(_selection, "confirm", Order + 3), "Boosters/Wand/confirm-normal", center, ConfirmRect.size);
            var confirm = TextView.Create(_selection, "confirm label", Texts.RegionShuffle, 24 * scale, Palette.Cream, order: Order + 4).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
            confirm.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(center);
        }

        public void TickSelection()
        {
            if (_ring != null && _selected.HasValue)
            {
                var r = _bar.RectOf(_selected.Value);
                Paint(_ring, Frame("Selection/selected-ring", 1 + (int)(Time.unscaledTime * 12) % 6), r.center, r.size * 1.35f);
            }
        }

        public void Effect(Booster? booster, float age, Vector2 target, bool vertical)
        {
            _tool.enabled = _shadow.enabled = _burst.enabled = _trail.enabled = _cut.enabled = false;
            if (!booster.HasValue) return;
            var b = booster.Value; var start = _bar.RectOf(b).center;
            float arrive = b == Booster.Scissors ? .3f : .4f;
            float travel = Mathf.Clamp01(age / arrive);
            var point = Vector2.Lerp(start, target, travel) - Vector2.up * (Mathf.Sin(travel * Mathf.PI) * 100);
            float size = Mathf.Lerp(68, 136, travel);
            string path; float angle = 0, alpha = 1;
            if (b == Booster.Hammer)
            {
                path = age < .4f ? Frame("Boosters/Hammer/flight", 1 + Mathf.Min(3, (int)(age * 15)))
                    : age < .67f ? Frame("Boosters/Hammer/strike", 1 + Mathf.Min(2, (int)((age - .4f) * 30)))
                    : Frame("Boosters/Hammer/rebound-fall", 1 + Mathf.Min(3, (int)((age - .67f) * 12)));
                if (age >= .27f && age < .4f) point.y -= 30 * Mathf.Sin((age - .27f) / .13f * Mathf.PI / 2);
                if (age >= .4f && age < .47f) point.y -= 30 * (1 - (age - .4f) / .07f);
                if (age >= .67f)
                {
                    float t = (age - .67f) / .33f;
                    point += new Vector2(180 * t, -80 * Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI) + 450 * t * t);
                    angle = -500 * t; alpha = 1 - t;
                }
                Paint(_shadow, "Boosters/Hammer/soft-shadow", Vector2.Lerp(start, target, travel) + new Vector2(0, 30), new Vector2(size, size * .35f), .55f * alpha);
                if (age >= .47f) Paint(_burst, Frame("Boosters/Hammer/impact", 1 + Mathf.Min(8, (int)((age - .47f) * 30))), target, Vector2.one * (_board.Cell * 3.8f), Mathf.Clamp01((1 - age) * 5));
            }
            else if (b == Booster.Scissors)
            {
                path = age < .55f ? Frame("Boosters/Scissors/walk", 1 + (int)(age * 14) % 4)
                    : Frame("Boosters/Scissors/exit", 1 + Mathf.Min(2, (int)((age - .55f) * 12)));
                if (age < .3f) Paint(_trail, "Boosters/Scissors/speed-trail", point, new Vector2(size * 1.5f, size * .5f), .7f, Mathf.Atan2(-(target.y - start.y), target.x - start.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
                if (age >= .3f && age < .55f)
                {
                    var tip = target + (vertical ? Vector2.up : Vector2.right) * (_board.Cell * ((age - .3f) / .25f - .5f));
                    point = tip;
                    Paint(_cut, Frame("Boosters/Scissors/cut-line", 1 + Mathf.Min(5, (int)((age - .3f) * 25))), target, new Vector2(_board.Cell, _board.Cell * .24f), 1, vertical ? -90 : 0);
                }
                if (age >= .3f)
                    Paint(_burst, Frame("Boosters/Scissors/cut-bits", 1 + Mathf.Min(8, (int)((age - .3f) * 16))),
                        target + (vertical ? Vector2.up : Vector2.right) * (_board.Cell * (Mathf.Clamp01((age - .3f) / .25f) - .5f)),
                        Vector2.one * (_board.Cell * 3), Mathf.Clamp01((1 - age) * 5));
                if (age >= .55f) { float t = (age - .55f) / .45f; point.y += 260 * t * t; alpha = 1 - t; }
            }
            else
            {
                path = Frame("Boosters/Wand/tool", 1 + (int)(age * 8) % 3);
                if (age >= .4f && age < 1.2f)
                {
                    point += new Vector2(10 * Mathf.Sin(age * 18), -12 * Mathf.Sin(age * 9));
                    Paint(_burst, Frame("Boosters/Wand/touch-burst", 1 + Mathf.Min(8, (int)((age - .4f) * 16))), target, Vector2.one * (_board.Cell * 3.8f), Mathf.Clamp01((1.2f - age) * 3));
                    Paint(_trail, "Boosters/Wand/star-trail", point, new Vector2(180, 100), .8f);
                }
                if (age >= 1.2f) { float t = (age - 1.2f) / .3f; point.x += _screen.x * t; point.y -= 70 * t; angle = -140 * t; alpha = 1 - t; }
            }
            Paint(_tool, path, point, Vector2.one * size, Mathf.Clamp01(alpha), angle);
        }

        // Key canvases share a fixed portrait origin; the arm reaches right to the Time panel.
        public bool Clock(float age, double freezeLeft, bool stopped, float safeTop)
        {
            _clock.enabled = _flyingClock.enabled = _button.enabled = _barFrame.enabled = _barFill.enabled = false;
            if (age < 0) return false;
            float s = Hud54.Scale(_screen.x), offset = Mathf.Max(0, safeTop - 37 * s);
            var center = new Vector2(227, 103) * s + new Vector2(0, offset);
            string key;
            if (age < .18f) key = "release-button";
            else if (age < .4f) key = "take-clock";
            else if (age < .65f) key = Frame("reach", 1 + Mathf.Min(2, (int)((age - .4f) * 12)));
            else if (freezeLeft > 0 && !stopped)
            {
                key = Frame("hold", 1 + (int)(Time.unscaledTime * 8) % 2);
                _clockWasActive = true; _clockEnded = -1;
            }
            else
            {
                if (!_clockWasActive) return false;
                if (_clockEnded < 0) _clockEnded = Time.unscaledTime;
                float t = Time.unscaledTime - _clockEnded;
                if (t > .6f) { _clockWasActive = false; return false; }
                key = t < .25f ? Frame("retract", 1 + Mathf.Min(2, (int)(t * 12))) : t < .45f ? "recover-button" : "idle";
            }
            Paint(_clock, "Clock/Keys/" + key, center, new Vector2(284, 210) * s);
            if (age < .4f)
            {
                float t = Mathf.Clamp01(age / .4f);
                Paint(_flyingClock, "Clock/Layers/flying-clock", Vector2.Lerp(_bar.RectOf(Booster.Clock).center, new Vector2(245, 115) * s + new Vector2(0, offset), t), Vector2.one * (48 * s), 1 - Mathf.Clamp01((t - .8f) * 5));
                Paint(_button, "Clock/Layers/button-shield", new Vector2(225, 125 + t * 70) * s + new Vector2(0, offset), Vector2.one * (36 * s), 1 - t);
            }
            if (freezeLeft > 0 && !stopped)
            {
                var p = new Vector2(324, 150) * s + new Vector2(0, offset);
                Paint(_barFrame, "Clock/Layers/bar-frame", p, new Vector2(100, 22) * s);
                float fraction = Mathf.Clamp01((float)(freezeLeft / GameSession.ClockBonusSeconds));
                Paint(_barFill, "Clock/Layers/bar-fill", p - new Vector2((1 - fraction) * 47 * s, 0), new Vector2(94 * fraction, 16) * s);
            }
            return true;
        }
    }
}
using System;
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    // The same short result surface owns the chest; no intermediate celebration.
    sealed class P21WinView
    {
        const int Order = 5600;
        readonly Transform _root;
        readonly SaveData _save;
        readonly int _rescued;
        readonly Action _next, _refresh;
        Transform _content;
        Vector2 _screen;
        Rect[] _targets;
        float _scale, _top, _started, _tapAt = -10;
        int _chest, _taps, _language = -1, _shownReward = -1;
        Booster _booster;
        bool _chestMode, _done;
        SpriteRenderer _case, _light, _hand, _item, _glow, _trail, _arrival;
        SpriteRenderer[] _walkers;
        TextView _hint, _amount, _coins;
        Rect _nextRect, _chestRect;

        public P21WinView(Transform parent, Vector2 screen, Rect[] targets, SaveData save, int rescued,
            bool chest, Action next, Action refresh)
        {
            _root = new GameObject("P21 win and chest").transform; _root.SetParent(parent, false);
            _save = save; _rescued = rescued; _next = next; _refresh = refresh;
            _started = Time.unscaledTime;
            if (chest) BeginChest();
            Layout(screen, targets);
        }
        Vector2 At(float x, float y) => new Vector2(_screen.x * .5f + (x - 195) * _scale, _top + y * _scale);
        SpriteRenderer Layer(string name, int order) { var r = Draw.Child<SpriteRenderer>(_content, name); r.sortingOrder = order; return r; }
        void Paint(SpriteRenderer r, string key, Vector2 point, Vector2 size, float alpha = 1)
        {
            r.sprite = key == null ? null : Art.Get("win21/" + key);
            r.enabled = r.sprite != null && alpha > 0;
            if (!r.enabled) return;
            r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(point);
            float k = Mathf.Min(size.x / r.sprite.bounds.size.x, size.y / r.sprite.bounds.size.y);
            r.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * k; r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, alpha);
        }
        SpriteRenderer Image(string key, float x, float y, float w, float h, int layer = 1)
        {
            var r = Layer(key, Order + layer); Paint(r, key, At(x, y), new Vector2(w, h) * _scale); return r;
        }
        TextView Label(string text, float x, float y, float size, int layer = 10)
        {
            var t = TextView.Create(_content, "label", text, size * _scale, Palette.Cream, order: Order + layer);
            t.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(At(x, y)); return t;
        }
        public void Layout(Vector2 screen, Rect[] targets)
        {
            _screen = screen; _targets = targets; _scale = Mathf.Min(screen.x / 390, screen.y / 780);
            _top = (screen.y - 760 * _scale) * .5f;
            if (_content != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(_content.gameObject);
            _content = new GameObject("surface").transform; _content.SetParent(_root, false);
            ShapeView.Create(_content, "shade", screen, 0, Order).Fill(Draw.Rgba(10, 18, 46, .88f)).transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen * .5f);
            Image("Victory/panel", 195, 380, 370, 740);
            Image("Titles/light", 195, 105, 340, 125, 2);
            // Live Baloo lettering: gold extrusion behind the blue rim and cream face.
            Label(_chestMode ? Texts.Chest21 : Texts.Win21, 195, 112, 48, 3).Heading(Draw.Hex("#D79A26"));
            Label(_chestMode ? Texts.Chest21 : Texts.Win21, 195, 105, 48, 4).Heading(Draw.Hex("#153E75"));
            _hint = Label("", 195, 575, 22);
            _amount = Label("", 195, 458, 30, 16).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
            _case = Layer("chest", Order + 5); _light = Layer("light", Order + 6); _hand = Layer("hand", Order + 7);
            _glow = Layer("reward glow", Order + 12); _item = Layer("reward", Order + 14);
            _trail = Layer("flight trail", Order + 13); _arrival = Layer("arrival", Order + 15);
            _nextRect = new Rect(At(75, 645), new Vector2(240, 90) * _scale);
            _chestRect = new Rect(At(40, 205), new Vector2(310, 340) * _scale);
            if (!_chestMode)
            {
                Image("Victory/portrait-glow", 195, 285, 260, 250, 2);
                Image("Jigo/mutlu/03", 195, 280, 235, 235, 3);
                Image("Victory/Progress/panel", 195, 455, 325, 110, 2);
                int progress = _save.ChestProgress21, pending = _save.PendingChest21;
                int next = pending > 0 ? pending : P21ChestRules.Next(progress), previous = P21ChestRules.Previous(next - 1);
                int slots = next - previous;
                for (int i = 0; i < slots; i++) Image("Victory/Progress/slot-" + (previous + i < progress ? "complete" : "empty"), 72 + i * 43, 435, 39, 39, 3);
                Image("Victory/Progress/chest", 315, 435, 58, 55, 3);
                Label(pending > 0 ? Texts.Chest21 : Texts.ChestIn21(next - progress), 195, 481, 18);
                Label(Texts.Rescued21(_rescued), 195, 535, 24).Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
                Image("Victory/Procession/floor", 195, 622, 320, 45, 2);
                _walkers = new SpriteRenderer[_rescued + 1];
                for (int i = 0; i < _walkers.Length; i++) _walkers[i] = Layer("rescued", Order + 5);
                NextButton();
            }
            else
            {
                _walkers = null;
                Image("Reward/Flight/coin-counter", 290, 52, 116, 37, 8);
                Image("Reward/Flight/coin", 251, 52, 30, 30, 9);
                _coins = Label(_save.Coins.ToString(), 299, 52, 18);
                if (_done) NextButton();
                foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All)
                {
                    var r = Layer("destination", Order + 8);
                    Paint(r, "Reward/Reveal/Items/" + b.Id(), _targets[(int)b].center, _targets[(int)b].size * .8f);
                }
            }
            _language = Lang.Revision; Tick();
        }
        void NextButton()
        {
            Image("Victory/Next/normal", 195, 690, 250, 100, 3);
            Label(Texts.NextLevel21, 195, 690, 25).Heading(TextView.RimGreen);
        }
        void BeginChest()
        {
            _chestMode = true; _chest = _save.PendingChest21; _taps = 0; _done = false;
            _booster = (Booster)UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, 4); _shownReward = -1;
        }
        public void Press(Vector2 point)
        {
            if (Time.unscaledTime - _started < .25f) return;
            if (!_chestMode)
            {
                if (!_nextRect.Contains(point)) return;
                if (_save.PendingChest21 > 0) { BeginChest(); Layout(_screen, _targets); }
                else _next();
                return;
            }
            if (_done) { if (_nextRect.Contains(point)) _next(); return; }
            if (_taps >= 3 || !_chestRect.Contains(point) || Time.unscaledTime - _tapAt < 4f / 14) return;
            _taps++; _tapAt = Time.unscaledTime;
            if (_taps == 3)
            {
                if (_chest > 0 && _save.ClaimChest21(_chest, _booster)) _refresh();
            }
        }
        static string Frame(string path, int frame) => path + "/" + frame.ToString("00");
        public void Tick()
        {
            if (_language != Lang.Revision) { Layout(_screen, _targets); return; }
            float now = Time.unscaledTime, age = now - _tapAt;
            if (!_chestMode)
            {
                string[] colours = { "blue", "green", "pink", "purple", "red" };
                float move = (now - _started) * 30 % (_walkers.Length * 43 + 360);
                for (int i = 0; i < _walkers.Length; i++) Paint(_walkers[i], i == 0 ? Frame("Victory/Procession/Walk/jigo", 1 + (int)(now * 10) % 6) : Frame("Rescued/Run/" + colours[(i - 1) % colours.Length], 1 + (int)(now * 10) % 4), At(305 - i * 43 + move, 590), new Vector2(57, 65) * _scale);
                return;
            }
            string key = "Reward/Chest/closed", light = null;
            if (_taps > 0 && _taps < 3)
            {
                int frame = Mathf.Min(4, 1 + (int)(age * 14));
                key = age < 4f / 14 ? Frame("Reward/Chest/Shake" + _taps, frame) : "Reward/Chest/closed";
                if (_taps == 2) light = Frame("Reward/Chest/Leak", frame);
            }
            if (_taps == 3)
            {
                int frame = Mathf.Min(8, 1 + (int)(age * 12)); key = Frame("Reward/Chest/Open", frame);
                if (frame >= 3) light = Frame("Reward/Chest/OpenLight", frame);
            }
            Paint(_case, key, At(195, 350), new Vector2(310, 268) * _scale);
            Paint(_light, light, At(195, 350), new Vector2(310, 268) * _scale);
            Paint(_hand, _taps < 3 ? Frame("Reward/Touch/Hand", 1 + (int)(now * 10) % 5) : null, At(252, 460), new Vector2(85, 100) * _scale);
            _hint.Text = _taps < 3 ? Texts.TapChest21 : "";
            _hint.Alpha = .8f + .2f * Mathf.Sin(now * 4);
            if (_taps < 3 || age < .67f || _done) return;
            const float pop = .34f, hold = .55f, fly = .45f, arrival = .34f;
            const float cycle = pop + hold + fly + arrival;
            float rewardAge = age - .67f;
            int reward = (int)(rewardAge / cycle);
            if (reward >= 2)
            {
                Paint(_item, null, default, default); Paint(_glow, null, default, default); Paint(_trail, null, default, default); Paint(_arrival, null, default, default);
                _amount.Text = ""; _done = true;
                NextButton(); return;
            }
            float t = rewardAge - reward * cycle;
            string item = reward == 0 ? "gold" : _booster.Id();
            Vector2 center = At(195, 375), target = reward == 0 ? At(290, 52) : _targets[(int)_booster].center;
            Vector2 position = t < pop ? Vector2.Lerp(At(195, 350), center, t / pop) : t < pop + hold ? center : Vector2.Lerp(center, target, Mathf.Clamp01((t - pop - hold) / fly));
            float size = t < pop + hold ? 135 : Mathf.Lerp(135, 32, Mathf.Clamp01((t - pop - hold) / fly));
            Paint(_item, t >= pop + hold + fly ? null : t < pop ? Frame("Reward/Reveal/Pop/" + item, Mathf.Min(4, 1 + (int)(t * 12))) : "Reward/Reveal/Items/" + item, position, Vector2.one * size * _scale);
            Paint(_glow, t < pop + hold ? Frame("Reward/Reveal/Glow", 1 + (int)(t * 8) % 4) : null, center, Vector2.one * 195 * _scale);
            _amount.Text = t < pop + hold ? (reward == 0 ? "+" + P21ChestRules.Coins : "x" + P21ChestRules.BoosterCount) : "";
            Paint(_trail, t >= pop + hold && t < pop + hold + fly ? "Reward/Flight/trail" : null, position, new Vector2(90, 28) * _scale);
            _trail.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, -Mathf.Atan2(target.y - center.y, target.x - center.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
            Paint(_arrival, t >= pop + hold + fly ? Frame("Reward/Flight/arrival", Mathf.Min(4, 1 + (int)((t - pop - hold - fly) * 12))) : null, target, Vector2.one * 80 * _scale);
            if (_shownReward != reward && t >= pop + hold + fly)
            {
                _shownReward = reward; if (reward == 0) _coins.Text = _save.Coins.ToString();
            }
        }
        public void Destroy() { if (_root != null) UnityEngine.Object.Destroy(_root.gameObject); }
    }
}
using System.Collections.Generic;
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public sealed class LevelsMap
    {
        // MenuUi.Sprite adds the t20- prefix; node art is the Task23 final set (PM_MESAJ_37).
        const string BackSprite = "button-back";
        const string PathSprite = "t20-levels-path-repeat.png";
        const string CurrentNodeSprite = "t23-node-current";
        const string CompleteNodeSprite = "t23-node-complete";
        const string LockedBaseSprite = "t23-node-locked";
        const string HardLockedBaseSprite = "t23-node-hard-locked";
        const string ShadowSprite = "t23-node-contact-shadow";
        const string PlayBubbleSprite = "t23-play-bubble";
        const string HardBadgeSprite = "t23-hard-marker";
        const string LockBadgeSprite = "t23-lock-badge";
        const string WonBadgeSprite = "t23-complete-check";

        public readonly MenuScroll Scroll;
        readonly Rect _back;
        readonly List<(Rect rect, int index)> _nodes = new List<(Rect, int)>();
        public LevelsMap(Transform root, Vector2 screen, float top, float bottom, IReadOnlyList<LevelData> levels, Progress progress, LevelImages images, int order, float? offset)
        {
            MenuUi.Scene(root, "levels", screen, order - 10);
            MenuUi.Title(root, screen, top, Texts.Levels, order + 30, heading: true);
            _back = MenuUi.Button(root, BackSprite, null, new Vector2(30, top + 34), Vector2.one * 38, order + 32);
            _back = new Rect(_back.center - Vector2.one * 22, Vector2.one * 44);
            const float pitch = 76;
            float start = top + 90, height = levels.Count * pitch + 120;
            Scroll = new MenuScroll(root, start, bottom, height);
            MenuUi.Viewport(root, "levels", screen, start, bottom, order + 20);
            // Each repeat is 256 x 512 pt, centred on the same vertical path.
            var pathRoot = Scroll.Root;
            System.Action<Sprite> showPath = sprite =>
            {
                if (pathRoot == null) return;
                for (float y = start + 256; y < start + height + 256; y += 512)
                {
                    var path = new GameObject("levels-path-repeat").AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
                    path.transform.SetParent(pathRoot, false);
                    path.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen.x / 2, y + 256);
                    path.sprite = sprite; path.sortingOrder = order;
                    path.drawMode = SpriteDrawMode.Tiled; path.tileMode = SpriteTileMode.Continuous;
                    path.size = new Vector2(256, 512); path.transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
                }
            };
            var pathSprite = MenuArt.Instance.Get(PathSprite, showPath);
            if (pathSprite != null) showPath(pathSprite);
            for (int i = 0; i < levels.Count; i++)
            {
                bool current = i == progress.Next(), open = progress.Open(i), won = progress.Won(i), hard = levels[i].Hard;
                float x = screen.x / 2 + (i % 2 == 0 ? -64 : 64), y = start + 90 + (levels.Count - 1 - i) * pitch;
                var cell = Scroll.Cell(x, y, current ? 108 : 44);
                bool locked = !open && !won;
                float diameter = current ? 66 : 54;
                // The node sits on its step: a soft contact shadow under the coin, no outer glow.
                MenuUi.Sprite(cell, ShadowSprite, new Vector2(0, diameter / 2 - 2), new Vector2(diameter * .93f, diameter * .24f), order + 1);
                // A hard level keeps the red rim; when open, its gold or teal coin sits inside the rim.
                string coin = current ? CurrentNodeSprite : won ? CompleteNodeSprite : CurrentNodeSprite;
                if (locked) MenuUi.Sprite(cell, hard ? HardLockedBaseSprite : LockedBaseSprite, Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * diameter, order + 2);
                else if (hard)
                {
                    MenuUi.Sprite(cell, HardLockedBaseSprite, Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * diameter, order + 2);
                    MenuUi.Sprite(cell, coin, Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * diameter * .78f, order + 3);
                }
                else MenuUi.Sprite(cell, coin, Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * diameter, order + 2);
                if (current)
                {
                    var avatar = Art.Avatar(cell, Art.AvatarIndex, Vector2.one * 38, order + 4);
                    // on a hard node the avatar steps left so it clears the Baron badge (top right)
                    if (avatar != null) avatar.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(hard ? -12 : 0, -53);
                    var bubbleAt = new Vector2(0, -94);
                    if (MenuUi.Sprite(cell, PlayBubbleSprite, bubbleAt, new Vector2(84, 44), order + 6) == null)
                        ShapeView.Create(cell, "PlayBubble", new Vector2(84, 44), 2, order + 6).Radius(14)
                            .Fill(Draw.Hex("#3e9a51")).transform.localPosition = Draw.W(bubbleAt);
                    TextView.Create(cell, "play", Texts.Play, 20, Palette.Cream, false, order: order + 7).Heading(TextView.RimGreen).FitWidth(70, 14)
                        .transform.localPosition = Draw.W(bubbleAt + new Vector2(0, -3));
                    _nodes.Add((new Rect(x - 42, y - 116, 84, 44), i));
                }
                // Baron on top right, lock or tick on bottom right: the two never overlap.
                if (hard) MenuUi.Sprite(cell, HardBadgeSprite, new Vector2(diameter * .36f, -diameter * .4f), new Vector2(25, 29), order + 8);
                MenuUi.Label(cell, (i + 1).ToString(), Vector2.zero, 20, order + 5, current);
                if (locked) MenuUi.Sprite(cell, LockBadgeSprite, Vector2.one * (diameter * .36f), new Vector2(20, 22), order + 7);
                else if (won) MenuUi.Sprite(cell, WonBadgeSprite, Vector2.one * (diameter * .36f), Vector2.one * 18, order + 7);
                if (open || won) _nodes.Add((new Rect(x - 33, y - 33, 66, 66), i)); // at least 54 x 54 pt
            }
            float currentY = start + 90 + (levels.Count - 1 - progress.Next()) * pitch;
            Scroll.Set(offset ?? currentY - (start + (bottom - start) * .7f));
        }
        public int Hit(Vector2 pt)
        {
            if (_back.Contains(pt)) return -2;
            if (!Scroll.Contains(pt)) return -1;
            foreach (var node in _nodes)
                if (node.rect.Contains(pt + new Vector2(0, Scroll.Offset))) return node.index;
            return -1;
        }
    }
}

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public enum JigoState { Normal, Tedirgin, Korku, Mutlu, Uzgun }

    public static class JigoMood
    {
        public const int WorriedSeconds = 10, FearSeconds = 3, FearFuse = 1;
        public const float HappySeconds = .6f, FadeSeconds = .15f, FramesPerSecond = 8f;

        public static JigoState Read(float timeLeft, int? minBombFuse, bool recentHappy, bool lost, bool won)
        {
            if (lost) return JigoState.Uzgun;
            if (timeLeft <= FearSeconds || minBombFuse == FearFuse) return JigoState.Korku;
            if (timeLeft <= WorriedSeconds) return JigoState.Tedirgin;
            if (recentHappy) return JigoState.Mutlu;
            return JigoState.Normal;
        }
    }
}
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public static class BoardSizing
    {
        public static float SideGap(float screenWidth, int columns) =>
            Mathf.Min(34f, Mathf.Max(24f, (screenWidth - 44f * columns) / 2f));

        public static float CellSize(float screenWidth, float innerHeight, int columns, int rows) =>
            Mathf.Floor(Mathf.Min((screenWidth - 2 * SideGap(screenWidth, columns)) / columns,
                (innerHeight - BoardLayout.VerticalReserve) / rows, BoardLayout.MaxCell));
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public enum MenuTab { Home, Collection, Jiggles, Trophies }

    // Coordinates and hit regions use points, y down, like Screens.Menu.
    public static class MenuUi
    {
        public static SpriteRenderer Sprite21(Transform root, string key, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order, bool sliced = false)
        {
            var r = Art.Place(root, "t21-" + key, size, order, sliced);
            if (r != null) r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return r;
        }

        public static SpriteRenderer Sprite(Transform root, string key, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order, bool sliced = false)
        {
            var r = Art.Place(root, "t20-" + key, size, order, sliced);
            if (r != null) r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return r;
        }

        public static TextView Label(Transform root, string text, Vector2 c, float size, int order, bool dark = false, float width = 0, bool titleFont = false)
        {
            var t = TextView.Create(root, "label", text, size, dark ? Palette.Navy : Palette.Cream, true, order: order);
            if (titleFont) t.Font(TextView.TitleFont);
            if (width > 0) t.FitWidth(width, 8);
            t.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return t;
        }

        /* PM_MESAJ_27 A3: banners, counters and button labels (TextView.Banner). */
        public static TextView Banner(Transform root, string text, Vector2 c, float size, int order, float width = 0, bool green = false)
        {
            var t = TextView.Create(root, "banner", text, size, Palette.Cream, false, order: order).Banner(green ? TextView.OnGreen : TextView.OnTeal);
            if (width > 0) t.FitWidth(width, 8);
            t.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
            return t;
        }

        public static void Scene(Transform root, string key, Vector2 screen, int order)
        {
            SceneRegion(root, key, screen, 0, screen.y, order, true);
        }

        // Opaque slices of the same stationary scene cover scrolling art and text at the viewport edges.
        // They sit below the fixed title/nav, so both sprite and TMP renderers clip identically.
        public static void Viewport(Transform root, string key, Vector2 screen, float top, float bottom, int order)
        {
            SceneSlice(root, key, screen, 0, top, order);
            SceneSlice(root, key, screen, bottom, screen.y, order);
        }

        static void SceneSlice(Transform root, string key, Vector2 screen, float from, float to, int order)
        {
            SceneRegion(root, key, screen, from, to, order, false);
        }

        static void SceneRegion(Transform root, string key, Vector2 screen, float from, float to, int order, bool fade)
        {
            if (to <= from) return;
            var fallback = ShapeView.Create(root, "scene-colour", new Vector2(screen.x, to - from), 0, order).Fill(MenuArt.Background(key));
            fallback.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen.x / 2, (from + to) / 2);
            Action<Sprite> show = source =>
            {
                if (root == null || fallback == null) return;
                var rect = source.rect;
                var crop = new Rect(rect.x, rect.y + rect.height * (1 - to / screen.y), rect.width, rect.height * (to - from) / screen.y);
                var sprite = fade ? source : UnityEngine.Sprite.Create(source.texture, crop, new Vector2(.5f, .5f), 1);
                var r = new GameObject(fade ? "scene-" + key : "viewport-cover").AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
                r.transform.SetParent(fallback.transform, false); r.sprite = sprite; r.sortingOrder = order + (fade ? 1 : 0);
                r.transform.localScale = new Vector3(screen.x / crop.width, (to - from) / crop.height, 1);
                if (!fade)
                {
                    r.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuPainting>().Owned = sprite;
                    fallback.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().enabled = false;
                    return;
                }
                r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 0);
                Tweens.Run(r, .2f, t => { if (r != null) r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, t); });
            };
            var ready = MenuArt.Instance.Get("t20-scene-" + key + ".jpg", show);
            if (ready != null) show(ready);
        }

        public static void Title(Transform root, Vector2 screen, float top, string title, int order, bool heading = false)
        {
            Sprite(root, "title-plaque", new Vector2(screen.x / 2, top + 34), new Vector2(240, 60), order, true);
            if (heading)   // PM_MESAJ_42: Baloo heading style
            {
                var t = TextView.Create(root, "banner", title, 28, Palette.Cream, false, order: order + 1).Heading(TextView.RimTeal).FitWidth(220, 19);
                t.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen.x / 2, top + 32);
                return;
            }
            Banner(root, title, new Vector2(screen.x / 2, top + 33), 27, order + 1, 220);
        }

        public static Rect Button(Transform root, string key, string text, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order)
        {
            Sprite(root, key, c, size, order, key == "button-secondary" || key == "button-small");
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) Banner(root, text, c, 18, order + 1, size.x - 18);
            return new Rect(c - size / 2, size);
        }

        public static void Painting(Transform root, LevelImages images, int index, Vector2 c, Vector2 size, int order)
        {
            Action<Texture2D> show = tex =>
            {
                if (root == null || tex == null) return;
                float h = Mathf.Min(tex.height, tex.width * 4f / 3f), w = h * .75f;
                var sp = UnityEngine.Sprite.Create(tex, new Rect((tex.width - w) / 2, (tex.height - h) / 2, w, h), new Vector2(.5f, .5f), h / size.y);
                var r = new GameObject("painting").AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
                r.transform.SetParent(root, false);
                r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(c);
                r.sprite = sp;
                r.sortingOrder = order;
                r.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuPainting>().Owned = sp;
                r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 0);
                Tweens.Run(r, .25f, t => { if (r != null) r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, t); });
            };
            var pic = images.Get($"painting-{index + 1:00}", show);
            if (pic != null) show(pic);
        }
    }

    public sealed class MenuTabs
    {
        public readonly float Top;
        readonly float _width, _bottom;
        readonly SpriteRenderer[] _slots = new SpriteRenderer[4], _icons = new SpriteRenderer[4], _dividers = new SpriteRenderer[3];
        readonly TextView[] _labels = new TextView[4];
        readonly float[] _edges = new float[5];
        readonly string[] _keys = { "home", "collection", "jiggles", "trophy" };
        readonly float[] _weights = new float[4];
        readonly SpriteRenderer _badge;
        readonly TextView _badgeText;
        int _selected;

        public MenuTabs(Transform root, Vector2 screen, Vector4 safe, MenuTab selected, int order, int TrophyBadge = 0)
        {
            _width = screen.x; _bottom = screen.y; _selected = (int)selected;
            Top = screen.y - Mathf.Max(34, safe.w) - 52;
            // The importer pads 1125x258 to 1128x260; push that transparent right/bottom padding offscreen.
            MenuUi.Sprite21(root, "tabbar-bg", new Vector2((screen.x + 1) / 2, (Top + screen.y + 2f / 3) / 2), new Vector2(screen.x + 1, screen.y - Top + 2f / 3), order, true);
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                _weights[i] = i == _selected ? 1 : 0;
                _slots[i] = MenuUi.Sprite21(root, "tab-slot", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one, order + 1, true);
                _icons[i] = MenuUi.Sprite21(root, "tab-icon-" + _keys[i] + "-normal", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one, order + 3);
                _labels[i] = MenuUi.Banner(root, Texts.Tab(i), Vector2.zero, 15, order + 5, screen.x * .4f - 12);
                if (i > 0) _dividers[i - 1] = MenuUi.Sprite21(root, "tab-divider", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one, order + 2, true);
            }
            if (TrophyBadge > 0)
            {
                _badge = MenuUi.Sprite21(root, "tab-badge", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * 19, order + 6);
                _badgeText = MenuUi.Label(root, TrophyBadge.ToString(), Vector2.zero, 12, order + 7, width: 16);
            }
            Arrange();
        }
        void Arrange()
        {
            float x = 0, slotTop = Top + 10f / 3, slotHeight = _bottom - slotTop;
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
            {
                float active = _weights[i], width = _width * (1 + active) / 5, cx = x + width / 2;
                _edges[i] = x; x += width;
                if (_slots[i] != null)
                {
                    _slots[i].sprite = Art.Get("t21-tab-slot" + (i == _selected ? "-selected" : ""));
                    _slots[i].size = new Vector2(width, slotHeight);
                    _slots[i].transform.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, slotTop + slotHeight / 2);
                }
                // PM_MESAJ_29: the selected icon rises at most a quarter of its height over the bar
                float height = Mathf.Lerp(49, 56, active), y = Top + Mathf.Lerp(3, -14, active);
                var icon = _icons[i];
                if (icon != null)
                {
                    icon.sprite = Art.Get("t21-tab-icon-" + _keys[i] + (i == _selected ? "-selected-large" : "-normal"));
                    float scale = height / icon.sprite.bounds.size.y;
                    icon.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
                    icon.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, y + height / 2);
                }
                _labels[i].Alpha = active;
                _labels[i].transform.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, Top + 50);
                if (i > 0 && _dividers[i - 1] != null)
                {
                    _dividers[i - 1].size = new Vector2(4f / 3, slotHeight);
                    _dividers[i - 1].transform.localPosition = Draw.W(_edges[i], slotTop + slotHeight / 2);
                }
                if (i == 3)
                {
                    float iw = icon != null ? icon.sprite.bounds.size.x * icon.transform.localScale.x : height;
                    var badgeC = new Vector2(cx + iw / 2 - 3, y + 5);
                    if (_badge != null) _badge.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(badgeC);
                    if (_badgeText != null) _badgeText.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(badgeC);
                }
            }
            _edges[4] = _width;
        }
        public int Hit(Vector2 pt)
        {
            if (pt.y < Top - 14 || pt.y > _bottom || pt.x < 0 || pt.x >= _width) return -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) if (pt.x < _edges[i + 1]) return i;
            return -1;
        }
        public void Press(int index, Action done)
        {
            if (index < 0 || index >= 4) return;
            var from = (float[])_weights.Clone();
            _selected = index;
            Tweens.Run(this, .15f, t =>
            {
                if (_labels[0] == null) return;
                for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) _weights[i] = Mathf.Lerp(from[i], i == index ? 1 : 0, t);
                Arrange();
            }, done);
        }
    }

    // The transform moves as a unit. Fully offscreen cells are inactive; scene slices clip the visible edges.
    // PM_MESAJ_27 A2: phone feel. A flick keeps going from the finger's speed over its last 80 ms and slows down
    // exponentially; past either end the list stretches with resistance and springs back (Unity ScrollRect's
    // rubber band and elasticity 0.1).
    public sealed class MenuScroll
    {
        public const float Decay = 2f, Elasticity = .1f, Window = .08f, StopSpeed = 20f;
        public readonly Transform Root;
        public readonly float Top, Bottom;
        public float Offset { get; private set; }
        public float Velocity { get; private set; }
        public bool Held { get; private set; }
        public bool Moving => !Held && (Mathf.Abs(Velocity) > StopSpeed || Offset < 0 || Offset > _max);
        readonly float _max;
        float _grab;
        readonly List<(float t, float y)> _samples = new List<(float, float)>();
        sealed class CellEntry
        {
            public Transform Root;
            public float Y, Half;
            public Action<Transform> Visible;
        }
        readonly List<CellEntry> _cells = new List<CellEntry>();
        public MenuScroll(Transform parent, float top, float bottom, float height)
        {
            Root = new GameObject("scroll-content").transform; Root.SetParent(parent, false);
            Top = top; Bottom = bottom; _max = Mathf.Max(0, height - (bottom - top));
        }
        public Transform Cell(float x, float y, float half, Action<Transform> visible = null)
        {
            var t = new GameObject("cell").transform; t.SetParent(Root, false); t.localPosition = Draw.W(x, y);
            _cells.Add(new CellEntry { Root = t, Y = y, Half = half, Visible = visible }); return t;
        }
        public bool Contains(Vector2 pt) => pt.y >= Top && pt.y <= Bottom;
        public float Resting => Mathf.Clamp(Offset, 0, _max);

        /* Finger down: whatever motion there was stops. */
        public void Grab(float y, float time)
        {
            Held = true; Velocity = 0; _grab = Offset + y;
            _samples.Clear(); _samples.Add((time, y));
        }
        /* Finger at y: the content follows it, with resistance beyond the ends. */
        public void Drag(float y, float time)
        {
            float raw = _grab - y;
            Apply(raw < 0 ? -Rubber(-raw) : raw > _max ? _max + Rubber(raw - _max) : raw);
            _samples.Add((time, y));
            while (_samples.Count > 2 && time - _samples[1].t >= Window) _samples.RemoveAt(0);
        }
        public void Release(float time)
        {
            Held = false; Velocity = 0;
            if (_samples.Count < 2 || time - _samples[_samples.Count - 1].t > Window) return;   // the finger rested before lifting
            var b = _samples[_samples.Count - 1]; var a = _samples[0];
            foreach (var x in _samples) if (b.t - x.t <= Window && x.t < b.t) { a = x; break; }   // the oldest one inside the last 80 ms
            if (b.t - a.t > 1e-3f) Velocity = -(b.y - a.y) / (b.t - a.t);
            if (Host.Debug && Mathf.Abs(Velocity) > StopSpeed) { _flickFrom = Offset; _flickAt = time; _flickV = Velocity; }
        }
        float _flickFrom, _flickAt = -1, _flickV;
        /* Mouse wheel: a nudge that keeps the same easing as a flick. */
        public void Wheel(float delta) { if (!Held) Velocity += delta * Decay; }

        public void Tick(float dt)
        {
            if (Held || dt <= 0) return;
            float bound = Offset < 0 ? 0 : Offset > _max ? _max : Offset;
            if (bound != Offset)
            {
                float v = Velocity;
                float o = Mathf.SmoothDamp(Offset, bound, ref v, Elasticity, Mathf.Infinity, dt);
                Velocity = v;
                if (Mathf.Abs(o - bound) < .5f) { o = bound; Velocity = 0; }
                Apply(o);
            }
            else if (Mathf.Abs(Velocity) > StopSpeed)
            {
                Velocity *= Mathf.Exp(-Decay * dt);
                Apply(Offset + Velocity * dt);
            }
            else Velocity = 0;
            if (_flickAt >= 0 && !Moving)
            {
                // ?debug=1: the flick test in Tools/verify_build.js reads this
                Debug.Log(string.Format(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "[scroll] v {0:0} pt/s, {1:0} pt, {2:0.00} s",
                                        _flickV, Offset - _flickFrom, Time.unscaledTime - _flickAt));
                _flickAt = -1;
            }
        }

        float Rubber(float over)
        {
            float view = Bottom - Top;
            return (1 - 1 / (over * .55f / view + 1)) * view;
        }

        public void Move(float delta) => Set(Offset - delta);
        public void Set(float offset) { Velocity = 0; Apply(Mathf.Clamp(offset, 0, _max)); }
        void Apply(float offset)
        {
            Offset = offset; Root.localPosition = Draw.W(0, -Offset);
            foreach (var c in _cells)
            {
                bool active = c.Y - Offset + c.Half >= Top && c.Y - Offset - c.Half <= Bottom;
                c.Root.gameObject.SetActive(active);
                if (active && c.Visible != null)
                {
                    var show = c.Visible; c.Visible = null; show(c.Root);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core
{
    /* One attempt at one level: the web game's session rules (index.html,
       flyOut / tickNails / releaseValve / tickBombs / finish / boosters /
       startClock) as plain C#.

       State changes only through the commands below. Time arrives through
       Tick(dt) and randomness through IRandom, so a session replays exactly
       from (level, seed, commands, ticks).

       A successful removal runs, in the web game's order:
         remove -> nails tick -> safety valve -> fuses tick -> win check

       Deliberate differences from the web game (decided in PRODUCT.md):
         - DurationSeconds counts the clock seconds this session actually ran.
           The web game used timer - timeLeft, which the clock booster skewed.
         - Every attempt ends in exactly one record, quitting included. The
           web game never wrote its quit rows.

       Jams. The web game counts a jam when the player presses a nailed piece,
       or drags a piece beyond the point where it stops (once per drag and
       direction). Both are input decisions, so Presentation reports them
       through RecordJam; the session only counts them. */
    public sealed partial class GameSession
    {
        public const int ClockBonusSeconds = 20;
        const int WandAttempts = 200;

        public LevelData Level { get; }
        public Board Board { get; }
        public int Attempt { get; }
        public string Client { get; }

        public int Continues { get; private set; }
        int _continueSeconds;
        public int TimeLeft { get; private set; }
        public int ElapsedSeconds { get; private set; }

        /* Zeyd 2026-10-18: the countdown starts with the player's first action (an exit, a jam or a
           booster), so looking at the board before the first move is free. False keeps the web game's
           rule (the clock runs from the start): the web golden scenarios and the fun model use it. */
        public bool ClockOnFirstMove { get; set; } = true;
        public bool ClockStarted { get; private set; }
        public int Moves { get; private set; }
        public int Jams { get; private set; }
        public int ValveCount { get; private set; }
        public Outcome Outcome { get; private set; }
        public bool IsOver => Outcome != Outcome.None;
        public AttemptRecord Record { get; private set; }

        public IReadOnlyList<GameEvent> Events => _events;
        public event Action<GameEvent> EventRaised;

        readonly IRandom _random;
        readonly Dictionary<int, int> _nails;
        readonly SortedDictionary<int, int> _bombs;   // ascending piece order, like JS integer keys
        readonly int[] _stock = new int[4];
        readonly int[] _used = new int[4];
        readonly List<GameEvent> _events = new List<GameEvent>();
        double _clockCarry;

        public GameSession(LevelData level, int attempt, string client, IRandom random)
        {
            Level = level ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(level));
            _random = random ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(random));
            Attempt = attempt;
            Client = client;
            Board = new Board(level);
            TimeLeft = level.TimerSeconds;
            _nails = new Dictionary<int, int>(level.Nails);
            _bombs = new SortedDictionary<int, int>();
            foreach (var b in level.Bombs) _bombs[b.Key] = b.Value;

            // The web game falls back to these when a level has no booster table.
            bool table = level.Boosters != null && level.Boosters.Count > 0;
            int[] fallback = { 5, 3, 7, 10 };
            foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All)
                _stock[(int)b] = table ? (level.Boosters.TryGetValue(b.Id(), out int n) ? n : 0) : fallback[(int)b];
        }

        GameSession(GameSession o)
        {
            Level = o.Level; Attempt = o.Attempt; Client = o.Client; _random = o._random;
            Board = o.Board.Clone();
            PreventTimeout = o.PreventTimeout;
            TimeLeft = o.TimeLeft; ElapsedSeconds = o.ElapsedSeconds; Moves = o.Moves; Jams = o.Jams;
            ClockOnFirstMove = o.ClockOnFirstMove; ClockStarted = o.ClockStarted; FreeUse = o.FreeUse;
            ValveCount = o.ValveCount; Outcome = o.Outcome; Record = o.Record;
            Continues = o.Continues; _continueSeconds = o._continueSeconds;
            _nails = new Dictionary<int, int>(o._nails);
            _bombs = new SortedDictionary<int, int>(o._bombs);
            Array.Copy(o._stock, _stock, 4);
            Array.Copy(o._used, _used, 4);
            _events.AddRange(o._events);
            _clockCarry = o._clockCarry;
            ClockFreezesTime = o.ClockFreezesTime; FreezeLeft = o.FreezeLeft;
            TestBoosterUnlock = o.TestBoosterUnlock;
        }

        /* A branch for search (solvers, Monte Carlo). Shares the random source,
           so only branch where no further draws matter, or pass a fresh one. */
        internal GameSession Clone() => new GameSession(this);

        // ---- queries ----------------------------------------------------------------

        public int NailAt(int piece) => _nails.TryGetValue(piece, out int n) ? n : 0;
        public int FuseAt(int piece) => _bombs.TryGetValue(piece, out int n) ? n : 0;
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<int, int> Nails => _nails;
        public IReadOnlyDictionary<int, int> Bombs => _bombs;
        public int Stock(Booster b) => _stock[(int)b];

        /* PM_MESAJ_30: the player's inventory replaces the level's table (set before the first command). */
        public void SetStock(Booster b, int n) => _stock[(int)b] = Math.Max(0, n);

        /* PM_MESAJ_30: the guided first use of a booster. Its next use is free: no stock spent, not counted in the
           record (b_* stays a count of the player's own uses), the clock does not start; the Used event still goes. */
        public Booster? FreeUse { get; set; }
        // Guided jams still raise the visual event without counting as player mistakes.
        public bool TutorialMove { get; set; }
        public bool PreventTimeout { get; set; }
        public Booster? TestBoosterUnlock { get; set; }
        public int Used(Booster b) => _used[(int)b];

        /* Locked while the level number is below the booster's unlock level. */
        public bool IsUnlocked(Booster b) =>
            TestBoosterUnlock == b || Level.Unlock == null || !Level.Unlock.TryGetValue(b.Id(), out int at) || Level.Number >= at;

        public TravelLimit Probe(int piece, Dir dir) => TravelRule.Limit(Board, piece, dir);

        /* 3 stars with >= 45% of the timer left, 2 with >= 20%, else 1 (web starsFor). */
        public int Stars => Outcome != Outcome.Win ? 0 : StarsFor(Math.Max(0, TimeLeft - _continueSeconds), Level.TimerSeconds);

        public static int StarsFor(int timeLeft, int timer)
        {
            if (timer <= 0) return 3;
            double frac = (double)timeLeft / timer;
            return frac >= .45 ? 3 : frac >= .2 ? 2 : 1;
        }

        // ---- commands ---------------------------------------------------------------

        /* A drag-out that the player released past the exit point. */
        public CommandResult TryExit(int piece, Dir dir)
        {
            if (IsOver) return CommandResult.GameOver;
            if (!IsPiece(piece)) return CommandResult.NoSuchPiece;
            if (NailAt(piece) > 0) return CommandResult.Pinned;
            if (!TravelRule.Limit(Board, piece, dir).Exit) return CommandResult.Blocked;
            Remove(piece, dir, byHammer: false);
            return CommandResult.Ok;
        }

        public CommandResult RecordJam(int piece)
        {
            if (IsOver) return CommandResult.GameOver;
            if (!IsPiece(piece)) return CommandResult.NoSuchPiece;
            if (!TutorialMove) { Jams++; ClockStarted = true; }
            Raise(GameEvent.Jam(piece));
            return CommandResult.Ok;
        }

        /* Removes any present piece, nailed or carrying a bomb, and counts as a move. */
        public CommandResult UseHammer(int piece)
        {
            var check = CanUse(Booster.Hammer);
            if (check != CommandResult.Ok) return check;
            if (!IsPiece(piece)) return CommandResult.NoSuchPiece;
            bool free = FreeUse == Booster.Hammer, started = ClockStarted;
            Spend(Booster.Hammer);
            Remove(piece, Dir.U, byHammer: true);
            if (free) ClockStarted = started;           // the guided use never starts the clock
            return CommandResult.Ok;
        }

        /* Flattens one tabbed joint between two different present pieces.
           Vertical: the joint left of cell (x, y). Horizontal: the joint above it. */
        public CommandResult UseScissors(int x, int y, bool vertical)

using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    // Board-owned, prewarmed pools. No tweens, coroutines or loads in the animation loop.
    public sealed class P21JuiceView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static bool Enabled => Art.T54 && TelemetryMeta.Current?.Cohort == "p21";
        const float Quick = 1.2f;
        struct Puff
        {
            public SpriteRenderer View;
            public Sprite[] Frames;
            public float At, Life, Size;
            public Color Tint;
        }
        struct Badge
        {
            public SpriteRenderer View;
            public TextView Text;
            public float At;
        }
        readonly Puff[] _puffs = new Puff[16];
        readonly Badge[] _badges = new Badge[2];
        readonly string[] _combos = new string[100];
        Sprite[] _pop, _confetti, _metal, _praise;
        BoardView _board;
        float _lastExit = -10;
        int _next, _combo, _praisedMove;

        public static P21JuiceView Create(BoardView board)
        {
            var v = Draw.Child<P21JuiceView>(board.transform, "P21 juice");
            v._board = board;
            v._praisedMove = board.Session.Moves;
            v._pop = Frames("pop", 9); v._confetti = Frames("confetti", 6); v._metal = Frames("metal", 9);
            v._praise = new[] { Load("great"), Load("super"), Load("perfect") };
            for (int i = 0; i < v._puffs.Length; i++)
                v._puffs[i] = new Puff { View = Layer(v.transform, "puff", 2200), At = -1 };
            for (int i = 0; i < v._badges.Length; i++)
            {
                var r = Layer(v.transform, "badge", 2250);
                var text = TextView.Create(r.transform, "label", "", i == 0 ? 27 : 23, Vector4.one, order: 2251)
                    .Heading(TextView.RimTeal);
                v._badges[i] = new Badge { View = r, Text = text, At = -1 };
                text.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            }
            for (int i = 2; i < v._combos.Length; i++) v._combos[i] = "x" + i;
            v._badges[0].View.sprite = Load("combo");
            return v;
        }

        static Sprite Load(string key) => Resources.Load<Sprite>("Art/yeni/juice21/" + key);
        static Sprite[] Frames(string key, int count)
        {
            var frames = new Sprite[count];
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) frames[i] = Load(key + "/" + (i + 1).ToString("00"));
            return frames;
        }
        static SpriteRenderer Layer(Transform parent, string name, int order)
        {
            var r = Draw.Child<SpriteRenderer>(parent, name);
            r.sortingOrder = order; r.enabled = false; return r;
        }

        public void OnEvent(GameEvent e, PieceView piece)
        {
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.JointCut)
            {
                var at = e.Vertical ? new Vector2(e.X, e.Y + .5f) : new Vector2(e.X + .5f, e.Y);
                Burst(_metal, at * _board.Cell, Color.white, .45f, 1.8f);
                return;
            }
            if (piece == null) return;
            var point = Vector2.zero;
            foreach (var c in piece.Cells) point += new Vector2(c.X + .5f, c.Y + .5f) * _board.Cell;
            point /= piece.Cells.Length;
            point += piece.Offset;
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.NailFreed || (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved && e.ByHammer && piece.Cells.Length > 1))
                Burst(_metal, point, Color.white, .45f, 1.8f);
            if (e.Kind != EventKind.PieceRemoved) return;
            var tint = PieceView.CellColor(_board.Session.Level, piece.Cells[0]);
            Burst(_pop, point, tint, .36f, 1.9f);
            Burst(_confetti, point, tint, .65f, 2.5f);
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            _combo = now - _lastExit <= Quick ? _combo + 1 : 1; _lastExit = now;
            if (_combo < 2) return;
            // Badges sit inside the board, away from both the timer and booster bar.
            var badgePoint = new Vector2(_board.Size.x * .5f, _board.Cell * .45f);
            ShowBadge(0, _badges[0].View.sprite, _combos[Mathf.Min(_combo, 99)], badgePoint, _board.Cell * 1.3f);
            int moves = _board.Session.Moves;
            if (moves - _praisedMove < 3) return;
            _praisedMove = moves;
            int tier = Mathf.Min(2, _combo - 2);
            ShowBadge(1, _praise[tier], Texts.JuicePraise(tier), badgePoint + new Vector2(0, _board.Cell * .8f), _board.Cell * 2.6f);
        }

        void Burst(Sprite[] frames, Vector2 point, Color tint, float life, float size)
        {
            ref var p = ref _puffs[_next++ % _puffs.Length];
            p.Frames = frames; p.At = Time.unscaledTime; p.Life = life; p.Size = _board.Cell * size; p.Tint = tint;
            p.View.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(point);
        }
        void ShowBadge(int index, Sprite sprite, string label, Vector2 point, float width)
        {
            ref var b = ref _badges[index];
            b.At = Time.unscaledTime; b.View.sprite = sprite;
            b.View.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(point);
            // Keep text in points: scale the renderer's size rather than its transform.
            b.View.drawMode = SpriteDrawMode.Sliced;
            b.View.size = sprite != null ? new Vector2(width, width * sprite.bounds.size.y / sprite.bounds.size.x) : Vector2.one;
            b.Text.Text = label; b.Text.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }
        void Update()
        {
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            for (int i = 0; i < _puffs.Length; i++)
            {
                ref var p = ref _puffs[i];
                if (p.At < 0) continue;
                float t = (now - p.At) / p.Life;
                if (t >= 1) { p.View.enabled = false; p.At = -1; continue; }
                var sprite = p.Frames[Mathf.Min((int)(t * p.Frames.Length), p.Frames.Length - 1)];
                p.View.sprite = sprite; p.View.enabled = sprite != null;
                if (sprite == null) continue;
                float scale = p.Size / sprite.bounds.size.x * (1 + .12f * Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI));
                p.View.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
                var tint = p.Tint; tint.a *= 1 - t * t; p.View.color = tint;
            }

        void Spend(Booster b)
        {
            if (FreeUse == b) { FreeUse = null; Raise(GameEvent.Used(b)); return; }
            ClockStarted = true;
            _stock[(int)b]--;
            _used[(int)b]++;
            Raise(GameEvent.Used(b));
        }

        bool IsPiece(int piece) => piece >= 0 && piece < Level.Pieces.Count && Board.IsPresent(piece);

        /* The row this attempt would produce if it were quit right now, without
           ending it: saved when the page is hidden, so an attempt cut short by
           closing the tab still leaves its quit row (and is dropped again if
           the player comes back). */
        public AttemptRecord PreviewQuit() => IsOver ? Record : BuildRecord(Outcome.Quit);

        void Finish(Outcome outcome)
        {
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* The web game's .nail and .bomb badges (index.html CSS), drawn on the
       piece's first cell and moving with it. */
    public sealed class NailBadge
    {
        public readonly Transform Root;
        readonly TextView _n;
        readonly SpriteRenderer _lock;
        readonly Vector3 _lockScale;
        readonly bool _t28;
        readonly float _cell;
        readonly float _one, _two;                  // counter size (pt) for one digit / two digits (art counters)

        public NailBadge(Transform parent, Vector2 center, float cell, int order)
        {
            Root = new GameObject("Nail").transform;
            Root.SetParent(parent, false);
            Root.localPosition = Draw.W(center);
            _t28 = Art.T28 && Art.On;
            _cell = cell;
            if (_t28)
            {
                // Closed lock: 311 visible pixels on a 384px canvas.
                float size = 1.2f * (Art.T54 ? cell * .55f * 384 / 311 : cell * (Art.T14 ? .86f : .8f));
                _lock = Art.Place(Root, Art.T54 ? "obstacles_v3/padlock-b/closed" : "board-nail", Vector2.one * size, order);
                if (_lock != null) _lockScale = _lock.transform.localScale;
                _n = TextView.Create(Root, "count", "", size * .315f, Palette.Navy, true, order: order + 2).Font(Art.Lilita);
                _n.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(0, size * .144f);
                _one = size * .315f; _two = size * .285f;
                return;
            }
            float d = Mathf.Max(22f, cell * 0.46f);
            // nail head seen from above (head = 80 % of the canvas), navy digits ~55 % of the head with a 2 px cream rim
            if (ArtBadge(Root, "board-nail", cell, Art.T14 ? .86f : .8f, 0, order, Palette.Navy, Art.T14 ? (Vector4?)null : Palette.Cream, out _n))
            { _one = cell * .46f; _two = cell * .36f; return; }
            if (ArtBadge(Root, "icon-nail", cell, new Vector2(-.2f, -.2f), order, out _n)) return;   // count on the nail head (upper left)
            // radial-gradient(60% 50% at 32% 26%, #f2f5f8, #8d9aa6 60%, #5b6874)
            ShapeView.Create(Root, "disc", new Vector2(d, d), 8, order)
                .Radius(d / 2)
                .Radial(Draw.Hex("#f2f5f8"), Draw.Hex("#8d9aa6"), Draw.Hex("#5b6874"), new Vector4(.32f, .26f, .6f, .5f))
                .Shadow(Draw.Rgba(0, 0, 0, .5f), 0, 2, 4)
                .Inner(Draw.Rgba(0, 0, 0, .35f), -2, 3);
            _n = TextView.Create(Root, "count", "", 13, Draw.Hex("#1d2b31"), true, order: order + 1);
        }

        public int Count { set { _n.Text = value.ToString(); Fit(_n, value, _one, _two); } }

        /* Two digits get a smaller size so they never crowd the disc. */
        internal static void Fit(TextView t, int value, float one, float two)
        {
            if (one > 0) t.SizePt = value >= 10 ? two : one;
        }

        /* Preview art (#10): the icon a bit larger than the drawn disc, the counter on a dark chip at its
           lower right so the number stays readable on a phone-size cell. */
        /* Codex board art (PM_MESAJ_11/12): the sprite sized so its body is `body` of the cell, shifted up by `lift`
           (share of the sprite) so the body, not the canvas, sits on the cell centre; the counter in Fredoka on top. */
using UnityEditor;

namespace ReverseSolver.EditorTools
{
    /* Approved menu art (PM_MESAJ_7): the corridor background is a sprite at 1 pixel per point (the
       game's world unit), at most 2048 px on the long side, compressed, no mipmaps. */
    public sealed class MenuArtImport : AssetPostprocessor
    {
        void OnPreprocessTexture()
        {
            // Codex art for the v3 preview (Art.cs): @3x sprites, 3 px per point; frame and button 9-sliced
            // Codex piece kit atlases (KitAtlas.cs): plain textures sampled by Piece.shader, 2592 px
            if (assetPath.Contains("Presentation/Resources/Art/kit-"))
            {
                var k = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
                k.textureType = TextureImporterType.Default;
                k.alphaIsTransparency = true;
                k.mipmapEnabled = false;
                k.maxTextureSize = 2048;   // PM_MESAJ_14: no texture above 2048 px
                k.wrapMode = UnityEngine.TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
                k.textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Compressed;
                k.crunchedCompression = true;
                k.compressionQuality = 50;   // PM_MESAJ_29 4.2: 2048 px is already the drawn size (cell ~137 px); lighter crunch
                return;
            }
            if (assetPath.Contains("Presentation/Resources/Art/"))
            {
                var a = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
                a.textureType = TextureImporterType.Sprite;
                a.spriteImportMode = SpriteImportMode.Single;
                if (assetPath.Contains("/yeni/"))
                {
                    a.alphaIsTransparency = true;
                    a.wrapMode = UnityEngine.TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
                    a.spritePivot = new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f, .5f);
                    var old = AssetImporter.GetAtPath("Assets/_Game/Presentation/Resources/Art/" + System.IO.Path.GetFileName(assetPath)) as TextureImporter;
                    if (old != null) a.spriteBorder = old.spriteBorder;
                }
                a.spritePixelsPerUnit = 3;
                a.mipmapEnabled = false;
                a.maxTextureSize = assetPath.Contains("/yeni/hud/") || assetPath.Contains("/yeni/devam_v2/") ? 2048 : 1024;
                if (assetPath.Contains("/t20-") || assetPath.Contains("/t21-") || assetPath.Contains("/t23-"))
                {
                    a.wrapMode = UnityEngine.TextureWrapMode.Clamp;
                    a.alphaIsTransparency = true;
                    a.maxTextureSize = 2048;
                    a.spritePixelsPerUnit = assetPath.Contains("/t20-scene-") ? 1 : 3;
                    a.filterMode = UnityEngine.FilterMode.Bilinear;
                    a.spritePivot = assetPath.Contains("-standing.png") || assetPath.EndsWith("/t20-levels-path-repeat.png")
                        ? new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f, 0) : new UnityEngine.Vector2(.5f, .5f);
                    if (assetPath.EndsWith("/t20-levels-path-repeat.png"))
                        a.wrapModeV = UnityEngine.TextureWrapMode.Repeat;
                }
                a.textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Compressed;
                a.crunchedCompression = true;
                a.compressionQuality = 60;
                if (assetPath.Contains("/t23-"))
                {
                    // PM_MESAJ_39: the PNGs are padded to multiples of 4, so at their own size they stay block-compressible
                    const int limit = 2048;   // a smaller cap rescales to odd sizes and the texture ships uncompressed
                    a.maxTextureSize = limit;
                    a.SetPlatformTextureSettings(new TextureImporterPlatformSettings
                    {
                        name = "WebGL", overridden = true, maxTextureSize = limit,
                        format = TextureImporterFormat.DXT5Crunched,
                        textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Compressed,
                        crunchedCompression = true, compressionQuality = assetPath.EndsWith("-panel.png") ? 30 : 50   // panels: flat colour, 30 holds up
                    });
                }
                var st = new TextureImporterSettings();
                a.ReadTextureSettings(st);
                st.spriteMeshType = UnityEngine.SpriteMeshType.FullRect;    // needed for sliced drawing
                if (assetPath.Contains("/t20-") || assetPath.Contains("/t21-") || assetPath.Contains("/t23-")) st.spriteAlignment = (int)UnityEngine.SpriteAlignment.Custom;
                a.SetTextureSettings(st);
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("skin-body.png")) { a.wrapMode = UnityEngine.TextureWrapMode.Repeat; a.crunchedCompression = false; }
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("/popup-card.png")) a.maxTextureSize = 2048;   // 972x1200 stays block-aligned (1024 would rescale it to 830 px)
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("game-background.png")) a.maxTextureSize = 1024;   // 576x1024: compressible (1125 px is not a multiple of 4)
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("/frame.png")) a.spriteBorder = new UnityEngine.Vector4(54, 54, 54, 54);
                // Codex Task 5 borders (left, bottom, right, top px), written by Tools/art_choice.py from Codex's manifest
                var m = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Match(System.IO.File.ReadAllText("Assets/_Game/Editor/art_borders.json"),
                    "\"" + System.IO.Path.GetFileName(assetPath) + "\":" + @"\s*\[\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+)");
                if (m.Success)
                    a.spriteBorder = new UnityEngine.Vector4(int.Parse(m.Groups[1].Value), int.Parse(m.Groups[2].Value),
                                                             int.Parse(m.Groups[3].Value), int.Parse(m.Groups[4].Value));
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("/hud-moves-pill.png") || assetPath.EndsWith("/hud-time-pill.png") || assetPath.EndsWith("/hud-time-pill-urgent.png"))
                    a.spritePixelsPerUnit = 2;   // PM_MESAJ_29: art_choice.py keeps them at 2x their drawn size
                if (assetPath.EndsWith("/button.png")) a.spriteBorder = new UnityEngine.Vector4(126, 42, 126, 42);
                return;
            }
            if (!assetPath.EndsWith("Presentation/Resources/menu_bg.png")) return;
            var t = (TextureImporter)assetImporter;
            t.textureType = TextureImporterType.Sprite;
            t.spriteImportMode = SpriteImportMode.Single;
            t.spritePixelsPerUnit = 1;
            t.mipmapEnabled = false;
            t.maxTextureSize = 2048;
            t.textureCompression = TextureImporterCompression.Compressed;
            t.crunchedCompression = true;
            t.compressionQuality = 60;
        }
# CODEX_TASK46 — Yeni saat booster'ı + Jigo geçiş kareleri (PM_62)

Mevcut dil: T44/T45 (old money mavi + krem, doygun altın/turuncu, hacimli, siyah kontur yok). Mevcut Jigo madalyon kareleri ve saat booster kareleri `Assets/Art/_yeni/` altında (önce bul ve bak). Eski saatteki **kopuk/yamalı kol kalkıyor**.

## 1. Saat: Jigo fırçayı kılıç gibi uzatır (tek parça poz kareleri)
Madalyondaki Jigo, gövdesiyle birlikte sağa eğilip fırçasını kılıç gibi uzatır; fırça madalyondan hafifçe taşar. Kol ayrı parça gibi GÖRÜNMESİN: her kare Jigo + fırça tek parça çizim.
- `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/clock/extend/01..06.png` — düğmeyi indirir, fırçayı uzatır (son kare = tam uzanmış, fırça ucu sağda).
- `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/clock/hold/01..04.png` — uzanmış hâlde hafif nefes döngüsü.
- `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/clock/retract/01..05.png` — fırçayı geri çeker, düğmeyi alır (son kare = normal madalyon pozuna eşit).
- Madalyon karelerinin tuvali ve pivotu mevcut normal Jigo kareleriyle aynı olsun (fırça taşan kısım için tuval sağa genişlerse `LISTE.md`'de pivot/ofseti piksel olarak yaz). Fırça ucunun kare içindeki konumunu (px) `LISTE.md`'ye yaz.

## 2. Dokunma ışıltısı + buz kaplaması
- `T46/clock/spark/01..06.png` — fırça ucunun panele değdiği yerde ışıltı (altın-beyaz yıldız patlaması).
- `T46/clock/ice_overlay.png` — Süre paneline oturan buz/ayaz kaplaması (yarı saydam, kenarları kırağılı). Panelin mevcut boyutuna göre çiz.
- `T46/clock/ice_break/01..07.png` — buz çatlar ve parçalanıp düşer.
- `T46/clock/freeze_bar_bg.png` + `freeze_bar_fill.png` — panelin altındaki 10 sn barı (buz mavisi dolgu, krem/mavi çerçeve, 9-slice kenar payını yaz).

## 3. Jigo normal ↔ mutlu geçiş + daha sık kareler
- `T46/jigo/normal_to_happy/01..05.png` ve `happy_to_normal/01..05.png` — ara kareler, ani atlama olmasın.
- Normal ve mutlu döngüleri şu an kesik görünüyor: varsa 12–24 fps için ara kareler `T46/jigo/normal_loop/`, `T46/jigo/happy_loop/` (mevcut kare sayısını 2 katına çıkar, aynı tuval).

## Teslim
`docs/art/task46/onizleme.png` (saat akışı şeridi + geçişler, 390×844 ölçekte) ve `docs/art/task46/LISTE.md` (dosyalar, kare sayıları, önerilen fps, pivot/ofset, fırça ucu konumu, 9-slice). 3 satır özet, dur. Kurallar: kurdele/afiş yok, Jigo mutlu/normalde kaş çatmaz, soluk efekt yok, RM'yi birebir kopyalama yok.

    public sealed class Hud
    {

        }
