using System.Collections.Generic;
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* The web game's .prompt line and .bar of four .bst buttons (index.html):
       60pt buttons, 13pt apart, count badge top-right, name underneath; armed
       (.on) amber, out of stock and locked greyed, the level's new booster
       pulsing (.fresh). It only draws BoosterControls' state; taps come back
       through Hit(). */
    public sealed class BoosterBar
    {
        public const float Height = 97f, PromptH = 17f;

        readonly Transform _root;
        readonly BoosterBar54 _t54;
        public BarAction HitAction(Vector2 pt) => _t54?.HitAction(pt) ?? BarAction.None;
        readonly TextView _prompt;
        readonly List<Slot> _slots = new List<Slot>();

        public void ShowTutorial(bool showing) => _root.gameObject.SetActive(!showing);

        sealed class Slot
        {
            public Booster B;
            public Rect Rect;
            public ShapeView Face, Shine, Badge;
            public TextView Count, Name;
            public Transform Lock, Glyph;
            public string Key;
            public bool Fresh;
        }

        public BoosterBar(Transform parent, Vector2 screen, float bottomInset, int order, bool t54 = false)
        {
            _root = new GameObject("BoosterBar").transform;
            _root.SetParent(parent, false);
            if (t54 && Art.T54) { _t54 = new BoosterBar54(_root, screen, bottomInset, order); return; }
            float top = screen.y - bottomInset - 4 - Height;
            float cy = top + 7 + 30;
            float x0 = screen.x / 2 - (4 * 60 + 3 * 13) / 2f + 30;

            _prompt = TextView.Create(_root, "prompt", "", 12.5f, Draw.Hex("#f0a742"), order: order);
            _prompt.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(screen.x / 2, top - PromptH / 2);

            foreach (var b in BoosterExt.All)
            {
                float cx = x0 + (int)b * 73;
                var s = new Slot { B = b, Rect = new Rect(cx - 30, cy - 30, 60, 60) };
                // .bst: radius 16, gradient #6ecbf5 -> #2b86cf, 3px white ring, 0 6px 13px rgba(0,0,0,.42)
                s.Face = ShapeView.Create(_root, "bst", new Vector2(60, 60), 20, order).Radius(16).Skin(Art.T14 ? $"booster-{b.Id()}-normal" : Art.Get("booster-button") != null ? "booster-button" : "booster-normal", false)
                    .Stroke(Draw.Rgba(255, 255, 255, .92f), 3).Shadow(Draw.Rgba(0, 0, 0, .42f), 0, 6, 13)
                    .Inner(Draw.Rgba(0, 0, 0, .22f), -3, 6);
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core.Tests
{
    public class P21BoosterTests
    {
        static LevelData Grid(int w = 4, int h = 4, bool chain = false, bool sealedBoard = false)
        {
            var pieces = new List<string>();
            for (int y = 0; y < h; y++) for (int x = 0; x < w; x++)
                if (!chain || y != 0 || (x != 1 && x != 2)) pieces.Add($"[[{x},{y}]]");
            if (chain) pieces.Add("[[1,0],[2,0]]");
            string v = "[null," + string.Join(",", Enumerable.Repeat("1", w - 1)) + ",null]";
            string edge = "[" + string.Join(",", Enumerable.Repeat("null", w)) + "]";
            string middle = "[" + string.Join(",", Enumerable.Repeat("0", w)) + "]";
            string walls = sealedBoard ? ",\"sealed\":{\"U\":[0,1,2,3],\"D\":[0,1,2,3],\"L\":[0,1,2,3],\"R\":[0,1,2,3]}" : "";
            string json = $"{{\"format\":1,\"levels\":[{{\"id\":\"W\",\"version\":1,\"lv\":50,\"w\":{w},\"h\":{h},\"timer\":10," +
                $"\"pieces\":[{string.Join(",", pieces)}],\"vEdge\":[{string.Join(",", Enumerable.Repeat(v, h))}]," +
                $"\"hEdge\":[{edge},{string.Join(",", Enumerable.Repeat(middle, h - 1))},{edge}]{walls}}}]}}";
            return LevelParser.Parse(json).Levels[0];
        }

        static GameSession Session(LevelData level = null, uint seed = 1) => new GameSession(level ?? Grid(), 1, "test", new Mulberry32(seed));
        static int[] Joints(Board b) => Enumerable.Range(0, b.Level.Height).SelectMany(y => Enumerable.Range(0, b.Level.Width + 1).Select(x => b.VJoint(x, y)))
            .Concat(Enumerable.Range(0, b.Level.Height + 1).SelectMany(y => Enumerable.Range(0, b.Level.Width).Select(x => b.HJoint(x, y)))).ToArray();

        [Test]
        public void QuadrantsCoverOddBoardsOnceAndSplitEvenly()
        {
            var level = Grid(5, 3);
            var regions = Enumerable.Range(0, 4).Select(q => WandRegion.Quadrant(level, q)).ToArray();
            for (int y = 0; y < 3; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 5; x++)
                Assert.That(regions.Count(r => r.Contains(new Cell(x, y))), Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(regions.Select(r => r.Width).Max() - regions.Select(r => r.Width).Min(), Is.LessThanOrEqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(regions.Select(r => r.Height).Max() - regions.Select(r => r.Height).Min(), Is.LessThanOrEqualTo(1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void RegionWandChangesOnlyInternalJointsAndKeepsSolvability()
        {
            foreach (uint seed in new uint[] { 1, 2, 7, 21, 99 }) for (int q = 0; q < 4; q++)
            {
                var s = Session(seed: seed); var before = s.Board.Clone(); var region = WandRegion.Quadrant(s.Level, q);
                int stock = s.Stock(Booster.Wand);
                Assert.That(s.CanWandRegion(q), Is.True);
                Assert.That(s.UseWandRegion(q), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.Ok));
                Assert.That(Joints(s.Board), Is.Not.EqualTo(Joints(before)), "must produce an actual change");
                Assert.That(s.Stock(Booster.Wand), Is.EqualTo(stock - 1));
                Assert.That(GreedySolver.Solve(s.Board).Solved, Is.True);
                for (int y = 0; y < s.Level.Height; y++) for (int x = 1; x < s.Level.Width; x++)
                    if (!region.Contains(new Cell(x - 1, y)) || !region.Contains(new Cell(x, y)))
                        Assert.That(s.Board.VJoint(x, y), Is.EqualTo(before.VJoint(x, y)));
                for (int y = 1; y < s.Level.Height; y++) for (int x = 0; x < s.Level.Width; x++)
                    if (!region.Contains(new Cell(x, y - 1)) || !region.Contains(new Cell(x, y)))
                        Assert.That(s.Board.HJoint(x, y), Is.EqualTo(before.HJoint(x, y)));
                Assert.That(s.Board.PresentPieces(), Is.EqualTo(before.PresentPieces()));
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void CrossingChainAndItsJointsStayUntouched()
        {
            var s = Session(Grid(chain: true)); var before = s.Board.Clone();
            Assert.That(s.UseWandRegion(0), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.Ok));
            Assert.That(s.Board.VJoint(1, 0), Is.EqualTo(before.VJoint(1, 0)));
            Assert.That(s.Board.VJoint(2, 0), Is.EqualTo(before.VJoint(2, 0)));
            Assert.That(s.Board.VJoint(3, 0), Is.EqualTo(before.VJoint(3, 0)));
            Assert.That(s.Board.HJoint(1, 1), Is.EqualTo(before.HJoint(1, 1)));
            Assert.That(s.Board.HJoint(2, 1), Is.EqualTo(before.HJoint(2, 1)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void PreviewConsumesNeitherRandomnessNorStockAndSeedReplays()
        {
            var a = Session(seed: 71); var b = Session(seed: 71);
            var before = Joints(a.Board); int stock = a.Stock(Booster.Wand);
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) Assert.That(a.CanWandRegion(0), Is.True);
            Assert.That(Joints(a.Board), Is.EqualTo(before)); Assert.That(a.Stock(Booster.Wand), Is.EqualTo(stock));
            a.UseWandRegion(0); b.UseWandRegion(0);
            Assert.That(Joints(a.Board), Is.EqualTo(Joints(b.Board)));
        }

        [Test]
        public void EmptyTinyAndImpossibleRegionsRejectWithoutSpending()
        {
            foreach (var s in new[] { Session(Grid(2, 2)), Session(Grid(sealedBoard: true)), Session() })
            {
                if (s.Level.Width == 4 && !s.Level.Sealed.Any())
                    foreach (int p in s.Board.PresentPieces().ToArray()) s.Board.Remove(p);
                var before = Joints(s.Board); int stock = s.Stock(Booster.Wand);
                Assert.That(s.CanWandRegion(0), Is.False);
                Assert.That(s.UseWandRegion(0), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.NoArrangement));
                Assert.That(Joints(s.Board), Is.EqualTo(before)); Assert.That(s.Stock(Booster.Wand), Is.EqualTo(stock));
                Assert.That(s.Events, Is.Empty);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void ClockFreezesWithoutAddingAndExpiresAcrossFractionalTicks()
        {
            var s = Session(); s.ClockFreezesTime = true;
            s.Tick(100); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10), "first move gate");
            Assert.That(s.UseClock(), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.Ok));
            Assert.That(s.ClockStarted, Is.True); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10));
            s.Tick(19.5); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10)); Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.EqualTo(.5).Within(1e-8));
            s.Tick(.75); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10)); Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.Zero);
            s.Tick(.75); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(s.Events.Any(e => e.Kind == EventKind.TimeAdded), Is.False);

using NUnit.Framework;
using ReverseSolver.Presentation;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core.Tests
{
    public sealed class JigoMoodTests
    {
        [TestCase(11, null, false, false, false, JigoState.Normal)]
        [TestCase(11, null, true, false, false, JigoState.Mutlu)]
        [TestCase(10, null, true, false, false, JigoState.Tedirgin)]
        [TestCase(3, null, true, false, false, JigoState.Korku)]
        [TestCase(4, 2, false, false, false, JigoState.Tedirgin)]
        [TestCase(30, 1, true, false, false, JigoState.Korku)]
        [TestCase(30, 2, true, false, false, JigoState.Mutlu)]
        [TestCase(3, 1, true, true, true, JigoState.Uzgun)]
        [TestCase(30, null, false, true, false, JigoState.Uzgun)]
        [TestCase(30, null, false, false, true, JigoState.Normal)]
        public void PriorityAndThresholds(float time, int? fuse, bool happy, bool lost, bool won, JigoState expected)
        {
            Assert.That(JigoMood.Read(time, fuse, happy, lost, won), Is.EqualTo(expected));
        }

        [Test]
        public void FractionalTimeAboveThresholdDoesNotEnterEarly()
        {
            Assert.That(JigoMood.Read(10.01f, null, false, false, false), Is.EqualTo(JigoState.Normal));
            Assert.That(JigoMood.Read(3.01f, null, false, false, false), Is.EqualTo(JigoState.Tedirgin));
            Assert.That(JigoMood.Read(0, null, true, false, false), Is.EqualTo(JigoState.Korku));
        }
    }
}

        [Test]
        public void ClockFreezesWithoutAddingAndExpiresAcrossFractionalTicks()
        {
            var s = Session(); s.ClockFreezesTime = true;
            s.Tick(100); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10), "first move gate");
            Assert.That(s.UseClock(), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.Ok));
            Assert.That(s.ClockStarted, Is.True); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10));
            s.Tick(19.5); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10)); Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.EqualTo(.5).Within(1e-8));
            s.Tick(.75); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10)); Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.Zero);
            s.Tick(.75); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Assert.That(s.Events.Any(e => e.Kind == EventKind.TimeAdded), Is.False);
        }

        [Test]
        public void FreezeDefersTimeoutAndContinueDoesNotResurrectExpiredFreeze()
        {
            var s = Session(); s.ClockOnFirstMove = false; s.ClockFreezesTime = true;
            s.Tick(9); s.UseClock(); s.Tick(GameSession.ClockBonusSeconds);
            Assert.That(s.IsOver, Is.False); Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(1));
            s.Tick(1); Assert.That(s.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(Outcome.Time));
            Assert.That(s.UseClock(), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.GameOver));
            Assert.That(s.Continue(5, 3), Is.True); s.Tick(1);
            Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(4)); Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ClockLegacyStillAddsTwentySeconds()
        {
            var s = Session(); s.UseClock();
            Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(10 + GameSession.ClockBonusSeconds));
            Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.Zero); s.Tick(1);
            Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(9 + GameSession.ClockBonusSeconds));
        }

        [Test]
        public void BombContinueRetainsFreezeAndFractionalCarryResumesAfterIt()
        {
            var level = P21Onboarding.Grid("freeze-bomb", 3, 1);
            level.Bombs = new Dictionary<int, int> { [2] = 1 };
            var s = Session(level); s.ClockFreezesTime = true; s.ClockOnFirstMove = false;
            s.Tick(.75); s.UseClock(); s.Tick(2);
            Assert.That(s.TryExit(0, Dir.L), Is.EqualTo(CommandResult.Ok));
            Assert.That(s.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(Outcome.Bomb));
            double freeze = s.FreezeLeft; s.Tick(100);
            Assert.That(s.FreezeLeft, Is.EqualTo(freeze), "loss screen does not drain the freeze");
            Assert.That(s.Continue(5, 3), Is.True);
            int time = s.TimeLeft; s.Tick(freeze + .25);
            Assert.That(s.TimeLeft, Is.EqualTo(time - 1), "carry from before freezing is retained");
        }
    }

        /* Drag dropped for the game's own reasons (level ends, layout changes):

        public void Cancel()

        void Sample(float x, float y, double time)
        {
            if (time < 0) return;
            if (_samples == _pt.Length)
            {
                System.Array.Copy(_px, 1, _px, 0, _px.Length - 1);
                System.Array.Copy(_py, 1, _py, 0, _py.Length - 1);
                System.Array.Copy(_pt, 1, _pt, 0, _pt.Length - 1);
                _samples--;
            }
            _px[_samples] = x; _py[_samples] = y; _pt[_samples] = time;
            _samples++;
        }

        /* Finger speed along Direction over the last SpeedWindow, pt/s (0 without timestamps). */
        float Speed()
        {
            if (!Direction.HasValue || _samples < 2) return 0;
            int last = _samples - 1, first = last;
            while (first > 0 && _pt[last] - _pt[first - 1] <= SpeedWindow) first--;
            if (first == last) first = last - 1;
            double dt = _pt[last] - _pt[first];
            if (dt <= 1e-4) return 0;
            var d = Direction.Value;
            float along = d.Dx() * (_px[last] - _px[first]) + d.Dy() * (_py[last] - _py[first]);
            return (float)(along / dt);
        }

        float MaxOffset()
        {
            if (Limit.Exit) return (Limit.Cells + ExitOvershoot) * CellSize;
            float give = Limit.Hard ? GiveHard : GiveSoft;
            return (Limit.Cells + give) * CellSize;
        }

using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;

namespace ReverseSolver.EditorTools
{
    // Copy only at build time. Never import or write source art metadata here.
    public sealed class T46BuildArt : IPreprocessBuildWithReport
    {
        public int callbackOrder => -100;
        public void OnPreprocessBuild(BuildReport report)
        {
            const string source = "Assets/Art/_yeni/T46";
            const string target = "Assets/_Game/Presentation/Resources/Art/yeni/T46";
            if (!Directory.Exists(source)) return;
            foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(source, "*.png", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
            {
                string dest = target + file.Substring(source.Length);
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(dest));
                File.Copy(file, dest, true);
                AssetDatabase.ImportAsset(dest.Replace('\\', '/'), ImportAssetOptions.ForceUpdate);
            }
        }
    }
}

        public int Hit(Vector2 pt)
        {

            if (_topButton.Contains(pt)) { SmoothTo(0); return -1; }

                if (node.rect.Contains(pt + new Vector2(0, Scroll.Offset))) return node.index;
            return -1;
        }

using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core.Tests
{
    /* The headless solver and Monte Carlo runs depend on Core never touching
       the engine. noEngineReferences enforces it at compile time; this test
       guards against someone flipping that flag. */
    public class CoreIsolationTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void CoreDoesNotReferenceUnity()
        {
            var core = Assembly.Load("ReverseSolver.Core");
            var unityRefs = core.GetReferencedAssemblies()
                .Select(a => a.Name)
                .Where(n => n.StartsWith("UnityEngine") || n.StartsWith("UnityEditor") || n.StartsWith("Unity."))
                .ToArray();
            Assert.That(unityRefs, Is.Empty);
        }
    }
}
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    /* Minimal time-based tweens, driven from one MonoBehaviour. Only what the
       M3 effects need: settle back, fly out, a bomb pulse and a card fade. */
    public sealed class Tweens : MonoBehaviour
    {
        sealed class T { public float Start, Duration; public Action<float> Step; public Action Done; public object Key; }

        readonly List<T> _live = new List<T>();
        static Tweens _instance;

        public static Tweens Instance
        {
            get
            {
                if (_instance == null) _instance = new GameObject("Tweens").AddComponent<Tweens>();
                return _instance;
            }
        }

        /* Runs step(t) for t in 0..1 over duration seconds. A new tween with the
           same key replaces the running one. */
        public static void Run(object key, float duration, Action<float> step, Action done = null)
        {
            var self = Instance;
            if (key != null) self._live.RemoveAll(t => Equals(t.Key, key));
            self._live.Add(new T { Start = Time.unscaledTime, Duration = Mathf.Max(duration, 1e-4f), Step = step, Done = done, Key = key });
            step(0);
        }

        public static void Kill(object key) => Instance._live.RemoveAll(t => Equals(t.Key, key));

        void Update()
        {
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            for (int i = _live.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                if (i >= _live.Count) continue;
                var t = _live[i];
                float k = Mathf.Clamp01((now - t.Start) / t.Duration);
                t.Step(k);
                if (k >= 1f)
                {
                    _live.Remove(t);
                    t.Done?.Invoke();
                }
            }
        }

        // ---- easing: the web game's CSS cubic-beziers -------------------------------

        /* cubic-bezier(x1, y1, x2, y2) evaluated at time x (Newton on x, then y). */
        public static float Bezier(float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, float x)
        {
            float t = x;
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)

using System;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core
{
    public enum Booster { Scissors, Wand, Hammer, Clock }

    public static class BoosterExt
    {
        public static readonly Booster[] All = { Booster.Scissors, Booster.Wand, Booster.Hammer, Booster.Clock };

        /* Ids used in the level file and in telemetry (b_scissors, ...). */
        public static string Id(this Booster b) => b switch
        {
            Booster.Scissors => "scissors",
            Booster.Wand => "wand",
            Booster.Hammer => "hammer",
            _ => "clock"
        };
    }

    public enum Outcome { None, Win, Time, Bomb, Quit }

    public static class OutcomeExt
    {
        /* Values of the `result` column in the plays table. */
        public static string Id(this Outcome o) => o switch
        {
            Outcome.Win => "win",
            Outcome.Time => "time",
            Outcome.Bomb => "bomb",
            Outcome.Quit => "quit",
            _ => ""
        };
    }

    /* Why a command was refused. Refused commands change nothing and raise no event. */
    public enum CommandResult
    {
        Ok,
        GameOver,        // the attempt has already ended
        NoSuchPiece,     // not a piece, or already removed
        Pinned,          // nail counter above zero
        Blocked,         // no exit in that direction
        Locked,          // booster not unlocked on this level
        OutOfStock,      // booster count is zero
        NotAJoint,       // scissors: not a tabbed joint between two different pieces
        NoArrangement    // wand: no solvable arrangement found; nothing spent
    }

    public enum EventKind
    {
        PieceRemoved,    // Piece, Dir (Dir is meaningless when ByHammer)
        Jammed,          // Piece
        NailsTicked,     // every nail counter above zero went down by one
        NailFreed,       // Piece: its counter reached zero on a tick
        ValveReleased,   // Piece: safety valve pulled the longest-waiting nail
        BombsTicked,     // every fuse on a present piece went down by one
        BombDefused,     // Piece: a bomb left the board with its piece
        TimeTick,        // Value = seconds left
        TimeAdded,       // Value = seconds added
        JointCut,        // X, Y, Vertical
        JointsReshuffled,
        BoosterUsed,     // Booster
        Finished,        // Outcome
        Continued        // revived after a time or bomb loss
    }

    /* One thing that happened in a session. Presentation listens to these; the
       full list is also kept so a replay can be compared event by event. */
    public readonly struct GameEvent : IEquatable<GameEvent>
    {
        public readonly EventKind Kind;
        public readonly int Piece;
        public readonly Dir Dir;
        public readonly bool ByHammer;

        readonly IKeyValueStore _store;

        public int Coins { get; private set; }

        /* Faz 4 G8 (ğŸ‘/ğŸ‘, SetFlags.AskFun): lost attempts per level and whether the question was asked. */

        public SaveData(IKeyValueStore store, Func<string> newId)
        {
            _store = store;
            Coins = int.TryParse(store.Get(Key("coins")), out int coins) && coins >= 0 ? coins : new ContinueRules().StartCoins;
            // PM_MESAJ_57: start balance rose to 5000; lift existing lower balances once.
            if (store.Get(Key("coins_topup57")) == null)
            {
                if (Coins < 5000) SetCoins(5000);
                store.Set(Key("coins_topup57"), "1");
                store.Save();
            }
            Player = store.Get(Key("player")) ?? "";
            Install = store.Get(Key("install"));
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Install))
            {
                Install = newId();
                store.Set(Key("install"), Install);
                store.Save();
            }
            ReadCounts("attempts", Attempts);
            ReadCounts("best", Best);
            ReadCounts("losses", Losses);
            ReadCounts("fun_asked", FunAsked);
            ReadCounts("chests21", _chests21);
        }

        public void SetCoins(int coins)
        {
            Coins = Math.Max(0, coins);

            _store.Save();
        }

        static bool HasLevels(TextAsset file)
        {
            if (file == null) return false;
            var root = Core.Json.JsonReader.Parse(file.text);
            return root.TryGet("levels", out var list) && list.Count > 0;
        }

        /* lv as a number (1-based position) or as a level id. */
        bool TryLevelIndex(string lv, out int index)
        {
            index = 0;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(lv)) return false;
            if (int.TryParse(lv, out int n)) { index = Mathf.Clamp(n, 1, _set.Levels.Count) - 1; return true; }
            for (int i = 0; i < _set.Levels.Count; i++)
                if (_set.Levels[i].Id == lv) { index = i; return true; }
            return false;
        }

            }

                _continueSender?.Flush();
                if (_continueResultOnReturn) { _continueResultOnReturn = false; ShowResult(); }

using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    sealed class WinCelebration54
    {
        const float Duration = 2;
        readonly Transform _root;
        readonly SpriteRenderer _jigo, _rays;
        readonly Sprite[] _frames = new Sprite[8];
        readonly SpriteRenderer[] _confetti = new SpriteRenderer[36];
        readonly Vector2[] _from = new Vector2[36], _travel = new Vector2[36];
        readonly float[] _spin = new float[36];
        readonly float _start;
        readonly ShapeView _shade;
        bool _skipped;
        int _frame = -1;

        public WinCelebration54(Transform parent, Vector2 screen, int order)
        {
            _root = new GameObject("Win celebration").transform;
            _root.SetParent(parent, false);
            _start = Time.unscaledTime;
            _shade = ShapeView.Create(_root, "shade", screen, 0, order).Fill(Draw.Rgba(10, 18, 46, .65f));
            _rays = Art.Place(_root, "jigo/sevinc/fx/light-rays", Vector2.one * 360, order + 1);
            _jigo = Art.Place(_root, "jigo/sevinc/01", Vector2.one * 320, order + 2);
            for (int i = 0; i < _frames.Length; i++) _frames[i] = Art.Get("jigo/sevinc/" + (i + 1).ToString("00"));
            string[] colours = { "coral", "gold", "purple", "teal" };
            for (int i = 0; i < _confetti.Length; i++)
            {
                _confetti[i] = Art.Place(_root, "jigo/sevinc/fx/confetti-" + colours[i % 4], Vector2.one * Random.Range(9f, 14f), order + 3);
                _from[i] = new Vector2(Random.Range(-.5f, .5f), Random.Range(-.65f, -.25f));
                _travel[i] = new Vector2(Random.Range(-.2f, .2f), Random.Range(.8f, 1.2f));
                _spin[i] = Random.Range(-720f, 720f);
            }
            Layout(screen);
            Tick(screen);
        }

        public void Layout(Vector2 screen)
        {
            _root.localPosition = Draw.W(screen * .5f);
            _shade.Resize(screen, 0);
            float size = Mathf.Min(screen.x * .86f, screen.y * .6f);
            Fit(_jigo, size);
            Fit(_rays, size * 1.25f);
        }

        static void Fit(SpriteRenderer r, float size)
        {
            if (r != null) r.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * (size / Mathf.Max(r.sprite.bounds.size.x, r.sprite.bounds.size.y));
        }

        public bool Tick(Vector2 screen)
        {
            float t = Mathf.Clamp01((Time.unscaledTime - _start) / Duration);
            int frame = Mathf.FloorToInt((Time.unscaledTime - _start) * 10) % 8;
            if (_jigo != null && frame != _frame) _jigo.sprite = _frames[frame];
            _frame = frame;
            if (_rays != null) _rays.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, t * 30);
            // Same falling, spinning, fading style as Screens.Confetti.
            for (int i = 0; i < _confetti.Length; i++)
            {
                var r = _confetti[i];
                if (r == null) continue;
                r.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(Vector2.Scale(_from[i] + _travel[i] * t, screen));
                r.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, _spin[i] * t);
                r.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 1 - t * t);
            }
            return _skipped || t >= 1;
        }

        public void Skip() { _skipped = true; }
        public void Destroy() { if (_root != null) Object.Destroy(_root.gameObject); }
    }
}

                s.Glyph.SetParent(_root, false);
                s.Glyph.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, cy);
                if (!Art.T14) Icons.Draw(b.Id(), s.Glyph, Art.On ? 27.2f : 33, order + 2);   // Task 14 buttons carry their icon   // art icons draw at 1.25x: 34 pt = ~75 % of the inner disc (PALETTE: never on the ring)
                // .badge: 24px circle at top -8 right -8, gradient #63c2ef -> #2f8fd6, 3px white ring
                // PM_MESAJ_13 (preview): red badge at the bottom right, 2 px light rim, white Fredoka digits
                var bc = Art.On ? new Vector2(cx + 30 - 5, cy + 30 - 5) : new Vector2(cx + 30 - 4, cy - 30 + 4);
                s.Badge = ShapeView.Create(_root, "badge", new Vector2(24, 24), 2, order + 3).Radius(12)
                    .Gradient(Draw.Hex("#63c2ef"), Draw.Hex("#2f8fd6")).Stroke(Draw.Rgba(255, 255, 255, .92f), 3);
                if (Art.On) s.Badge.Fill(Palette.Red).Stroke(Palette.WarnWhite, 2).Skin("booster-badge", false);   // PALETTE (Task 15): red, warning-white rim, cream digits
                else s.Badge.Skin("booster-badge-stock", false);
                s.Badge.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(bc);
                s.Count = TextView.Create(_root, "count", "", Art.On ? 15f : 12.5f, Draw.Hex("#ffffff"), order: order + 4);
                if (Art.On) s.Count.Font(Art.T14 ? Art.Lilita : Resources.Load<TMP_FontAsset>("Fonts/Counter"));
                if (Art.T14) s.Count.Color = Palette.Cream;
                s.Count.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(bc);
                s.Lock = new GameObject("lock").transform;
                s.Lock.SetParent(_root, false);
                s.Lock.localPosition = Draw.W(bc);
                if (!Art.T14) Icons.Draw("lock", s.Lock, 13, order + 4);
                // .bst-name: 9px, letter-spacing .16em, uppercase, muted
                s.Name = TextView.Create(_root, "name", Texts.BoosterName(b), 9, Draw.Hex("#8fb2b3"), order: order);
                s.Name.GetComponent<TextMeshPro>().characterSpacing = 16;
                s.Name.transform.localPosition = Draw.W(cx, cy + 30 + 5 + 6);
                if (Art.On) s.Name.gameObject.SetActive(false);                  // PM_MESAJ_13: no names under the buttons
                _slots.Add(s);
            }

using System.Collections.Generic;
using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    public sealed class LevelsMap
    {
        // MenuUi.Sprite adds the t20- prefix; node art is the Task23 final set (PM_MESAJ_37).
        const string BackSprite = "button-back";
        const string PathSprite = "t20-levels-path-repeat.png";
        const string CurrentNodeSprite = "t23-node-current";
        const string CompleteNodeSprite = "t23-node-complete";
        const string LockedBaseSprite = "t23-node-locked";
        const string HardLockedBaseSprite = "t23-node-hard-locked";
        const string ShadowSprite = "t23-node-contact-shadow";
        const string PlayBubbleSprite = "t23-play-bubble";
        const string HardBadgeSprite = "t23-hard-marker";
        const string LockBadgeSprite = "t23-lock-badge";
        const string WonBadgeSprite = "t23-complete-check";

        public readonly MenuScroll Scroll;
        readonly Rect _back, _topButton, _bottomButton;
        readonly Transform _currentPill;
        readonly TextView _currentLabel;
        readonly float _currentY, _height, _width;
        readonly int _currentLevel;
        Rect _pill;
        void SmoothTo(float target)
        {
            float from = Scroll.Offset;
            Tweens.Run(Scroll, .45f, t => { if (Scroll.Root != null && !Scroll.Held) Scroll.Set(Mathf.Lerp(from, target, t * t * (3 - 2 * t))); });
        }
        public void Tick()
        {
            float y = _currentY - Scroll.Offset;
            bool above = y < Scroll.Top + 35, below = y > Scroll.Bottom - 35;
            _currentPill.gameObject.SetActive(above || below);
            if (!above && !below) return;
            var center = new Vector2(_width / 2, above ? Scroll.Top + 22 : Scroll.Bottom - 22);
            _pill = new Rect(center - new Vector2(68, 18), new Vector2(136, 36));
            _currentPill.localPosition = Draw.W(center);
            _currentLabel.Text = Lang.T($"Level {_currentLevel}", $"Seviye {_currentLevel}") + (above ? " ↑" : " ↓");
        }
        readonly List<(Rect rect, int index)> _nodes = new List<(Rect, int)>();
        public LevelsMap(Transform root, Vector2 screen, float top, float bottom, IReadOnlyList<LevelData> levels, Progress progress, LevelImages images, int order, float? offset)
        {
            MenuUi.Scene(root, "levels", screen, order - 10);
            MenuUi.Title(root, screen, top, Texts.Levels, order + 30, heading: true);
            _back = MenuUi.Button(root, BackSprite, null, new Vector2(30, top + 34), Vector2.one * 38, order + 32);
            _back = new Rect(_back.center - Vector2.one * 22, Vector2.one * 44);
            const float pitch = 76;
            float start = top + 90, height = levels.Count * pitch + 120;
            Scroll = new MenuScroll(root, start, bottom, height);
            MenuUi.Viewport(root, "levels", screen, start, bottom, order + 20);
            // Each repeat is 256 x 512 pt, centred on the same vertical path.
            var pathRoot = Scroll.Root;
            System.Action<Sprite> showPath = sprite =>
            {
                if (pathRoot == null) return;
                for (float y = start + 256; y < start + height + 256; y += 512)
                {

function Compile($name, $dir, $refs, $extra = @()) {
  $sourceFiles = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath (Join-Path $projectRoot $dir) -Filter '*.cs' -Recurse | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }
  & dotnet $compiler @baseRefs @refs ('-out:' + $outDir + '/' + $name + '.dll') @extra @sourceFiles

  Write-Output "[compile] $name OK"
}

    void Failures(NUnit.Framework.Interfaces.ITestResult r) {
      if (!r.HasChildren && r.FailCount > 0) Console.WriteLine("FAIL " + r.FullName + ": " + r.Message + "\n" + r.StackTrace);

    Failures(result);

    System.IO.File.WriteAllText(".tmp/job-a/results.xml", result.ToXml(true).OuterXml);
    return result.FailCount == 0 ? 0 : 1;
  }

            _continueQueue = new TelemetryQueue(host.Store, "continue");
            _continueSender = new TelemetrySender(_continueQueue, send ? host.Transport : null, host.SupabaseUrl, host.AnonKey, send, "continue_events?on_conflict=row_id");

        void Sync(GameSession session)
        {
            _time = session.TimeLeft;
            _lost = session.IsOver && session.Outcome != Outcome.Win;
            _won = session.Outcome == Outcome.Win;
            _fuse = null;
            foreach (var bomb in session.Bombs)
                if (!_fuse.HasValue || bomb.Value < _fuse.Value) _fuse = bomb.Value;
        }

        public void OnEvent(GameEvent e, GameSession session)
        {
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved) _happyUntil = Time.unscaledTime + JigoMood.HappySeconds;
            Sync(session);
        }

        public void Update()
        {
            if (_joy) { _back.color = Color.clear; return; }
            float now = Time.unscaledTime;
            int mood = _forced >= 0 ? _forced : (int)JigoMood.Read(_time, _fuse, now < _happyUntil, _lost, _won);
            if (mood != _current)
            {
                _previous = _current < 0 ? mood : _current;
                _current = mood; _changedAt = now;
            }
            if (now >= _nextBlink) { _blinkAt = now; _nextBlink = now + Random.Range(4f, 7f); }
            // Legacy loop frames include a blink; play that sequence only at random intervals.
            int frame = now - _blinkAt < .375f ? Mathf.Min(5, (int)((now - _blinkAt) * 16)) : 0;
            Sprite Read(int m)
            {
                var loop = m == 0 ? _loops[0] : m == 3 ? _loops[1] : null;
                return loop != null && loop.Length > 0 ? loop[(int)(now * 16) % loop.Length] : _frames[m][frame] ?? Art.Get(Art54.NormalFrame);
            }
            _front.sprite = Read(_current);
            _back.sprite = Read(_previous);
            int transition = _previous == 0 && _current == 3 ? 0 : _previous == 3 && _current == 0 ? 1 : -1;
            if (transition >= 0 && now - _changedAt < JigoMood.FadeSeconds && _transitions[transition].Length > 0)
            {
                var frames = _transitions[transition];
                _front.sprite = frames[Mathf.Min(frames.Length - 1, (int)((now - _changedAt) / JigoMood.FadeSeconds * frames.Length))];
            }
            float breath = Mathf.Sin(now * Mathf.PI * 2 / 2.2f);
            float stretch = .035f * Mathf.Sin(Mathf.Clamp01((now - _changedAt) / JigoMood.FadeSeconds) * Mathf.PI);
            var scale = new Vector3(1 + .02f * breath + stretch, 1 + .02f * breath - stretch, 1);
            _front.transform.localScale = Vector3.Scale(_frontScale, scale);
            _back.transform.localScale = Vector3.Scale(_backScale, scale);
            _front.transform.localRotation = _back.transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, 1.5f * breath);
            float alpha = Mathf.Clamp01((now - _changedAt) / JigoMood.FadeSeconds);
            _front.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, alpha);
            _back.color = new Color(1, 1, 1, 1 - alpha);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core
{
    public readonly struct WandRegion
    {
        public readonly int X, Y, Width, Height;
        public WandRegion(int x, int y, int width, int height) { X = x; Y = y; Width = width; Height = height; }
        public bool Contains(Cell c) => c.X >= X && c.Y >= Y && c.X < X + Width && c.Y < Y + Height;
        public static WandRegion Quadrant(LevelData level, int index)
        {
            if (index < 0 || index > 3) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(index));
            int w = (level.Width + 1) / 2, h = (level.Height + 1) / 2;
            return new WandRegion(index % 2 == 0 ? 0 : w, index < 2 ? 0 : h,
                index % 2 == 0 ? w : level.Width - w, index < 2 ? h : level.Height - h);
        }
    }

            if (arrangement == null) return CommandResult.NoArrangement;

namespace ReverseSolver.EditorTools

    /* One-click web build into Builds/WebGL, ready to push to GitHub Pages.
       Pages serves files as-is without a Content-Encoding header, so the build
       ships compressed files with the JS decompression fallback rather than
       relying on the server. Those settings are applied here too, so a build
       can never go out with a config that will not load on Pages.

       Every build appends its output sizes to Builds/size-log.csv and writes
       Builds/<dir>-report.txt (what went into the wasm and the data file), so
       the effect of a size change is measured rather than guessed. */
    public static class WebBuild
    {
        public const string OutputDir = "Builds/WebGL";        // shipped: Brotli, served at /br/
        public const string GzipOutputDir = "Builds/WebGL-gz"; // backup: gzip, served at /gz/
        /* Brotli was 0.8 s faster to open than gzip on an iPhone (PRODUCT.md,
           2026-10-06); its smaller download outweighs the slower JS decode. */
        public const WebGLCompressionFormat ShippedCompression = WebGLCompressionFormat.Brotli;
        const string SizeLog = "Builds/size-log.csv";
        const string StrippedDir = "Library/Bee/artifacts/WebGL/ManagedStripped";

        [MenuItem("Reverse Solver/Build WebGL")]
        public static void Build() => Build("manual", OutputDir, ShippedCompression);

        [MenuItem("Reverse Solver/Build WebGL (gzip backup)")]
        public static void BuildGzipBackup() => Build("manual gzip", GzipOutputDir, WebGLCompressionFormat.Gzip);

        /* Batch mode entry (CI: game-ci/unity-builder buildMethod). Builds the
           shipped Brotli build and the gzip backup with the same settings as
           the menu, then exits non-zero if either failed.
           Optional: -rsLabel "<text>" for the size log. */
        public static void BuildFromCommandLine()
        {
            var args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            string label = "ci";
            for (int i = 0; i < args.Length - 1; i++) if (args[i] == "-rsLabel") label = args[i + 1];
            bool ok = Build(label + " Brotli", OutputDir, ShippedCompression)
                    & Build(label + " gzip", GzipOutputDir, WebGLCompressionFormat.Gzip);
            if (Application.isBatchMode) EditorApplication.Exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
        }

        /* applySettings: false builds with the project settings as they are, so a
           size experiment can change one thing at a time. */

                Debug.LogError("WebBuild: no enabled scenes in Build Settings.");
                return false;

                target = BuildTarget.WebGL,
                options = BuildOptions.DetailedBuildReport

# T46 — Saat booster ve Jigo geçişleri

**65 RGBA PNG**, tek parça saat pozları, rahat kaşlar, mavi/krem ve doygun altın dili. [390 × 844 önizleme](onizleme.png), [saat animasyonu](clock_review.gif), [Jigo animasyonu](jigo_review.gif), [dosya/ölçü/SHA-256 manifestosu](manifest.json).

## Dosyalar ve oynatma

Tüm yollar `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/` köküne göredir. PNG'lerin yanında sabit GUID, şeffaflık, bilinear filtre, mipmap kapalı ve özel pivot içeren `.png.meta` dosyaları bulunur. Saat pozlarında ayrı kol katmanı yoktur; eski T42 rig parçaları kullanılmaz.

| Dosya/dizi | Kare | Tuval px | FPS | Oynatma |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| `clock/extend/01..06.png` | 6 | 352 × 256 | 12 | Bir kez, 0,50 sn. Düğme iner, gövde döner ve fırça sağa uzanır. |
| `clock/hold/01..04.png` | 4 | 352 × 256 | 12 | 0,333 sn nefes döngüsü; 10 sn boyunca 30 tur. Temas ucu sabit. |
| `clock/retract/01..05.png` | 5 | 352 × 256 | 12 | Bir kez, 0,417 sn. Fırça geri gelir ve düğme alınır. |
| `clock/spark/01..06.png` | 6 | 96 × 96 | 24 | İlk temasta bir kez, 0,25 sn. Altın-beyaz yıldızlar. |
| `clock/ice_overlay.png` | 1 | 339 × 290 | — | Temastan itibaren panelde 10 sn; sayılar görünür kalır. |
| `clock/ice_break/01..07.png` | 7 | 339 × 434 | 18 | Bir kez, 0,389 sn. 01 sağlam, 02 çatlak, 03–06 düşen parçalar, 07 boş. |
| `clock/freeze_bar_bg.png` | 1 | 300 × 42 | — | Krem çerçeve ve mavi kanal. |
| `clock/freeze_bar_fill.png` | 1 | 288 × 30 | — | Buz mavisi dolgu; sağdan kırpılır. |
| `jigo/normal_to_happy/01..05.png` | 5 | 256 × 256 | 20 | Bir kez, 0,25 sn; göz, yanak, ağız ve fırça yüksekliği kademeli. |
| `jigo/happy_to_normal/01..05.png` | 5 | 256 × 256 | 20 | Aynı pozların tersi, 0,25 sn. |
| `jigo/normal_loop/01..12.png` | 12 | 256 × 256 | 20 | 0,60 sn döngü; altı özgün kare + altı hareket telafili ara kare. |
| `jigo/happy_loop/01..12.png` | 12 | 256 × 256 | 20 | 0,60 sn döngü; son→ilk arası da örneklenmiştir. |

Döngüler 12–24 FPS aralığında kullanılabilir; eski altı karelik 10 FPS zamanlamasını korumak için **20 FPS** önerilir. Kaynak 01..06, yeni 01/03/05/07/09/11 kareleriyle piksel düzeyinde aynıdır. 12. kare, eski 06→01 hareketinin ortasıdır. İfade geçişlerini döngünün 01 fazında başlatıp hedef döngünün 01 fazına bağla; iki farklı yüzü alfa ile üst üste bindirme. GIF zamanları 10 ms adımlara yuvarlanabilir; tabloda motor için kesin hızlar verilmiştir.

## Tuval, pivot ve ofset

Koordinatlar **PNG sol üstünden**, x sağa/y aşağıya; tüm değerler kaynak pikselidir.

| Grup | Pivot px (sol üst) | Unity normalize pivot (sol alt) | Yerleşim/ofset |
|---|---|---|---|
| Jigo döngü/geçiş | (128, 128) | (0,5; 0,5) | T44 ile aynı 256 × 256 tuval, sıfır ofset. |
| Saat extend/hold/retract | (128, 128) | (0,363636364; 0,5) | Eski 256 × 256 alanın sağına **96 px** saydam pay eklenir; eski alanın sol üstü (0,0), içerik ofseti (0,0). |
| Işıltı | (48, 48) | (0,5; 0,5) | Merkezini her karenin fırça ucuna bağla. |
| Buz kaplaması | (169,5; 145) | (0,5; 0,5) | Süre paneli gövdesiyle aynı merkez/ölçek. |
| Buz kırılma | (169,5; 145) | (0,5; 0,665898618) | İlk 290 satır kaplamayla birebir hizalı, aşağıda **144 px** düşüş payı. Kaplamayla aynı dünya pivotu. |
| Bar bg/fill | tuval merkezi | (0,5; 0,5) | Dolgu bg sol üstüne göre (+6,+6) px; merkezleri ortaktır. |

Saat karesini genişliğe sığdırarak küçültme: normal büstün piksel ölçeğini koru. Normal büst 108 px yüksekliğinde gösteriliyorsa yeni saat tuvali 148,5 × 108 px gösterilir. Madalyon arka planı sabit kalır; saat çizimi madalyon maskesinin **üstünde** yer alır, dışarı uzanan fırça kırpılmaz. `retract/05` ve `extend/01` içindeki ilk 256 × 256 piksel normal kaynakla aynıdır; ek 96 px tamamen saydamdır.

## Fırça ucu

Temas/ışıltı yerleşim noktaları, saat tuvali içinde px; başlangıçta fırçanın mavi üst ucu, uzanmış halde sağdaki mavi uç referans alınır. Bütün kareler ayrıca `manifest.json` içindedir.

| Kare | x | y |
|---|---:|---:|
| extend/01 | 23 | 119 |
| extend/02 | 52 | 119 |
| extend/03 | 214 | 123 |
| extend/04 | 248 | 118 |
| extend/05 | 273 | 134 |
| extend/06 | 290 | 140 |
| hold/01..04 | 290 | 140 |
| retract/01 | 273 | 134 |
| retract/02 | 248 | 118 |
| retract/03 | 214 | 123 |
| retract/04 | 52 | 119 |
| retract/05 | 23 | 119 |

Genel yerleşim formülü: `uç_ekran = pivot_ekran + (uç_px − (128,128)) × ölçek`. Tutma nefesi bütün büste temas noktası etrafında ±%0,8 uygulanır; kol veya el ayrı hareket ettirilmez. Buz 10 saniye boyunca sabit kalır; başlangıç ışıltısı hold döngüsünde tekrar tetiklenmez. Buz kırılırken statik kaplamayı kaldır; son boş kareden sonra katmanı kapat ve geri alma dizisini oynat.

## Panel ve 9-slice

Mevcut `Hud54.Panel`: 390 × 844 ekranda **113 × 96,6667 px**, merkez (324;108,6667), sol üst (267,5;60,3333), güvenli üst alan ofseti ayrıca eklenir. 3× dokuda **339 × 290 px** bu ölçüyü tam karşılar. Eski HUD31 panel bitmap'i 340 × 290 olsa da kod gövdeyi 113 px genişliğe gerer. Buz, başlık grafiğine değil bu gövdeye oturur. Önizlemedeki HUD yakın planı akışı göstermek için aşağı taşınmıştır; motor yerleşim koordinatları bu paragraftadır.

| PNG | 9-slice sol/alt/sağ/üst | 390 px ekranda | Kullanım |
|---|---|---|---|
| freeze_bar_bg | 14 / 14 / 14 / 14 px | 100 × 14 px | Sliced, en az 28 × 28 kaynak px. |
| freeze_bar_fill | 10 / 10 / 10 / 10 px | 96 × 10 px | Sabit tam boy dolgu üzerinde soldan ankrajlı kırpma. |

Bar merkezi için panelin altında (324;150) + güvenli üst alan ofseti kullanılabilir. Dolgu oranı `clamp(kalanSüre / 10, 0, 1)`; 0'da görünürlüğü kapat. Uç kapakların ezilmemesi için dolguyu daraltarak ölçeklemek yerine maskeyle sağdan kırp. Buz kaplaması ve kırılma kareleri slice edilmez.

## Üretim ve kontrol

- Kaynaklar: `T44/Jigo/normal/01..06.png`, `T44/Jigo/mutlu/01..06.png`, T44/T45 paftaları, eski T42 saat akışı ve `Hud54.cs` ölçüleri. T44 Jigo kareleri onaylı Jigo_v3 kaynaklarının kopyalarıdır.
- **Yerleşik image_gen** kullanıldı; CLI/API kullanılmadı. [Saat kaynak paftası](clock_source.png), [ifade kaynak paftası](jigo_source.png). Saydam alfa korundu. Kare kesme, sabit pivot yerleşimi, bütün büstte nefes dönüşümü, fırça yüksekliği geçişi ve hareket telafili zaman örnekleme [build_task46.py](build_task46.py) içindedir. UI buz/ışıltı/bar grafikleri aynı betikle yüksek çözünürlükte çizilip küçültülür.
- Saat üretim istemi: exact T44 Jigo identity; transparent 1536×1024, 3×2 equal cells; six poses normal→lower button→turn torso→bring brush across chest→reach right→full fencing reach; one continuous torso/shoulder/arm/hand/brush drawing, no rig patches, one brush, friendly brows, silver thimble, gold/blue brush, cream four-hole button, rich glossy shading, no text/grid/banner/ribbon/clock prop.
- İfade üretim istemi: exact normal and happy T44 references; transparent 1536×1024, 3×2 equal cells; six progressively redrawn expressions from open amber eyes/small smile to closed smiling eyes/open laughing mouth; fixed scale and body placement, brush left/button right, relaxed eyebrows throughout, no crossfade/double features, no text/grid/banner/ribbon.
- Doğrulama: **65/65 RGBA PNG**, istenen kare sayıları ve boyutları, 12 özgün döngü anahtarının piksel eşitliği, dönüş sonunun kaynak normalle eşitliği, sağ payın saydamlığı ve **390 × 844** önizleme boyutu betikte assert ile kontrol edildi. SHA-256 manifestosu yeniden üretildi. PNG'ler lacivert zemin üzerinde görsel incelendi; kaşlar rahat, kol tek parça, buz merkezi okunabilir.
- Teslim yalnızca `Assets/Art/_yeni/T46/` ve `docs/art/task46/` içindedir; oyun kodu entegrasyonu bu teslimin parçası değildir. Motor içinde oynatma testi yapılmadı; import ayarları ve animasyon inceleme GIF'leri sağlandı.

using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;

namespace ReverseSolver.EditorTools
{
    // Copy only at build time. Never import or write source art metadata here.
    public sealed class T46BuildArt : IPreprocessBuildWithReport
    {
        public int callbackOrder => -100;
        public void OnPreprocessBuild(BuildReport report)
        {
            const string source = "Assets/Art/_yeni/T46";
            const string target = "Assets/_Game/Presentation/Resources/Art/yeni/T46";
            if (!Directory.Exists(source)) return;
            foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(source, "*.png", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
            {
                string dest = target + file.Substring(source.Length);
                Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(dest));
                File.Copy(file, dest, true);
                dest = dest.Replace('\\', '/');
                AssetDatabase.ImportAsset(dest, ImportAssetOptions.ForceUpdate);
                var importer = (TextureImporter)AssetImporter.GetAtPath(dest);
                var original = AssetImporter.GetAtPath(file.Replace('\\', '/')) as TextureImporter;
                if (original != null)
                {
                    var settings = new TextureImporterSettings(); original.ReadTextureSettings(settings);
                    importer.SetTextureSettings(settings);
                    importer.spritePixelsPerUnit = original.spritePixelsPerUnit;
                    importer.spriteBorder = original.spriteBorder;
                }
                importer.SaveAndReimport();
            }
        }
    }
}

Assets/_Game/Presentation/Board/BoardSizing.cs:8:        public const float SafeMarginPt = 24f;
Assets/_Game/Core/Input/BoardLayout.cs:35:        public const float Reach = 24f;
using System;

namespace ReverseSolver.Core
{
    /* Board size on screen and the touch margin around it; the one place the
       game (GameRoot) and the level editor's phone warning both take it from.

       The web game's sizeCell is min((W - 38) / w, (H - 262) / h, 78): 19 pt
       either side, which leaves the tray 1-2 pt from the screen edge, and a
       piece in an outer column has to be pulled past the edge to leave
       (PRODUCT.md K1). The side gap is now
           G = clamp((W - 44 w) / 2, 19, 34)
       so boards keep 44 pt cells where they can and get up to 34 pt of room to
       pull in. A deliberate difference from the web. */
    public static class BoardLayout
    {
        public const float MinCell = 44f, MaxCell = 78f;
        public const float MinGap = 19f, MaxGap = 34f;
        public const float VerticalReserve = 262f;   // strip, prompt, booster bar (web sizeCell)

        public static float SideGap(float screenW, int width) =>
            Math.Max(MinGap, Math.Min(MaxGap, (screenW - MinCell * width) / 2f));

        /* innerH: screen height minus the safe-area insets. */
        public static float CellSize(float screenW, float innerH, int width, int height) =>
            (float)Math.Floor(Math.Min(Math.Min((screenW - 2 * SideGap(screenW, width)) / width,
                                                (innerH - VerticalReserve) / height), MaxCell));
    }

    /* Grabbing a piece from the margin around the board (PRODUCT.md K1): a
       press up to Reach points outside the board, on the tray's rim or on a
       wall, takes the piece in the nearest outer cell within that distance. */
    public static class EdgeGrab
    {
        public const float Reach = 24f;

        /* (px, py): the press in board points, (0, 0) at the board's top-left. */
        public static int PieceAt(Board b, float px, float py, float cell, out bool outside)
        {
            var l = b.Level;
            float w = l.Width * cell, h = l.Height * cell;
            outside = px < 0 || py < 0 || px >= w || py >= h;
            if (!outside) return b.PieceAt(new Cell((int)(px / cell), (int)(py / cell)));

            int best = -1;
            float bestD = Reach + 1e-3f;
            for (int y = 0; y < l.Height; y++)
                for (int x = 0; x < l.Width; x++)
                {
                    if (x != 0 && y != 0 && x != l.Width - 1 && y != l.Height - 1) continue;
                    int p = b.PieceAt(new Cell(x, y));

using ReverseSolver.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace ReverseSolver.Presentation
{
    sealed class JigoPortrait54
    {
        readonly Sprite[][] _frames = new Sprite[5][];
        readonly SpriteRenderer _front, _back;
        readonly int _forced;
        readonly bool _joy;
        public void Visible(bool visible) { _front.gameObject.SetActive(visible); _back.gameObject.SetActive(visible); }
        int _current = -1, _previous, _time;
        int? _fuse;
        bool _lost, _won;
        float _happyUntil, _changedAt, _nextBlink, _blinkAt = -10;
        float _portraitHeight;
        readonly Sprite[][] _loops = new Sprite[2][], _transitions = new Sprite[2][];
        static Sprite[] Frames(string path)
        {
            var frames = new System.Collections.Generic.List<Sprite>();
            for (int i = 1; i <= 48; i++) { var sprite = Art.Get("T46/jigo/" + path + "/" + i.ToString("00")); if (sprite == null) break; frames.Add(sprite); }
            return frames.ToArray();
        }

        public JigoPortrait54(Transform parent, Vector2 center, Vector2 size, int order, GameSession session)
        {
            string forced = Host.Current.Query("jigo");
            _forced = System.Array.IndexOf(Art54.Moods, forced);
            _joy = forced == "sevinc";
            for (int m = 0; m < _frames.Length; m++)
            {
                _frames[m] = new Sprite[6];
                for (int f = 0; f < 6; f++) _frames[m][f] = Art.Get((m == 4 ? "jigo-clip/" : "jigo/") + Art54.Moods[m] + "/" + (f + 1).ToString("00"));
            }
            // Frames are pre-clipped to the arch: High stripping removes SpriteMask from the player.
            var portraitSize = Vector2.one * (size.x * .85f);
            _back = Art54.Place(parent, _joy ? Art54.Joy : Art54.NormalFrame, center, portraitSize, order);
            _front = Art54.Place(parent, _joy ? Art54.Joy : Art54.NormalFrame, center, portraitSize, order + 1);
            _portraitHeight = portraitSize.y;
            _loops[0] = Frames("normal_loop"); _loops[1] = Frames("happy_loop");
            _transitions[0] = Frames("normal_to_happy"); _transitions[1] = Frames("happy_to_normal");
            _nextBlink = Time.unscaledTime + Random.Range(4f, 7f);
            Sync(session);
            Update();
        }

        void Sync(GameSession session)
        {
            _time = session.TimeLeft;
            _lost = session.IsOver && session.Outcome != Outcome.Win;
            _won = session.Outcome == Outcome.Win;
            _fuse = null;
            foreach (var bomb in session.Bombs)
                if (!_fuse.HasValue || bomb.Value < _fuse.Value) _fuse = bomb.Value;
        }

        public void OnEvent(GameEvent e, GameSession session)
        {
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved) _happyUntil = Time.unscaledTime + JigoMood.HappySeconds;
            Sync(session);
        }

        /* ZeydBot: every action of the attempt with its time and the time spent before it (thinking). */
        void RecordMove(GameEvent e)
        {
            string kind;
            if (e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved) kind = e.ByHammer ? "hammer" : "exit";
            else if (e.Kind == EventKind.Jammed) kind = "jam";
            else if (e.Kind == EventKind.BoosterUsed && e.Booster != Booster.Hammer) kind = e.Booster.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
            else return;
            string dir = e.Kind == EventKind.PieceRemoved && !e.ByHammer ? e.Dir.ToString()
                       : e.Kind == EventKind.Jammed ? _drag?.Direction?.ToString() : null;   // jam: the way it was pulled (null: a press on a nail)
            WriteMove(kind, e.Piece, dir);
        }

        /* One moves row; touch rows carry how long the piece was held. Pause / resume and touches do not
           reset the thinking clock: think_ms runs from the last real action. */

        void Update()
        {
            if (_introPlaying) return;
            _continuePanel?.Tick();
            _win21?.Tick();
            _levelsMap?.Tick();
            if (Screen.width != _screenPx.x || Screen.height != _screenPx.y)
            {
                _drag?.Cancel();
                Layout();
            }

            if (PlayOverride.Exit != null && Keyboard.current != null && Keyboard.current.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame)
            {
                ShowMenu();
                return;
            }
            // PM_MESAJ_40: before the clock runs (intro, card, spotlight, any tutorial step) a jam is a 'tut' row, never a jam
            if (_mode == Mode.Playing && _session != null)
                _session.TutorialMove = P21Guided || TutorialActive || _introOpen || _card != null || _tutorialClockHold && _session.Moves == 0;
            if (!_awaitingName) HandlePointer();
            if (_celebration != null && _celebration.Tick(_screen)) EndCelebration();
            if (_pendingHard > 0 && Time.unscaledTime >= _hardDue)
            {
                CommitPendingHard();
                if (_cardKind == "result")
                {
                    string action = _pendingResultAction;
                    _pendingResultAction = null;
                    if (action == null) ShowResult(); else NavigateResult(action);
                }
            }
